===== FILE: .github/workflows/deploy.yml =====
name: Deploy to Cloudflare Pages

on:
  push:
    branches: [main, dev]
  repository_dispatch:
    types: [data-updated]
  workflow_dispatch:

jobs:
  build-deploy:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    permissions:
      contents: read
      deployments: write

    steps:
      - name: Checkout qimochi
        uses: actions/checkout@v7

      - name: Checkout qimochi-data
        uses: actions/checkout@v7
        with:
          repository: bimaakbar-dev/qimochi-data
          path: _data

      - name: Inject data
        run: |
          rm -rf src/content/anime
          rm -rf src/data/anime
          rm -rf src/data/actors
          rm -f  src/data/genres.json
          rm -f  src/data/studios.json
          mkdir -p src/content/anime
          mkdir -p src/data
          if [ -d _data/src/content/anime ]; then
            cp -r _data/src/content/anime/. src/content/anime/
          fi
          if [ -d _data/data/anime ]; then
            cp -r _data/data/anime src/data/anime
          fi
          if [ -d _data/data/actors ]; then
            cp -r _data/data/actors src/data/actors
          fi
          if [ -f _data/data/genres.json ]; then
            cp _data/data/genres.json src/data/genres.json
          fi
          if [ -f _data/data/studios.json ]; then
            cp _data/data/studios.json src/data/studios.json
          fi
          echo "=== src/content/anime (from qimochi-data) ==="
          ls -1 src/content/anime/ | wc -l
          echo "=== src/data/anime ==="
          ls -1 src/data/anime/ 2>/dev/null | wc -l
          echo "=== genres / studios ==="
          ls -la src/data/genres.json src/data/studios.json 2>/dev/null || true

      - name: Setup Node
        uses: actions/setup-node@v4
        with:
          node-version: 24
          cache: npm

      - name: Install dependencies
        run: npm ci

      - name: Build
        run: npm run build
        env:
          PUBLIC_GA_ID: ${{ vars.PUBLIC_GA_ID }}
          PUBLIC_API_BASE: ${{ vars.PUBLIC_API_BASE }}
          PUBLIC_TURNSTILE_SITE_KEY: ${{ vars.PUBLIC_TURNSTILE_SITE_KEY }}
          PUBLIC_GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID: ${{ vars.PUBLIC_GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID }}

      - name: Deploy
        uses: cloudflare/wrangler-action@v4
        with:
          apiToken: ${{ secrets.CF_API_TOKEN }}
          accountId: ${{ secrets.CF_ACCOUNT_ID }}
          command: pages deploy dist --project-name=qimochi --branch=${{ github.ref_name }} --commit-dirty=true

===== FILE: .github/workflows/dump.yml =====
name: Dump repo

on:
  workflow_dispatch:
    inputs:
      max_kb:
        description: Batas ukuran per file (KB)
        default: '200'

permissions:
  contents: write

jobs:
  dump:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Gabungkan isi repo
        env:
          MAX_KB: ${{ inputs.max_kb }}
        run: |
          OUT=dump.txt
          MAX=$(( ${MAX_KB:-200} * 1024 ))
          SKIP='(^|/)(package-lock\.json|pnpm-lock\.yaml|yarn\.lock|bun\.lockb?)$|^src/content/anime/|^src/data/anime/|^src/data/actors/|^src/data/genres\.json$|^src/data/studios\.json$'

          {
            echo "# Repo: $GITHUB_REPOSITORY"
            echo "# Commit: $GITHUB_SHA"
            echo
            echo "## Struktur file"
            git ls-files | grep -Ev "$SKIP"
            echo
            echo "## Isi file"
          } > "$OUT"

          : > skipped.txt

          git ls-files -z | while IFS= read -r -d '' f; do
            if echo "$f" | grep -Eq "$SKIP"; then continue; fi
            [ -f "$f" ] || continue

            if ! grep -Iq . "$f"; then
              echo "biner atau kosong: $f" >> skipped.txt
              continue
            fi

            size=$(wc -c < "$f")
            if [ "$size" -gt "$MAX" ]; then
              echo "terlalu besar ($size byte): $f" >> skipped.txt
              continue
            fi

            {
              echo
              echo "===== FILE: $f ====="
              cat "$f"
            } >> "$OUT"
          done

          {
            echo
            echo "## File yang dilewati"
            cat skipped.txt
          } >> "$OUT"

          {
            echo "### Dump selesai"
            echo "Ukuran: $(wc -c < "$OUT") byte"
            echo "Dilewati: $(wc -l < skipped.txt) file"
          } >> "$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY"

      - name: Simpan hasil ke branch dump
        run: |
          git config user.name "github-actions[bot]"
          git config user.email "41898282+github-actions[bot]@users.noreply.github.com"

          git checkout --orphan dump
          git rm -rf --quiet .
          git add dump.txt
          git commit -m "Dump repo @ ${GITHUB_SHA::7}"
          git push --force origin dump

          echo "File: https://github.com/$GITHUB_REPOSITORY/blob/dump/dump.txt" >> "$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY"

===== FILE: astro.config.mjs =====
import { defineConfig, fontProviders, svgoOptimizer } from 'astro/config';
import sitemap from '@astrojs/sitemap';

export default defineConfig({
  site: 'https://qimochi.web.id',
  trailingSlash: 'always',
  fonts: [
    {
      provider: fontProviders.local(),
      name: "Montserrat",
      cssVariable: "--font-montserrat",
      options: {
        variants: [
          {
            weight: "100 900",
            style: "normal",
            src: ["./src/assets/fonts/Montserrat.woff2"],
          },
        ],
      },
    },
    {
      provider: fontProviders.local(),
      name: "Inter",
      cssVariable: "--font-inter",
      options: {
        variants: [
          {
            weight: "100 900",
            style: "normal",
            src: ["./src/assets/fonts/Inter.woff2"],
          },
        ],
      },
    },
    {
      provider: fontProviders.local(),
      name: "FiraCode",
      cssVariable: "--font-firaCode",
      options: {
        variants: [
          {
            weight: "100 900",
            style: "normal",
            src: ["./src/assets/fonts/FiraCode.woff2"],
          },
        ],
      },
    },
  ],
  integrations: [
    sitemap({
      filter: (page) => {
        if (page.includes('/search/')) return false;
        if (page.includes('/404/')) return false;
        if (page.includes('/dmca/')) return false;
        if (page.includes('/anime/watch/')) return false;
        if (page.includes('/disclaimer/')) return false;
        if (/\/\d+\/$/.test(page)) return false;

        return true;
      },
      changefreq: 'weekly',
      priority: 0.7,
    }),
  ],
  experimental: {
    incrementalBuild: true,
    svgOptimizer: svgoOptimizer(),
  },
});

import { ADS } from '~/constants';

type Format = 'leaderboard' | 'rectangle' | 'skyscraper' | 'inline';

interface Props {
  format?: Format;
  id?: string;
  code?: string;
  label?: string;
}

const {
  format = 'inline',
  id,
  code = '',
  label = 'Slot Iklan',
} = Astro.props;

const dims: Record<Format, string> = {
  leaderboard: '728 × 90',
  rectangle: '300 × 250',
  skyscraper: '300 × 600',
  inline: 'Responsive',
};

const hasCode = code.trim().length > 0;
const showPlaceholder = !hasCode && ADS.showPlaceholder;

    class:list={['ad-slot', 'ad-slot-filled', `ad-slot-${format}`]}

    set:html={code}

    id={id}
    data-ad-slot={format}
    aria-label="Slot iklan"
  >
    <span class="ad-slot-label">{label}</span>
    <span class="ad-slot-dims">{dims[format]}</span>
  </div>
) : null}

import Badge from '~/user-components/Badge.astro';
import Icon from '~/user-components/Icons.astro';
import BookmarkButton from './BookmarkButton.astro';
import { URLS, STATUS_VARIANT, type Status } from '~/constants';

interface CardAnime {
  id: string;
  body?: string;
  data: {
    title: string;
    cover: string;
    banner?: string;
    status: string;
    type: string;
    releaseDate: Date | null;
    score?: number | null;
    stats?: { scoredBy?: number } | null;
    studio?: string;
    episodes?: number | null;
    episodeList?: { number: number }[];
  };
}

interface Props {
  mode?: 'grid' | 'compact' | 'featured' | 'template';
  anime?: CardAnime;
  featured?: boolean;
  relationBadge?: string;
  rich?: boolean;
}

const {
  mode = 'grid',
  anime,
  featured = false,
  relationBadge,
  rich = true,
} = Astro.props;

const isTemplate = mode === 'template';
const isGrid = mode === 'grid' || isTemplate;
const isCompact = mode === 'compact';
const isFeatured = mode === 'featured';

if (!isTemplate && !anime) {
  throw new Error('[AnimeCard] Prop "anime" wajib diisi.');
}

const id = anime?.id ?? '';
const data = anime?.data;

const variant = data
  ? STATUS_VARIANT[data.status as Status] ?? 'default'
  : 'default';

const episodes = data?.episodeList ?? [];
const totalEpisodes = episodes.length;
const hasEpisodes = totalEpisodes > 0;

const firstEpisode = hasEpisodes
  ? Math.min(...episodes.map((e) => e.number))
  : 1;
const lastEpisode = hasEpisodes
  ? Math.max(...episodes.map((e) => e.number))
  : 0;

const episodeLabel = hasEpisodes ? `${totalEpisodes} Episode` : null;
const latestEpisodeLabel = lastEpisode > 0 ? `Ep ${lastEpisode}` : null;

const score = data?.score ?? null;
const votedBy = data?.stats?.scoredBy ?? 0;
const studio =
  data?.studio && data.studio !== 'Unknown' ? data.studio : null;

const landscape = data?.banner || data?.cover || '';

function formatCount(n: number): string {
  if (n >= 1_000_000) return (n / 1_000_000).toFixed(1).replace(/\.0$/, '') + 'M';
  if (n >= 1_000) return (n / 1_000).toFixed(1).replace(/\.0$/, '') + 'K';
  return String(n);
}

function plainExcerpt(input: string | undefined, maxLen = 110): string {
  if (!input) return '';
  const cleaned = input
    .replace(/!\[[^\]]*\]\([^)]*\)/g, '')
    .replace(/\[([^\]]+)\]\([^)]*\)/g, '$1')
    .replace(/[*_`>#]/g, '')
    .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
    .trim();
  return cleaned.length <= maxLen
    ? cleaned
    : cleaned.slice(0, maxLen).trimEnd() + '…';
}

const excerpt = plainExcerpt(anime?.body);
const scorePercent = score != null ? (score / 10) * 100 : 0;

<script>
  (function () {
    function setupCoverImage(img: HTMLImageElement): void {
      if (img.dataset.ready === '1') return;
      img.dataset.ready = '1';

      const wrap = img.parentElement;
      const placeholder =
        wrap?.querySelector<HTMLElement>('.cover-placeholder') ?? null;

      const stopShimmer = () => placeholder?.classList.remove('is-loading');
      const fail = () => {
        img.hidden = true;
        stopShimmer();
      };

      if (img.complete && img.naturalWidth > 0) {
        stopShimmer();
        return;
      }
      if (img.complete && img.naturalWidth === 0) {
        fail();
        return;
      }

      img.addEventListener('load', stopShimmer, { once: true });
      img.addEventListener('error', fail, { once: true });
    }

    function init(scope: ParentNode = document): void {
      scope
        .querySelectorAll<HTMLImageElement>('[data-cover-img]')
        .forEach(setupCoverImage);
    }

    init();

    window.addEventListener('qimochi:covers', (e) => {
      const detail = (e as CustomEvent<ParentNode>).detail;
      if (detail) init(detail);
    });
  })();
</script>

===== FILE: src/components/AnimeFranchise.astro =====
---
import { getAllAnime } from '~/lib/anime';
import {
  RELATION_LABEL,
  type RelationType,
} from '~/constants';
import SectionLabel from './SectionLabel.astro';
import AnimeCard from './AnimeCard.astro';
import Badge from '~/user-components/Badge.astro';

interface Franchise {
  relation: string;
  slug: string;
  title?: string;
}

interface Props {
  title?: string;
  currentSlug: string;
  franchises?: Franchise[];
}

const {
  title = 'Seri Terkait',
  currentSlug,
  franchises = [],
} = Astro.props;

const filtered = franchises.filter((f) => f.slug !== currentSlug);
if (filtered.length === 0) return null;

const RELATION_PRIORITY: Record<RelationType, number> = {
  prequel: 1,
  sequel: 2,
  parent_story: 3,
  side_story: 4,
  spin_off: 5,
  alternative: 6,
  full_story: 7,
  summary: 8,
  compilation: 9,
  contains: 10,
  adaptation: 11,
  character: 12,
  other: 13,
};

const allAnime = await getAllAnime();
const animeMap = new Map(allAnime.map((a) => [a.id, a]));

interface Item {
  relation: string;
  relationLabel: string;
  slug: string;
  anime: (typeof allAnime)[number] | null;
  displayTitle: string;
  year: number | null;
}

const items: Item[] = filtered.map((f) => {
  const anime = animeMap.get(f.slug) ?? null;
  const relationLabel = RELATION_LABEL[f.relation as RelationType] ?? f.relation;

  let displayTitle: string;
  if (anime) {
    displayTitle = anime.data.title;
  } else if (f.title) {
    displayTitle = f.title;
  } else {
    displayTitle = f.slug
      .split('-')
      .map((w) => w.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + w.slice(1))
      .join(' ');
  }

  return {
    relation: f.relation,
    relationLabel,
    slug: f.slug,
    anime,
    displayTitle,
    year: anime?.data.releaseDate?.getFullYear() ?? null,
  };
});

const groupMap = new Map<string, Item[]>();
for (const item of items) {
  const list = groupMap.get(item.relation) ?? [];
  list.push(item);
  groupMap.set(item.relation, list);
}

const groups = [...groupMap.entries()]
  .map(([relation, groupItems]) => ({
    relation,
    label: RELATION_LABEL[relation as RelationType] ?? relation,
    items: [...groupItems].sort((a, b) => {
      const ya = a.year ?? 9999;
      const yb = b.year ?? 9999;
      if (ya !== yb) return ya - yb;
      return a.displayTitle.localeCompare(b.displayTitle);
    }),
  }))
  .sort((a, b) => {
    const pa = RELATION_PRIORITY[a.relation as RelationType] ?? 99;
    const pb = RELATION_PRIORITY[b.relation as RelationType] ?? 99;
    return pa - pb;
  });
---

<section class="section">
  <SectionLabel>{title}</SectionLabel>

  <div class="franchise-groups">
    {groups.map((group) => (
      <div class="franchise-group">
        <h3 class="franchise-group__label">{group.label}</h3>
        <div class="franchise-grid">
          {group.items.map((item) => {
            if (item.anime) {
              return <AnimeCard anime={item.anime} />;
            }
            return (
              <article class="anime-card anime-card--disabled" aria-disabled="true">
                <div class="disabled-cover">
                  <Badge variant="outline" size="sm">{item.relationLabel}</Badge>
                </div>
                <div class="disabled-info">
                  <h3>{item.displayTitle}</h3>
                  <span class="disabled-meta">Belum tersedia</span>
                </div>
              </article>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </div>
    ))}
  </div>
</section>

import LinkButton from '~/user-components/LinkButton.astro';
import BookmarkButton from './BookmarkButton.astro';

export interface HeroGenreLink {
  name: string;
  href: string;
}

export interface HeroItem {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  background: string;
  age?: string | null;
  subIndo?: boolean;
  genres?: string[];
  genreLinks?: HeroGenreLink[];
  excerpt?: string | null;
  watchUrl?: string;
  watchLabel?: string;
  watchDisabled?: boolean;
}

interface Props {
  item: HeroItem;
  titleTag?: 'h1' | 'h2';
  size?: 'carousel' | 'detail';
  showBookmark?: boolean;
  class?: string;
}

const {
  item,
  titleTag: TitleTag = 'h1',
  size = 'carousel',
  showBookmark = true,
  class: className,
} = Astro.props;

const {
  id, title, background, age,
  subIndo = true, genres, genreLinks,
  excerpt, watchUrl, watchLabel = 'Mulai Nonton',
  watchDisabled = false,
} = item;

const hasGenres = (genreLinks?.length ?? 0) > 0 || (genres?.length ?? 0) > 0;
const hasMeta = Boolean(age) || subIndo || hasGenres;

const belowMetaSlot = Astro.slots.has('below-meta');
const actionsSlot = Astro.slots.has('actions');
const showWatch = Boolean(watchUrl) || watchDisabled;
---

<div class:list={['hero', `hero--${size}`, className]}>
  {background && (
    <div class="hero__bg" aria-hidden="true">
      <img
        class="hero__bg-img"
        src={background}
        alt=""
        loading="eager"
        fetchpriority="high"
        decoding="async"
        draggable="false"
      />
    </div>
  )}

  <div class="hero__scrim" aria-hidden="true"></div>

  <div class="hero__inner">
    <TitleTag class="hero__title" title={title}>{title}</TitleTag>

    {hasMeta && (
      <ul class="hero__meta">
        {age && <li class="hero__age">{age}</li>}
        {subIndo && <li class="hero__subindo">Sub Indo</li>}

        {genreLinks && genreLinks.length > 0 ? (
          <li class="hero__genres">
            {genreLinks.map((g) => (
              <a href={g.href} class="hero__genre-link">{g.name}</a>
            ))}
          </li>
        ) : (
          genres && genres.length > 0 && (
            <li class="hero__genres">{genres.join(', ')}</li>
          )
        )}
      </ul>
    )}

    {excerpt && <p class="hero__excerpt">{excerpt}</p>}

    {belowMetaSlot && (
      <div class="hero__below">
        <slot name="below-meta" />
      </div>
    )}

    {(showWatch || showBookmark || actionsSlot) && (
      <div class="hero__actions">
        {showWatch && (
          <LinkButton
            href={watchUrl}
            variant="accent"
            size="lg"
            icon="play"
            disabled={watchDisabled}
            ariaLabel={watchDisabled ? undefined : `${watchLabel}: ${title}`}
            class="hero__play"
          >
            {watchLabel}
          </LinkButton>
        )}

        {showBookmark && <BookmarkButton animeId={id} class="hero__bookmark" />}

        {actionsSlot && <slot name="actions" />}
      </div>
    )}
  </div>
</div>

interface InfoItem {
  label: string;
  value: string | number;
}

interface GenreLink {
  name: string;
  href: string;
}

interface Props {
  items: InfoItem[];
  genres?: GenreLink[];
  class?: string;
}

const { items, genres = [], class: className } = Astro.props;

const visible = items.filter(
  (it) => it.value !== null && it.value !== undefined && String(it.value).length > 0
);
---

{(visible.length > 0 || genres.length > 0) && (
  <dl class:list={['anime-info', className]}>
    {visible.map((it) => (
      <div class="anime-info__row">
        <dt class="anime-info__label">{it.label}:</dt>
        <dd class="anime-info__value">{it.value}</dd>
      </div>
    ))}

    {genres.length > 0 && (
      <div class="anime-info__row">
        <dt class="anime-info__label">Genre:</dt>
        <dd class="anime-info__value anime-info__value--genres">
          {genres.map((g) => (
            <a href={g.href} class="anime-info__link">{g.name}</a>
          ))}
        </dd>
      </div>
    )}
  </dl>
)}

<script>
  interface RatingData {
    animeId: string;
    average: number;
    votes: number;
    userScore: number | null;
    canRate: boolean;
    distribution: Record<string, number>;
  }

  function formatCount(n: number): string {
    if (n >= 1_000_000) return (n / 1_000_000).toFixed(1).replace(/\.0$/, '') + 'M';
    if (n >= 1_000) return (n / 1_000).toFixed(1).replace(/\.0$/, '') + 'K';
    return String(n);
  }

  function formatFull(n: number): string {
    return n.toLocaleString('id-ID');
  }

  function openUserMenu(): void {
    const menu = (window as any).__qimochiUserMenu;
    if (menu?.open) {
      menu.open();
    } else {
      document.dispatchEvent(
        new CustomEvent('qimochi:auth-required', { detail: { feature: 'rating' } })
      );
    }
  }

  function supportsPopover(): boolean {
    return (
      typeof HTMLElement !== 'undefined' &&
      'popover' in HTMLElement.prototype
    );
  }

  function positionPopover(popover: HTMLElement, trigger: HTMLElement): void {
    popover.style.top = '0px';
    popover.style.left = '0px';

    const trigRect = trigger.getBoundingClientRect();
    const popRect = popover.getBoundingClientRect();
    const vh = window.innerHeight;
    const vw = window.innerWidth;
    const MARGIN = 8;

    let top = trigRect.bottom + MARGIN;
    let left = trigRect.left;

    if (top + popRect.height > vh - MARGIN) {
      const above = trigRect.top - popRect.height - MARGIN;
      if (above >= MARGIN) top = above;
      else top = Math.max(MARGIN, vh - popRect.height - MARGIN);
    }

    if (left + popRect.width > vw - MARGIN) {
      left = vw - popRect.width - MARGIN;
    }
    if (left < MARGIN) left = MARGIN;

    popover.style.top = `${Math.round(top)}px`;
    popover.style.left = `${Math.round(left)}px`;
  }

  function initWidget(root: HTMLElement): void {
    if (root.dataset.bound === '1') return;
    root.dataset.bound = '1';

    const ANIME_ID = root.dataset.anime ?? '';
    const API = (root.dataset.api ?? '').replace(/\/$/, '');
    const POPOVER_ID = root.dataset.popoverId ?? '';
    if (!ANIME_ID || !API || !POPOVER_ID) return;

    const triggerEl = root.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-rating-trigger]');
    const popoverEl = document.getElementById(POPOVER_ID) as HTMLElement | null;
    if (!triggerEl || !popoverEl) return;

    const triggerBtn = triggerEl;
    const popover = popoverEl;

    const closeBtn = popover.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-rating-close]');
    if (!closeBtn) return;

    const starsWrap = root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('.rating__stars');
    const starsFill = root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-stars-fill]');
    const valueEl = root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-value]');
    const votesEl = root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-votes]');

    const summaryTitle = popover.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-summary-title]');
    const summaryCount = popover.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-summary-count]');
    const histogram = popover.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-histogram]');
    const userLabel = popover.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-user-label]');
    const clearBtn = popover.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-clear]');
    const hintEl = popover.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-hint]');

    if (
      !starsWrap || !starsFill || !valueEl || !votesEl ||
      !summaryTitle || !summaryCount || !histogram || !userLabel ||
      !clearBtn || !hintEl
    ) {
      return;
    }

    const inputStars = Array.from(
      popover.querySelectorAll<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-input-star]')
    );

    let state: RatingData | null = null;
    let submitting = false;

    function fillWidth(avg5: number, hasVotes: boolean): string {
      if (!hasVotes) return '0%';
      return `${Math.max(0, Math.min(100, (avg5 / 5) * 100))}%`;
    }

    function renderTriggerStars(avg5: number, hasVotes: boolean): void {
      starsWrap!.dataset.empty = hasVotes ? 'false' : 'true';
      starsFill!.style.width = fillWidth(avg5, hasVotes);
    }

    function renderTrigger(): void {
      if (!state) return;
      const hasVotes = state.votes > 0;

      if (hasVotes) {
        const avg5 = state.average / 2;
        valueEl!.textContent = avg5.toFixed(1);
        valueEl!.classList.remove('is-empty');
        votesEl!.textContent = `(${formatCount(state.votes)})`;
        renderTriggerStars(avg5, true);
      } else {
        valueEl!.textContent = 'Belum ada rating';
        valueEl!.classList.add('is-empty');
        votesEl!.textContent = '';
        renderTriggerStars(0, false);
      }
    }

    function renderHistogram(dist: Record<string, number>, total: number): void {
      histogram!.innerHTML = '';

      if (total === 0) {
        const empty = document.createElement('div');
        empty.className = 'rating__hist-empty';
        empty.textContent = 'Belum ada rating. Jadilah yang pertama!';
        histogram!.appendChild(empty);
        return;
      }

      for (let star = 5; star >= 1; star--) {
        const count = dist[String(star)] ?? 0;
        const pct = total > 0 ? (count / total) * 100 : 0;

        const row = document.createElement('div');
        row.className = 'rating__hist-row';
        row.innerHTML = `
          <span class="rating__hist-label">
            <span class="rating__hist-label-star" aria-hidden="true">★</span>${star}
          </span>
          <span class="rating__hist-track">
            <span class="rating__hist-fill" style="width: ${pct.toFixed(1)}%"></span>
          </span>
          <span class="rating__hist-pct">${Math.round(pct)}%</span>
        `;
        histogram!.appendChild(row);
      }
    }

    function renderInput(): void {
      if (!state) return;
      const canRate = state.canRate;
      const userScore = state.userScore;

      for (const btn of inputStars) {
        const n = Number(btn.dataset.inputStar);
        btn.disabled = !canRate || submitting;
        btn.classList.remove('is-active', 'is-preview');
        if (userScore != null && n <= userScore) btn.classList.add('is-active');
        btn.setAttribute('aria-checked', String(userScore === n));
      }

      if (!canRate) {
        userLabel!.textContent = 'Beri rating:';
        hintEl!.innerHTML = `<a href="#" data-login-link>Login</a> untuk memberi rating.`;
        hintEl!.hidden = false;
      } else if (userScore != null) {
        userLabel!.textContent = `Rating kamu: ${userScore}★`;
        hintEl!.hidden = true;
      } else {
        userLabel!.textContent = 'Beri rating:';
        hintEl!.hidden = true;
      }

      clearBtn!.hidden = !(canRate && userScore != null);
      clearBtn!.disabled = submitting;
    }

    function renderPopover(): void {
      if (!state) return;
      const hasVotes = state.votes > 0;
      const avg5 = state.average / 2;

      if (hasVotes) {
        summaryTitle!.textContent = `Rata-rata ${avg5.toFixed(1)} dari 5 bintang`;
        summaryCount!.textContent = `${formatFull(state.votes)} rating`;
      } else {
        summaryTitle!.textContent = 'Belum ada rating';
        summaryCount!.textContent = '';
      }

      renderHistogram(state.distribution, state.votes);
      renderInput();
    }

    function render(): void {
      renderTrigger();
      renderPopover();
    }

    async function fetchRating(): Promise<void> {
      try {
        const res = await fetch(
          `${API}/api/v1/ratings/${encodeURIComponent(ANIME_ID)}`,
          { credentials: 'include' }
        );
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}`);
        const json = (await res.json()) as { data: RatingData };
        state = json.data;
        render();
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('[rating] fetch failed', err);
        state = {
          animeId: ANIME_ID,
          average: 0,
          votes: 0,
          userScore: null,
          canRate: false,
          distribution: {},
        };
        render();
      }
    }

    async function submitRating(score: number): Promise<void> {
      if (!state?.canRate || submitting) return;
      submitting = true;
      renderInput();

      try {
        const res = await fetch(
          `${API}/api/v1/ratings/${encodeURIComponent(ANIME_ID)}`,
          {
            method: 'POST',
            credentials: 'include',
            headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
            body: JSON.stringify({ score }),
          }
        );

        if (res.status === 401) {
          state.canRate = false;
          render();
          submitting = false;
          openUserMenu();
          return;
        }

        if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}`);

        const json = (await res.json()) as { data: RatingData };
        state = { ...state, ...json.data };
        render();
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('[rating] submit failed', err);
      } finally {
        submitting = false;
        renderInput();
      }
    }

    async function clearRating(): Promise<void> {
      if (!state?.userScore || submitting) return;
      submitting = true;
      renderInput();

      try {
        const res = await fetch(
          `${API}/api/v1/ratings/${encodeURIComponent(ANIME_ID)}`,
          { method: 'DELETE', credentials: 'include' }
        );

        if (res.status === 401) {
          state.canRate = false;
          render();
          submitting = false;
          openUserMenu();
          return;
        }

        if (!res.ok && res.status !== 404) throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}`);

        const json = (await res.json()) as { data: RatingData };
        state = { ...state, ...json.data };
        render();
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('[rating] clear failed', err);
      } finally {
        submitting = false;
        renderInput();
      }
    }

    let fallbackOpen = false;
    let fallbackHandlersAttached = false;

    function openFallback(): void {
      popover.style.display = 'block';
      popover.style.position = 'fixed';
      fallbackOpen = true;
      triggerBtn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true');
      positionPopover(popover, triggerBtn);

      if (!fallbackHandlersAttached) {
        fallbackHandlersAttached = true;

        const onDocClick = (e: MouseEvent) => {
          const target = e.target as Node;
          if (popover.contains(target) || triggerBtn.contains(target)) return;
          closeFallback();
        };
        const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
          if (e.key === 'Escape') closeFallback();
        };
        const onScrollOrResize = () => {
          if (fallbackOpen) positionPopover(popover, triggerBtn);
        };

        document.addEventListener('click', onDocClick, true);
        document.addEventListener('keydown', onKey);
        window.addEventListener('scroll', onScrollOrResize, true);
        window.addEventListener('resize', onScrollOrResize);
      }
    }

    function closeFallback(): void {
      if (!fallbackOpen) return;
      popover.style.display = 'none';
      fallbackOpen = false;
      triggerBtn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
    }

    if (supportsPopover()) {
      popover.addEventListener('toggle', (e) => {
        const ev = e as ToggleEvent;
        if (ev.newState === 'open') positionPopover(popover, triggerBtn);
      });

      const onScrollOrResize = () => {
        if (popover.matches(':popover-open')) positionPopover(popover, triggerBtn);
      };
      window.addEventListener('scroll', onScrollOrResize, true);
      window.addEventListener('resize', onScrollOrResize);

      closeBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        if (typeof (popover as any).hidePopover === 'function') {
          (popover as any).hidePopover();
        }
      });
    } else {
      popover.style.display = 'none';

      triggerBtn.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (fallbackOpen) closeFallback();
        else openFallback();
      });

      closeBtn.addEventListener('click', closeFallback);
    }

    for (const btn of inputStars) {
      const n = Number(btn.dataset.inputStar);

      btn.addEventListener('mouseenter', () => {
        if (!state?.canRate || submitting) return;
        for (const b of inputStars) {
          const bn = Number(b.dataset.inputStar);
          b.classList.remove('is-active', 'is-preview');
          if (bn <= n) b.classList.add('is-preview');
        }
      });

      btn.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => {
        if (!state?.canRate || submitting) return;
        renderInput();
      });

      btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        if (!state?.canRate || submitting) return;
        submitRating(n);
      });
    }

    clearBtn.addEventListener('click', clearRating);

    hintEl.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
      const target = e.target as HTMLElement;
      if (target.dataset.loginLink !== undefined) {
        e.preventDefault();
        openUserMenu();
      }
    });

    document.addEventListener('qimochi:auth-changed', () => {
      fetchRating();
    });

    renderTriggerStars(0, false);
    fetchRating();
  }

  function initAll(scope: ParentNode = document): void {
    scope.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('[data-rating-widget]').forEach(initWidget);
  }

  initAll();
</script>

const { animeId } = Astro.props;

  class="reaction-bar"
  data-reaction-bar
  data-anime={animeId}
  data-api={apiBase}
  role="group"
  aria-label="Reaksi anime"

<style is:global>
  .reaction-bar {

  .reaction-bar .reaction-btn {

<script>
  interface AuthUser {
    sub: string;
    name: string;
    email: string;
    picture: string | null;
  }

  (function () {
    const root = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-reaction-bar]');
    if (!root) return;

    const ANIME_ID = root.dataset.anime || '';
    const API = (root.dataset.api || '').replace(/\/$/, '');
    if (!ANIME_ID || !API) return;

    let currentUser: AuthUser | null = null;
    let allowed: string[] = ['👍', '❤️', '🔥', '😂', '😮'];
    let counts: Record<string, number> = {};
    let userReactions: Set<string> = new Set();
    let canReact = false;
    let submitting = false;
    let buttons: Record<string, HTMLButtonElement> = {};

    function buildButtons(): void {
      for (const btn of Object.values(buttons)) btn.remove();
      buttons = {};
      root!.innerHTML = '';

      for (const emoji of allowed) {
        const btn = document.createElement('button');
        btn.type = 'button';
        btn.className = 'reaction-btn';
        btn.dataset.emoji = emoji;
        btn.setAttribute('aria-label', `Reaksi ${emoji}`);
        btn.innerHTML = `
          <span class="reaction-emoji">${emoji}</span>
          <span class="reaction-count" data-count data-empty="true">0</span>
        `;
        btn.addEventListener('click', () => toggle(emoji));
        root!.appendChild(btn);
        buttons[emoji] = btn;
      }
    }

    function applyState(): void {
      for (const emoji of allowed) {
        const btn = buttons[emoji];
        if (!btn) continue;

        const countEl = btn.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-count]')!;
        const count = counts[emoji] ?? 0;
        const active = userReactions.has(emoji);

        countEl.textContent = count > 99 ? '99+' : String(count);
        countEl.dataset.empty = count === 0 ? 'true' : 'false';

        btn.classList.toggle('is-active', active);
        btn.disabled = !canReact || submitting;

        btn.setAttribute('aria-label', `Reaksi ${emoji}, ${count} orang`);
        btn.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(active));
      }
    }

    function popButton(emoji: string): void {
      const btn = buttons[emoji];
      if (!btn) return;
      btn.classList.remove('pop');
      void btn.offsetWidth;
      btn.classList.add('pop');
      window.setTimeout(() => btn.classList.remove('pop'), 400);
    }

    async function fetchReactions(): Promise<void> {
      try {
        const res = await fetch(
          `${API}/api/v1/reactions/${encodeURIComponent(ANIME_ID)}`,
          { credentials: 'include' }
        );
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}`);
        const json = (await res.json()) as {
          data: {
            counts: Record<string, number>;
            userReactions: string[];
            canReact: boolean;
            allowed: string[];
          };
        };
        const d = json.data;
        counts = d.counts || {};
        userReactions = new Set(d.userReactions || []);
        canReact = d.canReact;
        if (Array.isArray(d.allowed) && d.allowed.length) {
          allowed = d.allowed;
        }
        if (Object.keys(buttons).length !== allowed.length) {
          buildButtons();
        }
        applyState();
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('[reaction] fetch failed', err);
      }
    }

    async function toggle(emoji: string): Promise<void> {
      if (!canReact || submitting) return;

      const wasActive = userReactions.has(emoji);
      userReactions = new Set(userReactions);
      if (wasActive) userReactions.delete(emoji);
      else userReactions.add(emoji);

      counts = { ...counts };
      counts[emoji] = Math.max(0, (counts[emoji] ?? 0) + (wasActive ? -1 : 1));

      submitting = true;
      applyState();
      popButton(emoji);

      try {
        const res = await fetch(
          `${API}/api/v1/reactions/${encodeURIComponent(ANIME_ID)}`,
          {
            method: 'POST',
            credentials: 'include',
            headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
            body: JSON.stringify({ emoji }),
          }
        );

        if (res.status === 401) {
          canReact = false;
          await fetchReactions();
          return;
        }

        if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}`);
        const json = (await res.json()) as {
          data: {
            counts: Record<string, number>;
            userReactions: string[];
          };
        };
        counts = json.data.counts || {};
        userReactions = new Set(json.data.userReactions || []);
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('[reaction] toggle failed', err);
        await fetchReactions();
      } finally {
        submitting = false;
        applyState();
      }
    }

    function readCache(): AuthUser | null {
      try {
        const raw = localStorage.getItem('qimochi:user-cache');
        if (!raw) return null;
        const parsed = JSON.parse(raw) as Partial<AuthUser> | null;
        if (
          parsed &&
          typeof parsed.sub === 'string' &&
          typeof parsed.name === 'string'
        ) {
          return {
            sub: parsed.sub,
            name: parsed.name,
            email: typeof parsed.email === 'string' ? parsed.email : '',
            picture:
              typeof parsed.picture === 'string' ? parsed.picture : null,
          };
        }
      } catch {}
      return null;
    }

    function onAuthChanged(e: Event): void {
      const detail = (e as CustomEvent<{ user: AuthUser | null }>).detail;
      if (!detail) return;
      const same =
        (detail.user?.sub ?? null) === (currentUser?.sub ?? null);
      if (same) return;
      currentUser = detail.user;
      fetchReactions();
    }

    document.addEventListener('qimochi:auth-changed', onAuthChanged);

    (function bootstrap(): void {
      currentUser = readCache();
      buildButtons();
    })();

    fetchReactions();

    (async () => {
      const menu = (window as any).__qimochiUserMenu;
      if (menu) {
        const u = menu.getUser();
        if ((u?.sub ?? null) !== (currentUser?.sub ?? null)) {
          currentUser = u ?? null;
          fetchReactions();
        }
      }
    })();
  })();
</script>

import Icon from '~/user-components/Icons.astro';
import bannerData from '~/data/banner.json';

type BannerType = 'info' | 'warning' | 'success' | 'danger';

interface BannerLink {
  href: string;
  label: string;
}

interface Banner {
  id: string;
  active?: boolean;
  type?: BannerType;
  message: string;
  link?: BannerLink;
  dismissible?: boolean;
}

interface Props {
  class?: string;
}

const { class: className } = Astro.props;

const banners = ((bannerData as { banners?: Banner[] }).banners ?? []).filter(
  (b) => b.active !== false && b.message
);

if (banners.length === 0) return null;
---

<div class:list={['site-banner-stack', className]} data-site-banner-stack>
  {banners.map((b) => {
    const type = b.type ?? 'info';
    const dismissible = b.dismissible !== false;

    return (
      <div
        class:list={['site-banner', `site-banner--${type}`]}
        data-site-banner
        data-banner-id={b.id}
        data-dismissible={dismissible ? '1' : '0'}
        role={type === 'danger' || type === 'warning' ? 'alert' : 'status'}
      >
        <div class="site-banner__inner">
          <p class="site-banner__message">
            <span>{b.message}</span>
            {b.link && (
              <>
                {' '}
                <a class="site-banner__link" href={b.link.href}>
                  {b.link.label}
                </a>
              </>
            )}
          </p>

          {dismissible && (
            <button
              type="button"
              class="site-banner__close"
              data-banner-close
              aria-label="Tutup pengumuman"
            >
              <Icon name="close" size={14} />
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  })}
</div>

<script>
  (function () {
    const STORAGE_KEY = 'qimochi:banner-dismissed';

    function loadDismissed(): Set<string> {
      try {
        const raw = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
        if (!raw) return new Set();
        const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
        if (!Array.isArray(parsed)) return new Set();
        return new Set(parsed.filter((x) => typeof x === 'string'));
      } catch {
        return new Set();
      }
    }

    function saveDismissed(ids: Set<string>): void {
      try {
        localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify([...ids]));
      } catch {}
    }

    function init(): void {
      const stack = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-site-banner-stack]');
      if (!stack) return;

      const dismissed = loadDismissed();
      const items = stack.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('[data-site-banner]');

      items.forEach((el) => {
        const id = el.dataset.bannerId ?? '';
        const dismissible = el.dataset.dismissible === '1';

        if (id && dismissed.has(id)) {
          el.remove();
          return;
        }

        if (!dismissible || !id) return;

        const btn = el.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-banner-close]');
        if (!btn) return;

        btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
          dismissed.add(id);
          saveDismissed(dismissed);
          el.remove();

          if (stack.querySelectorAll('[data-site-banner]').length === 0) {
            stack.remove();
          }
        });
      });

      if (stack.querySelectorAll('[data-site-banner]').length === 0) {
        stack.remove();
      }
    }

    init();
    document.addEventListener('astro:page-load', init);
  })();
</script>

<style>
  .site-banner-stack {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    width: 100%;
  }

  .site-banner {
    position: relative;

    background: transparent;
    border: none;
    color: currentColor;
    cursor: pointer;
    transition: opacity var(--t-fast), background var(--t-fast);
  }

  .site-banner__close:hover {
    background: hsla(0, 0%, 0%, 0.06);
  }

  .site-banner__close :global(svg) {
    width: 100%;
    height: 100%;
    flex-shrink: 0;
  }

  .site-banner--info {
    background: var(--accent);
    color: var(--accent-text);

import Icon from '~/user-components/Icons.astro';

interface Props {
  animeId: string;
  class?: string;
  variant?: 'default' | 'plain';
}

const { animeId, class: className, variant = 'default' } = Astro.props;
const apiBase = import.meta.env.PUBLIC_API_BASE ?? 'https://api.qimochi.web.id';
---

<button
  type="button"
  class:list={[
    'icon-btn',
    `icon-btn--${variant}`,
    className,
  ]}
  data-bookmark-btn
  data-anime={animeId}
  data-api={apiBase}
  aria-label="Simpan ke bookmark"
  aria-pressed="false"
  title="Simpan ke bookmark"
>
  <span class="icon-btn__idle">
    <Icon name="bookmark" size={20} />
  </span>
  <span class="icon-btn__active">
    <Icon name="bookmark-filled" size={20} />
  </span>
</button>

<script>
  interface AuthUser {
    sub: string;
    name: string;
    email: string;
    picture: string | null;
  }

  (function () {
    function readCache(): AuthUser | null {
      try {
        const raw = localStorage.getItem('qimochi:user-cache');
        if (!raw) return null;
        const parsed = JSON.parse(raw) as Partial<AuthUser> | null;
        if (
          parsed &&
          typeof parsed.sub === 'string' &&
          typeof parsed.name === 'string'
        ) {
          return {
            sub: parsed.sub,
            name: parsed.name,
            email: typeof parsed.email === 'string' ? parsed.email : '',
            picture: typeof parsed.picture === 'string' ? parsed.picture : null,
          };
        }
      } catch {}
      return null;
    }

    function init(btn: HTMLButtonElement): void {
      if (btn.dataset.bound === '1') return;
      btn.dataset.bound = '1';

      const ANIME_ID = btn.dataset.anime || '';
      const API = (btn.dataset.api || '').replace(/\/$/, '');
      if (!ANIME_ID || !API) return;

      let currentUser: AuthUser | null = null;
      let bookmarked = false;
      let submitting = false;

      function applyState(): void {
        btn.classList.toggle('is-active', bookmarked);
        btn.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(bookmarked));
        btn.setAttribute(
          'aria-label',
          bookmarked ? 'Hapus dari bookmark' : 'Simpan ke bookmark'
        );
        btn.title = bookmarked ? 'Hapus dari bookmark' : 'Simpan ke bookmark';
        btn.disabled = submitting;
      }

      async function fetchStatus(): Promise<void> {
        if (!currentUser) {
          bookmarked = false;
          applyState();
          return;
        }
        try {
          const res = await fetch(
            `${API}/api/v1/bookmarks/${encodeURIComponent(ANIME_ID)}`,
            { credentials: 'include' }
          );
          if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}`);
          const json = (await res.json()) as {
            data?: { bookmarked?: boolean };
          };
          bookmarked = Boolean(json.data?.bookmarked);
        } catch (err) {
          console.error('[bookmark] fetch failed', err);
          bookmarked = false;
        } finally {
          applyState();
        }
      }

      async function toggle(): Promise<void> {
        if (submitting) return;

        if (!currentUser) {
          const menu = (window as any).__qimochiUserMenu;
          if (menu?.open) menu.open();
          return;
        }

        submitting = true;
        applyState();

        const next = !bookmarked;
        bookmarked = next;
        applyState();

        try {
          const res = await fetch(
            `${API}/api/v1/bookmarks/${encodeURIComponent(ANIME_ID)}`,
            {
              method: next ? 'POST' : 'DELETE',
              credentials: 'include',
            }
          );

          if (res.status === 401) {
            currentUser = null;
            bookmarked = false;
            applyState();
            const menu = (window as any).__qimochiUserMenu;
            if (menu?.open) menu.open();
            return;
          }
          if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}`);
        } catch (err) {
          console.error('[bookmark] toggle failed', err);
          bookmarked = !next;
        } finally {
          submitting = false;
          applyState();
        }
      }

      btn.addEventListener('click', toggle);

      function onAuthChanged(e: Event): void {
        const detail = (e as CustomEvent<{ user: AuthUser | null }>).detail;
        if (!detail) return;
        const same = (detail.user?.sub ?? null) === (currentUser?.sub ?? null);
        if (same) return;
        currentUser = detail.user;
        fetchStatus();
      }

      document.addEventListener('qimochi:auth-changed', onAuthChanged);

      (function bootstrap(): void {
        currentUser = readCache();
        applyState();
      })();

      fetchStatus();

      (async () => {
        const menu = (window as any).__qimochiUserMenu;
        if (menu?.getUser) {
          const u = menu.getUser();
          if ((u?.sub ?? null) !== (currentUser?.sub ?? null)) {
            currentUser = u ?? null;
            fetchStatus();
          }
        }
      })();
    }

    function initAll(scope: ParentNode = document): void {
      scope
        .querySelectorAll<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-bookmark-btn]')
        .forEach(init);
    }

    initAll();

    window.addEventListener('qimochi:bookmarks', (e) => {
      const detail = (e as CustomEvent<ParentNode>).detail;
      if (detail) initAll(detail);
    });
  })();
</script>

<style>
  .icon-btn {
    display: inline-flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    flex-shrink: 0;
    padding: 0;
    background: transparent;
    color: var(--text-inverted);
    border: 1.5px solid hsla(0, 0%, 100%, 0.35);
    border-radius: var(--r-full);
