    line-height: var(--lh-relaxed);
    overflow-wrap: anywhere;
  }

  .comment-body :global(strong) {
    color: var(--text-primary);
    font-weight: var(--fw-semibold);
  }

  .comment-body :global(em) {
    font-style: italic;
  }

  .comment-body :global(del) {
    opacity: 0.6;
  }

  .comment-body :global(code) {
    font-family: var(--font-mono);
    font-size: 0.9em;
    padding: 0.1em 0.35em;
    background: var(--bg-surface);
    border: 1px solid var(--border);
    border-radius: var(--r-sm);
    color: var(--text-primary);
  }

  .comment-body :global(a) {
    color: var(--accent);
    text-decoration: underline;
    text-underline-offset: 2px;
  }

  .comment-body :global(a:hover) {
    color: var(--accent-hover);
  }

  .comment-body :global(.mention) {
    color: var(--accent);
    font-weight: var(--fw-semibold);
    background: var(--accent-soft);
    padding: 0 4px;
    border-radius: var(--r-sm);
  }

  .comment-body :global(.spoiler) {
    display: inline;
    padding: 0 3px;
    background: var(--text-primary);
    color: transparent;
    cursor: pointer;
    user-select: none;
    border-radius: 2px;
    transition: all var(--t-fast);
  }

  .comment-body :global(.spoiler.revealed) {
    background: var(--bg-surface);
    color: var(--text-secondary);
    user-select: auto;
  }

  .comment-edit {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: var(--s-2);
  }

  .comment-edit[hidden] {
    display: none;
  }

  .comment-edit-input {
    width: 100%;
    padding: var(--s-3);
    background: var(--bg-surface);
    border: 1px solid var(--accent);
    color: var(--text-primary);
    font-family: inherit;
    font-size: var(--fs-sm);
    line-height: var(--lh-relaxed);
    resize: vertical;
    min-height: 4rem;
  }

  .comment-edit-input:focus {
    outline: none;
  }

  .comment-edit-actions {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: flex-end;
    gap: var(--s-2);
  }

  .comment-edit-status {
    margin-right: auto;
    font-size: var(--fs-xs);
    color: var(--danger);
  }

  .comment-edit-status[hidden] {
    display: none;
  }

  .btn-mini {
    padding: var(--s-1) var(--s-3);
    background: transparent;
    border: 1px solid var(--border);
    color: var(--text-secondary);
    font-size: var(--fs-xs);
    font-weight: var(--fw-medium);
    cursor: pointer;
    transition: all var(--t-fast);
  }

  .btn-mini:hover:not(:disabled) {
    border-color: var(--accent);
    color: var(--accent);
  }

  .btn-mini:disabled {
    opacity: 0.5;
    cursor: not-allowed;
  }

  .btn-mini-primary {
    background: var(--accent);
    color: var(--accent-text);
    border-color: var(--accent);
  }

  .btn-mini-primary:hover:not(:disabled) {
    background: var(--accent-hover);
    border-color: var(--accent-hover);
    color: var(--accent-text);
  }

  .comment-actions {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: var(--s-3);
    margin-top: var(--s-1);
    flex-wrap: wrap;
  }

  .comment-action-btn {
    display: inline-flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: var(--s-1);
    padding: 0;
    background: none;
    border: none;
    font-size: var(--fs-xs);
    font-weight: var(--fw-medium);
    color: var(--text-muted);
    cursor: pointer;
    transition: color var(--t-fast);
  }

  .comment-action-btn:hover {
    color: var(--accent);
  }

  .comment-action-btn[hidden] {
    display: none;
  }

  .comment-action-btn.is-liked {
    color: var(--accent);
  }

  .like-icon {
    display: inline-block;
    font-size: 1rem;
    line-height: 1;
    transition: transform var(--t-fast);
  }

  .comment-action-btn.is-liked .like-icon {
    transform: scale(1.15);
  }

  .like-count {
    font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
  }

  .like-count[hidden] {
    display: none;
  }

  .comment-action-danger:hover {
    color: var(--danger);
  }

  .comment-action-report:hover {
    color: var(--warning);
  }

  .comment-replies {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: var(--s-3);
    list-style: none;
    padding: 0;
    margin: var(--s-2) 0 0 var(--s-6);
    padding-left: var(--s-3);
    border-left: 2px solid var(--border);
  }

  .comment-replies[hidden] {
    display: none;
  }

  .load-more-wrap {
    display: flex;
    justify-content: center;
    padding-block: var(--s-2);
  }

  .load-more-wrap[hidden] {
    display: none;
  }

  .load-more-btn {
    padding: var(--s-2) var(--s-5);
    background: transparent;
    border: 1px solid var(--border);
    color: var(--text-secondary);
    font-size: var(--fs-xs);
    font-weight: var(--fw-semibold);
    letter-spacing: 0.05em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    cursor: pointer;
    transition: all var(--t-fast);
  }

  .load-more-btn:hover {
    border-color: var(--accent);
    color: var(--accent);
  }
</style>

<script>
  interface AuthUser {
    sub: string;
    name: string;
    email: string;
    picture: string | null;
  }

  interface CommentItem {
    id: string;
    parentId: string | null;
    body: string;
    status: string;
    createdAt: number;
    editedAt: number | null;
    likes: number;
    isLiked: boolean;
    isOwn: boolean;
    isAdmin: boolean;
    canEdit: boolean;
    user: { sub: string; name: string; picture: string | null };
  }

  type SortKey = 'oldest' | 'newest' | 'popular';

  (function () {
    const CACHE_KEY = 'qimochi:user-cache';
    const SORT_KEY = 'qimochi:comment-sort';
    const COOLDOWN_SEC = 15;
    const PAGE_SIZE = 10;

    const root = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-comments]');
    if (!root) return;

    const SLUG = root.dataset.slug || '';
    const API = (root.dataset.api || '').replace(/\/$/, '');
    const SITE_KEY = root.dataset.sitekey || '';

    const countEl = root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-count]')!;
    const skeletonBox = root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-auth-skeleton]')!;
    const loginBox = root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-login-box]')!;
    const gsiSlot = root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-gsi-slot]')!;
    const userBox = root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-user-box]')!;
    const userAvatar = root.querySelector<HTMLImageElement>('[data-user-avatar]')!;
    const userName = root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-user-name]')!;
    const logoutBtn = root.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-logout]')!;
    const form = root.querySelector<HTMLFormElement>('[data-form]')!;
    const replyContext = root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-reply-context]')!;
    const replyLabel = root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-reply-label]')!;
    const replyCancel = root.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-reply-cancel]')!;
    const input = root.querySelector<HTMLTextAreaElement>('[data-input]')!;
    const charCount = root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-charcount]')!;
    const submitBtn = root.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-submit]')!;
    const formStatus = root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-form-status]')!;
    const toolbar = root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-toolbar]')!;
    const sortSelect = root.querySelector<HTMLSelectElement>('[data-sort]')!;
    const statusEl = root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-status]')!;
    const listEl = root.querySelector<HTMLOListElement>('[data-list]')!;
    const loadMoreWrap = root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-load-more-wrap]')!;
    const loadMoreBtn = root.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-load-more]')!;
    const tmpl = root.querySelector<HTMLTemplateElement>('[data-item-tmpl]')!;

    if (!SLUG || !API) {
      statusEl.textContent = 'Konfigurasi komentar tidak lengkap.';
      return;
    }

    let currentUser: AuthUser | null = null;
    let allComments: CommentItem[] = [];
    let rootComments: CommentItem[] = [];
    let childrenMap: Record<string, CommentItem[]> = {};
    let visibleRoots = PAGE_SIZE;
    let sortKey: SortKey = 'oldest';
    let turnstileWidgetId: string | null = null;
    let turnstileToken = '';
    let submitting = false;
    let gsiRendered = false;
    let replyTarget: { id: string; name: string } | null = null;
    let cooldownUntil = 0;
    let cooldownTimer: number | null = null;
    let highlightId: string | null = null;

    try {
      const saved = localStorage.getItem(SORT_KEY) as SortKey | null;
      if (saved === 'oldest' || saved === 'newest' || saved === 'popular') {
        sortKey = saved;
      }
    } catch {}

    sortSelect.value = sortKey;

    function readCache(): AuthUser | null {
      try {
        const raw = localStorage.getItem(CACHE_KEY);
        if (!raw) return null;
        const parsed = JSON.parse(raw) as Partial<AuthUser> | null;
        if (
          parsed &&
          typeof parsed.sub === 'string' &&
          typeof parsed.name === 'string'
        ) {
          return {
            sub: parsed.sub,
            name: parsed.name,
            email: typeof parsed.email === 'string' ? parsed.email : '',
            picture:
              typeof parsed.picture === 'string' ? parsed.picture : null,
          };
        }
      } catch {}
      return null;
    }

    function loadScript(src: string): Promise<void> {
      return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        const existing = document.querySelector<HTMLScriptElement>(
          `script[src="${src}"]`
        );
        if (existing) {
          if (existing.dataset.loaded === '1') return resolve();
          existing.addEventListener('load', () => resolve(), { once: true });
          existing.addEventListener('error', () => reject(), { once: true });
          return;
        }
        const s = document.createElement('script');
        s.src = src;
        s.async = true;
        s.defer = true;
        s.addEventListener(
          'load',
          () => {
            s.dataset.loaded = '1';
            resolve();
          },
          { once: true }
        );
        s.addEventListener('error', () => reject(), { once: true });
        document.head.appendChild(s);
      });
    }

    function escapeHtml(s: string): string {
      return s
        .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
        .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
        .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
        .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
        .replace(/'/g, '&#39;');
    }

    function renderMarkdown(text: string): string {
      let s = escapeHtml(text);

      s = s.replace(
        /\|\|([^|\n]+?)\|\|/g,
        '<span class="spoiler" data-spoiler>$1</span>'
      );

      s = s.replace(/`([^`\n]+)`/g, '<code>$1</code>');
      s = s.replace(/\*\*([^*\n]+)\*\*/g, '<strong>$1</strong>');
      s = s.replace(/(^|[^*])\*([^*\n]+)\*(?!\*)/g, '$1<em>$2</em>');
      s = s.replace(/~~([^~\n]+)~~/g, '<del>$1</del>');

      s = s.replace(
        /(^|\s)@([A-Za-z0-9_]{2,30})/g,
        '$1<span class="mention">@$2</span>'
      );

      s = s.replace(
        /\[([^\]]+)\]\((https?:\/\/[^\s)]+)\)/g,
        '<a href="$2" target="_blank" rel="nofollow ugc noopener noreferrer">$1</a>'
      );

      s = s.replace(
        /(^|[^"=])(https?:\/\/[^\s<]+)/g,
        (_, prefix, url) =>
          `${prefix}<a href="${url}" target="_blank" rel="nofollow ugc noopener noreferrer">${url}</a>`
      );

      s = s.replace(/\n/g, '<br>');

      return s;
    }

    function formatTime(ts: number): string {
      const d = new Date(ts * 1000);
      const now = Date.now();
      const diff = Math.floor((now - d.getTime()) / 1000);
      if (diff < 60) return 'baru saja';
      if (diff < 3600) return `${Math.floor(diff / 60)} menit lalu`;
      if (diff < 86400) return `${Math.floor(diff / 3600)} jam lalu`;
      if (diff < 604800) return `${Math.floor(diff / 86400)} hari lalu`;
      return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('id-ID', {
        day: 'numeric',
        month: 'short',
        year: 'numeric',
      }).format(d);
    }

    function statusBadgeText(status: string): string {
      if (status === 'pending') return 'Menunggu moderasi';
      if (status === 'rejected') return 'Ditolak';
      return '';
    }

    function sortRoots(items: CommentItem[]): CommentItem[] {
      const arr = [...items];
      if (sortKey === 'newest') {
        arr.sort((a, b) => b.createdAt - a.createdAt);
      } else if (sortKey === 'popular') {
        arr.sort((a, b) => {
          if (b.likes !== a.likes) return b.likes - a.likes;
          return b.createdAt - a.createdAt;
        });
      } else {
        arr.sort((a, b) => a.createdAt - b.createdAt);
      }
      return arr;
    }

    function makeItem(item: CommentItem, isReply: boolean): HTMLLIElement {
      const node = tmpl.content.firstElementChild!.cloneNode(
        true
      ) as HTMLLIElement;
      node.dataset.id = item.id;
      node.dataset.parentId = item.parentId ?? '';

      const avatar = node.querySelector<HTMLImageElement>('[data-avatar]')!;
      if (item.user.picture) {
        avatar.src = item.user.picture;
        avatar.alt = item.user.name;
      } else {
        avatar.removeAttribute('src');
        avatar.style.background = 'var(--bg-surface)';
      }

      node.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-name]')!.textContent =
        item.user.name;

      const roleEl = node.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-role]')!;
      if (item.isAdmin) {
        roleEl.hidden = false;
      }

      const timeEl = node.querySelector<HTMLTimeElement>('[data-time]')!;
      timeEl.dateTime = new Date(item.createdAt * 1000).toISOString();
      timeEl.textContent = formatTime(item.createdAt);

      const editedEl = node.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-edited]')!;
      if (item.editedAt) editedEl.hidden = false;

      const bodyEl = node.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-body]')!;
      bodyEl.innerHTML = renderMarkdown(item.body);

      bodyEl.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('[data-spoiler]').forEach((el) => {
        el.addEventListener('click', () => {
          el.classList.toggle('revealed');
        });
      });

      const badge = node.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-badge]')!;
      if (item.isOwn && item.status !== 'approved') {
        const text = statusBadgeText(item.status);
        if (text) {
          badge.textContent = text;
          badge.classList.add(
            item.status === 'rejected' ? 'is-rejected' : 'is-pending'
          );
          badge.hidden = false;
        }
      }

      const likeBtn = node.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>(
        '[data-action="like"]'
      )!;
      likeBtn.dataset.id = item.id;

      const likeIcon = likeBtn.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-like-icon]')!;
      const likeCount = likeBtn.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-like-count]')!;

      likeIcon.textContent = item.isLiked ? '♥' : '♡';
      if (item.likes > 0) {
        likeCount.textContent = String(item.likes);
        likeCount.hidden = false;
      }
      if (item.isLiked) likeBtn.classList.add('is-liked');
      if (!currentUser) likeBtn.hidden = true;

      const replyBtn = node.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>(
        '[data-action="reply"]'
      )!;
      if (isReply || !currentUser) {
        replyBtn.hidden = true;
      } else {
        replyBtn.dataset.id = item.id;
        replyBtn.dataset.name = item.user.name;
      }

      const editBtn = node.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>(
        '[data-action="edit"]'
      )!;
      if (item.canEdit) {
        editBtn.hidden = false;
        editBtn.dataset.id = item.id;
      }

      const deleteBtn = node.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>(
        '[data-action="delete"]'
      )!;
      if (item.isOwn) {
        deleteBtn.hidden = false;
        deleteBtn.dataset.id = item.id;
        if (item.parentId === null) deleteBtn.dataset.root = '1';
      }

      const reportBtn = node.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>(
        '[data-action="report"]'
      )!;
      if (currentUser && !item.isOwn) {
        reportBtn.hidden = false;
        reportBtn.dataset.id = item.id;
      }

      const editBox = node.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-edit-box]')!;
      const editInput = node.querySelector<HTMLTextAreaElement>(
        '[data-edit-input]'
      )!;
      editInput.value = item.body;
      editBox.dataset.id = item.id;

      return node;
    }

    function render(): void {
      const visible = rootComments.slice(0, visibleRoots);

      listEl.innerHTML = '';
      for (const r of visible) {
        const node = makeItem(r, false);
        const replies = childrenMap[r.id] ?? [];
        if (replies.length > 0) {
          const repliesEl = node.querySelector<HTMLOListElement>(
            '[data-replies]'
          )!;
          for (const reply of replies) {
            repliesEl.appendChild(makeItem(reply, true));
          }
          repliesEl.hidden = false;
        }
        listEl.appendChild(node);
      }

      countEl.textContent = String(allComments.length);
      listEl.hidden = visible.length === 0;

      const remaining = rootComments.length - visible.length;
      if (remaining > 0) {
        loadMoreWrap.hidden = false;
        loadMoreBtn.textContent = `Muat lebih banyak (${remaining})`;
      } else {
        loadMoreWrap.hidden = true;
      }

      toolbar.hidden = rootComments.length === 0;

      if (highlightId) {
        const target = listEl.querySelector<HTMLElement>(
          `[data-id="${highlightId}"]`
        );
        if (target) {
          target.classList.add('just-posted');
          target.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' });
          window.setTimeout(() => {
            target.classList.remove('just-posted');
          }, 2500);
          highlightId = null;
        }
      }
    }

    async function fetchComments(): Promise<void> {
      statusEl.hidden = false;
      statusEl.textContent = 'Memuat komentar…';
      listEl.hidden = true;
      try {
        const res = await fetch(
          `${API}/api/v1/comments?slug=${encodeURIComponent(SLUG)}`,
          { credentials: 'include' }
        );
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}`);
        const json = (await res.json()) as { data: CommentItem[] };
        allComments = Array.isArray(json.data) ? json.data : [];

        rootComments = allComments.filter((c) => c.parentId === null);
        childrenMap = {};
        for (const c of allComments) {
          if (c.parentId) {
            (childrenMap[c.parentId] ??= []).push(c);
          }
        }

        for (const key of Object.keys(childrenMap)) {
          childrenMap[key]!.sort((a, b) => a.createdAt - b.createdAt);
        }

        rootComments = sortRoots(rootComments);
        visibleRoots = Math.max(PAGE_SIZE, visibleRoots);

        if (allComments.length === 0) {
          statusEl.textContent = 'Belum ada komentar. Jadilah yang pertama.';
          listEl.hidden = true;
          loadMoreWrap.hidden = true;
          toolbar.hidden = true;
          countEl.textContent = '0';
          return;
        }

        render();
        statusEl.hidden = true;
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('[comments] fetch failed', err);
        statusEl.textContent = 'Gagal memuat komentar. Coba refresh.';
      }
    }

    function renderGsiButton(): void {
      if (gsiRendered) return;
      if (currentUser) return;
      const g = (window as any).google;
      if (!g?.accounts?.id) return;
      try {
        g.accounts.id.renderButton(gsiSlot, {
          theme: 'outline',
          size: 'large',
          text: 'signin_with',
          shape: 'pill',
        });
        gsiRendered = true;
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('[comments] render gsi failed', err);
      }
    }

    function applyUserState(): void {
      skeletonBox.hidden = true;

      if (currentUser) {
        loginBox.hidden = true;
        userBox.hidden = false;
        form.hidden = false;
        userName.textContent = currentUser.name;
        if (currentUser.picture) {
          userAvatar.src = currentUser.picture;
          userAvatar.alt = currentUser.name;
        } else {
          userAvatar.removeAttribute('src');
        }
        mountTurnstile();
      } else {
        loginBox.hidden = false;
        userBox.hidden = true;
        form.hidden = true;
        gsiRendered = false;
        gsiSlot.innerHTML = '';
        renderGsiButton();
      }
    }

    function mountTurnstile(): void {
      if (!SITE_KEY) return;
      if (turnstileWidgetId) return;
      const container = root!.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-turnstile]');
      if (!container) return;
      const ts = (window as any).turnstile;
      if (!ts) return;
      try {
        turnstileWidgetId = ts.render(container, {
          sitekey: SITE_KEY,
          callback: (token: string) => {
            turnstileToken = token;
            updateSubmitState();
          },
          'expired-callback': () => {
            turnstileToken = '';
            updateSubmitState();
          },
          'error-callback': () => {
            turnstileToken = '';
            updateSubmitState();
          },
        });
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('[turnstile] render failed', err);
      }
    }

    function isCooldown(): boolean {
      return Date.now() < cooldownUntil;
    }

    function updateSubmitState(): void {
      if (isCooldown()) return;
      const text = input.value.trim();
      const ok =
        !!currentUser && text.length >= 2 && !!turnstileToken && !submitting;
      submitBtn.disabled = !ok;
      submitBtn.textContent = replyTarget ? 'Balas' : 'Kirim';
    }

    function tickCooldown(): void {
      const remain = cooldownUntil - Date.now();
      if (remain <= 0) {
        if (cooldownTimer !== null) {
          window.clearInterval(cooldownTimer);
          cooldownTimer = null;
        }
        cooldownUntil = 0;
        updateSubmitState();
        return;
      }
      submitBtn.disabled = true;
      submitBtn.textContent = `Tunggu ${Math.ceil(remain / 1000)}s`;
    }

    function startCooldown(): void {
      cooldownUntil = Date.now() + COOLDOWN_SEC * 1000;
      if (cooldownTimer !== null) window.clearInterval(cooldownTimer);
      cooldownTimer = window.setInterval(tickCooldown, 500);
      tickCooldown();
    }

    function setFormStatus(
      text: string,
      kind: 'error' | 'success' | null
    ): void {
      formStatus.textContent = text;
      formStatus.classList.remove('is-error', 'is-success');
      if (kind === 'error') formStatus.classList.add('is-error');
      if (kind === 'success') formStatus.classList.add('is-success');
      formStatus.hidden = !text;
    }

    function setReplyTarget(target: { id: string; name: string } | null): void {
      replyTarget = target;
      if (target) {
        replyLabel.textContent = `Membalas ${target.name}`;
        replyContext.hidden = false;
        form.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' });
        window.setTimeout(() => input.focus(), 300);
      } else {
        replyContext.hidden = true;
        replyLabel.textContent = '';
      }
      updateSubmitState();
    }

    function setCurrentUser(user: AuthUser | null): void {
      const sameUser = (user?.sub ?? null) === (currentUser?.sub ?? null);
      if (sameUser) return;
      currentUser = user;
      applyUserState();
      updateSubmitState();
      fetchComments();
    }

    function onAuthChanged(e: Event): void {
      const detail = (e as CustomEvent<{ user: AuthUser | null }>).detail;
      if (!detail) return;
      setCurrentUser(detail.user);
    }

    document.addEventListener('qimochi:auth-changed', onAuthChanged);
    document.addEventListener('qimochi:gsi-ready', () => renderGsiButton());

    if ((window as any).google?.accounts?.id) {
      renderGsiButton();
    }

    logoutBtn.addEventListener('click', async () => {
      const api = (window as any).__qimochiUserMenu;
      if (api) api.close();
      try {
        await fetch(`${API}/api/v1/auth/logout`, {
          method: 'POST',
          credentials: 'include',
        });
      } catch {}
      setCurrentUser(null);
      turnstileToken = '';
      setFormStatus('', null);
      setReplyTarget(null);
      document.dispatchEvent(
        new CustomEvent('qimochi:auth-changed', { detail: { user: null } })
      );
    });

    replyCancel.addEventListener('click', () => setReplyTarget(null));

    sortSelect.addEventListener('change', () => {
      const v = sortSelect.value as SortKey;
      if (v !== 'oldest' && v !== 'newest' && v !== 'popular') return;
      sortKey = v;
      try {
        localStorage.setItem(SORT_KEY, v);
      } catch {}
      rootComments = sortRoots(rootComments);
      visibleRoots = PAGE_SIZE;
      render();
    });

    loadMoreBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      visibleRoots += PAGE_SIZE;
      render();
    });

    input.addEventListener('input', () => {
      const len = input.value.length;
      charCount.textContent = `${len} / 2000`;
      updateSubmitState();
    });

    listEl.addEventListener('click', async (e) => {
      const target = e.target as HTMLElement;

      const likeBtn = target.closest<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-action="like"]');
      if (likeBtn) {
        const id = likeBtn.dataset.id;
        if (!id || !currentUser) return;
        likeBtn.disabled = true;
        try {
          const res = await fetch(
            `${API}/api/v1/comments/${encodeURIComponent(id)}/like`,
            { method: 'POST', credentials: 'include' }
          );
          if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}`);
          const json = (await res.json()) as { liked: boolean; likes: number };

          const item = allComments.find((c) => c.id === id);
          if (item) {
            item.isLiked = json.liked;
            item.likes = json.likes;
          }

          const icon = likeBtn.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-like-icon]')!;
          const count = likeBtn.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-like-count]')!;
          icon.textContent = json.liked ? '♥' : '♡';
          if (json.likes > 0) {
            count.textContent = String(json.likes);
            count.hidden = false;
          } else {
            count.hidden = true;
          }
          likeBtn.classList.toggle('is-liked', json.liked);
        } catch (err) {
          console.error('[comments] like failed', err);
        } finally {
          likeBtn.disabled = false;
        }
        return;
      }

      const replyBtn = target.closest<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-action="reply"]');
      if (replyBtn) {
        const id = replyBtn.dataset.id;
        const name = replyBtn.dataset.name;
        if (!id || !name) return;
        setReplyTarget({ id, name });
        return;
      }

      const editBtn = target.closest<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-action="edit"]');
      if (editBtn) {
        const itemNode = editBtn.closest<HTMLElement>('[data-comment-item]');
        if (!itemNode) return;
        const body = itemNode.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-body]')!;
        const editBox = itemNode.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-edit-box]')!;
        const editInput = itemNode.querySelector<HTMLTextAreaElement>(
          '[data-edit-input]'
        )!;
        const actions = itemNode.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-actions]')!;
        body.hidden = true;
        actions.hidden = true;
        editBox.hidden = false;
        editInput.focus();
        const len = editInput.value.length;
        editInput.setSelectionRange(len, len);
        return;
      }

      const cancelBtn = target.closest<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-edit-cancel]');
      if (cancelBtn) {
        const itemNode = cancelBtn.closest<HTMLElement>('[data-comment-item]');
        if (!itemNode) return;
        const body = itemNode.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-body]')!;
        const editBox = itemNode.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-edit-box]')!;
        const actions = itemNode.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-actions]')!;
        const editStatus = itemNode.querySelector<HTMLElement>(
          '[data-edit-status]'
        )!;
        body.hidden = false;
        actions.hidden = false;
        editBox.hidden = true;
        editStatus.hidden = true;
        return;
      }

      const saveBtn = target.closest<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-edit-save]');
      if (saveBtn) {
        const itemNode = saveBtn.closest<HTMLElement>('[data-comment-item]');
        if (!itemNode) return;
        const id = itemNode.dataset.id;
        if (!id) return;
        const editInput = itemNode.querySelector<HTMLTextAreaElement>(
          '[data-edit-input]'
        )!;
        const editStatus = itemNode.querySelector<HTMLElement>(
          '[data-edit-status]'
        )!;
        const text = editInput.value.trim();
        if (text.length < 2) {
          editStatus.textContent = 'Komentar minimal 2 karakter.';
          editStatus.hidden = false;
          return;
        }
        saveBtn.disabled = true;
        editStatus.hidden = true;
        try {
          const res = await fetch(
            `${API}/api/v1/comments/${encodeURIComponent(id)}`,
            {
              method: 'PATCH',
              credentials: 'include',
              headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
              body: JSON.stringify({ body: text }),
            }
          );
          if (!res.ok) {
            const json = (await res.json()) as {
              error?: { code?: string; message: string };
            };
            editStatus.textContent =
              json.error?.message ?? `Gagal (HTTP ${res.status})`;
            editStatus.hidden = false;
            return;
          }
          await fetchComments();
        } catch (err) {
          console.error('[comments] edit failed', err);
          editStatus.textContent = 'Gagal menyimpan. Coba lagi.';
          editStatus.hidden = false;
        } finally {
          saveBtn.disabled = false;
        }
        return;
      }

      const deleteBtn = target.closest<HTMLButtonElement>(
        '[data-action="delete"]'
      );
      if (deleteBtn) {
        const id = deleteBtn.dataset.id;
        if (!id) return;
        const isRoot = deleteBtn.dataset.root === '1';
        const msg = isRoot
          ? 'Hapus komentar ini beserta semua balasannya?'
          : 'Hapus komentar ini?';
        if (!window.confirm(msg)) return;

        deleteBtn.disabled = true;
        try {
          const res = await fetch(
            `${API}/api/v1/comments/${encodeURIComponent(id)}`,
            { method: 'DELETE', credentials: 'include' }
          );
          if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}`);
          await fetchComments();
        } catch (err) {
          console.error('[comments] delete failed', err);
          window.alert('Gagal menghapus komentar.');
          deleteBtn.disabled = false;
        }
        return;
      }

      const reportBtn = target.closest<HTMLButtonElement>(
        '[data-action="report"]'
      );
      if (reportBtn) {
        const id = reportBtn.dataset.id;
        if (!id) return;
        const reason = window.prompt(
          'Alasan melaporkan komentar ini? (spam, kasar, spoiler, dll)'
        );
        if (reason === null) return;
        reportBtn.disabled = true;
        try {
          const res = await fetch(
            `${API}/api/v1/comments/${encodeURIComponent(id)}/report`,
            {
              method: 'POST',
              credentials: 'include',
              headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
              body: JSON.stringify({ reason: reason.trim() }),
            }
          );
          if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}`);
          window.alert('Laporan terkirim. Terima kasih.');
        } catch (err) {
          console.error('[comments] report failed', err);
          window.alert('Gagal mengirim laporan.');
        } finally {
          reportBtn.disabled = false;
        }
        return;
      }
    });

    form.addEventListener('submit', async (e) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      if (submitting || isCooldown()) return;

      const text = input.value.trim();
      if (text.length < 2) {
        setFormStatus('Komentar minimal 2 karakter.', 'error');
        return;
      }
      if (!turnstileToken) {
        setFormStatus('Selesaikan captcha dulu.', 'error');
        return;
      }

      submitting = true;
      updateSubmitState();
      setFormStatus('Mengirim…', null);

      try {
        const res = await fetch(`${API}/api/v1/comments`, {
          method: 'POST',
          credentials: 'include',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify({
            slug: SLUG,
            body: text,
            parentId: replyTarget?.id ?? null,
            turnstileToken,
          }),
        });

        const json = (await res.json()) as {
          ok?: boolean;
          status?: string;
          reason?: string;
          data?: { id: string };
          error?: { code?: string; message: string };
        };

        if (!res.ok) {
          if (json.error?.code === 'COMMENT_REJECTED') {
            turnstileToken = '';
            if (turnstileWidgetId) {
              try {
                (window as any).turnstile.reset(turnstileWidgetId);
              } catch {}
            }
            setFormStatus(json.error.message, 'error');
            input.focus();
            updateSubmitState();
            return;
          }

          const msg = json.error?.message ?? `Gagal (HTTP ${res.status})`;
          setFormStatus(msg, 'error');
          return;
        }

        const newId = json.data?.id ?? null;
        input.value = '';
        charCount.textContent = '0 / 2000';
        turnstileToken = '';
        if (turnstileWidgetId) {
          try {
            (window as any).turnstile.reset(turnstileWidgetId);
          } catch {}
        }
        setReplyTarget(null);

        const newStatus = json.status ?? 'pending';

        if (newStatus === 'approved') {
          setFormStatus('Komentar kamu langsung tampil.', 'success');
        } else {
          setFormStatus('Komentar terkirim. Menunggu moderasi.', 'success');
        }
        startCooldown();
        if (newId) highlightId = newId;
        await fetchComments();
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('[comments] submit failed', err);
        setFormStatus('Gagal mengirim. Coba lagi.', 'error');
      } finally {
        submitting = false;
        if (!isCooldown()) updateSubmitState();
      }
    });

    (function bootstrap(): void {
      currentUser = readCache();
      applyUserState();
      updateSubmitState();
    })();

    (async () => {
      await Promise.allSettled([
        fetchComments(),
        loadScript(
          'https://challenges.cloudflare.com/turnstile/v0/api.js?render=explicit'
        )
          .then(() => {
            if (currentUser) mountTurnstile();
          })
          .catch((err) =>
            console.error('[comments] turnstile load failed', err)
          ),
      ]);

      const menu = (window as any).__qimochiUserMenu;
      if (menu) {
        const u = menu.getUser();
        if ((u?.sub ?? null) !== (currentUser?.sub ?? null)) {
          setCurrentUser(u ?? null);
        }
      }

      renderGsiButton();
      updateSubmitState();
    })();
  })();
</script>

<script>
  interface Server { name: string; url: string; }
  interface Download { quality: string; size: string; servers: Server[]; }

  function esc(s: string): string {
    return s.replace(/&/g, '&amp;').replace(/</g, '&lt;').replace(/>/g, '&gt;').replace(/"/g, '&quot;');
  }

  const CHEVRON_SVG = '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="14" height="14" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="square" stroke-linejoin="miter" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M6 9l6 6l6 -6"/></svg>';
  const DL_SVG = '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="14" height="14" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="square" stroke-linejoin="miter" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M8 2v8M4 7l4 4 4-4M3 14h10"/></svg>';

  function buildHtml(downloads: Download[]): string {
    if (!downloads || downloads.length === 0) return '';

    return downloads
      .map((d) => {
        const servers = d.servers
          .map(
            (srv) =>
              `<a href="${esc(srv.url)}" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer nofollow" class="dl-link">${DL_SVG}<span>${esc(srv.name)}</span></a>`
          )
          .join('');
        return `<details class="quality-item"><summary><span class="quality">${esc(d.quality)}</span><span class="size">· ${esc(d.size)}</span><span class="chevron" aria-hidden="true">${CHEVRON_SVG}</span></summary><div class="servers">${servers}</div></details>`;
      })
      .join('');
  }

  function mount(root: HTMLElement, downloads: Download[]): void {
    const outer = root.querySelector<HTMLDetailsElement>('[data-download-outer]');
    const list = root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-download-list]');
    if (!outer || !list) return;

    const has = Array.isArray(downloads) && downloads.length > 0;
    root.hidden = !has;

    if (!has) {
      list.innerHTML = '';
      outer.open = false;
      return;
    }

    list.innerHTML = buildHtml(downloads);
    outer.open = false;
  }

  (window as any).__qimochiDownload = { mount };
</script>
===== FILE: src/components/EpisodeActionBar.astro =====

import Icon from '~/user-components/Icons.astro';
import { URLS } from '~/constants';

interface NavItem {
  number: number;
  title?: string | null;
  duration?: number | null;
}

interface Props {
  slug?: string;
  animeTitle?: string;
  currentEpisode?: number;
  totalEpisodes?: number;
  prevEpisode?: NavItem | null;
  nextEpisode?: NavItem | null;
  thumbnail?: string;
  class?: string;
}

const {
  slug = '',
  animeTitle = '',
  currentEpisode = 1,
  totalEpisodes = 0,
  prevEpisode = null,
  nextEpisode = null,
  thumbnail = '',
  class: className,
} = Astro.props;

const watchBase = URLS.watch(slug, 1);

function formatDur(d?: number | null): string | null {
  if (typeof d !== 'number' || d <= 0) return null;
  return `${d}m`;
}

function epLabel(item: NavItem): string {
  return item.title
    ? `E${item.number} – ${item.title}`
    : `Episode ${item.number}`;
}

const prevDur = formatDur(prevEpisode?.duration);
const nextDur = formatDur(nextEpisode?.duration);
---

<nav
  class:list={['ep-nav', className]}
  data-action-bar
  data-watch-base={watchBase}
  data-slug={slug}
  aria-label="Navigasi episode"
>
  <div class="ep-nav__stack">
    <div
      class="ep-nav__slot"
      data-nav-prev-wrap
      hidden={!prevEpisode}
    >
      <span class="ep-nav__label">Episode Sebelumnya</span>

<style>

<script>
  interface NavItem {
    number: number;
    title: string | null;
    duration: number | null;
  }

  interface MountData {
    slug: string;
    current: number;
    total: number;
    thumbnail: string;
    prev: NavItem | null;
    next: NavItem | null;
  }

  interface ActionBarAPI {
    mount(root: HTMLElement, data: MountData): void;
  }

  function resolveNav(root: HTMLElement): HTMLElement | null {
    if (root.hasAttribute('data-action-bar')) return root;
    return root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-action-bar]');
  }

  function urlFor(watchBase: string, ep: number): string {
    if (!watchBase) return '#';
    return ep <= 1 ? watchBase : `${watchBase}?ep=${ep}`;
  }

  function titleText(item: NavItem): string {
    return item.title
      ? `E${item.number} – ${item.title}`
      : `Episode ${item.number}`;
  }

  function durText(item: NavItem): string | null {
    if (typeof item.duration !== 'number' || item.duration <= 0) return null;
    return `${item.duration}m`;
  }

  function renderSlot(
    slot: HTMLElement | null,
    item: NavItem | null,
    watchBase: string,
    kind: 'prev' | 'next',
    thumbnail: string
  ): void {
    if (!slot) return;

    if (!item) {
      slot.hidden = true;
      return;
    }

    slot.hidden = false;

    const card = slot.querySelector<HTMLAnchorElement>('.ep-nav__card');
    if (!card) return;

    const img = card.querySelector<HTMLImageElement>('.ep-nav__thumb img');
    const fallbackEl = card.querySelector<HTMLElement>('.ep-nav__thumb-fallback');
    const durEl = card.querySelector<HTMLElement>('.ep-nav__dur');
    const titleEl = card.querySelector<HTMLElement>(
      '[data-nav-prev-label], [data-nav-next-label]'
    );
    const metaEl = card.querySelector<HTMLElement>('.ep-nav__meta');

    card.href = urlFor(watchBase, item.number);
    card.setAttribute('data-episode-nav', String(item.number));
    card.setAttribute(
      'aria-label',
      kind === 'prev'
        ? `Episode sebelumnya, Ep ${item.number}`
        : `Episode berikutnya, Ep ${item.number}`
    );

    if (img && thumbnail) {
      img.src = thumbnail;
      img.hidden = false;
      if (fallbackEl) fallbackEl.hidden = true;
    } else if (img) {
      img.hidden = true;
      if (fallbackEl) {
        fallbackEl.hidden = false;
        fallbackEl.textContent = String(item.number).padStart(2, '0');
      }
    }

    const d = durText(item);
    if (durEl) {
      if (d) {
        durEl.textContent = d;
        durEl.hidden = false;
      } else {
        durEl.hidden = true;
      }
    }

    if (titleEl) titleEl.textContent = titleText(item);
    if (metaEl) metaEl.textContent = 'Sub Indo';
  }

  function mount(root: HTMLElement, data: MountData): void {
    const nav = resolveNav(root);
    if (!nav) return;

    const watchBase = nav.dataset.watchBase ?? '';
    if (!watchBase) return;

    const prevWrap = nav.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-nav-prev-wrap]');
    const nextWrap = nav.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-nav-next-wrap]');

    renderSlot(prevWrap, data.prev, watchBase, 'prev', data.thumbnail);
    renderSlot(nextWrap, data.next, watchBase, 'next', data.thumbnail);
  }

  (window as any).__qimochiActionBar = { mount } satisfies ActionBarAPI;
</script>

===== FILE: src/components/EpisodeList.astro =====

import { URLS } from '~/constants';
import Icon from '~/user-components/Icons.astro';

interface EpisodeItem {
  number: number;
  title?: string | null;
  duration?: number | null;
  aired?: string | null;
}

interface Props {
  slug?: string;
  animeTitle?: string;
  episodes?: EpisodeItem[];
  currentEpisode?: number | null;
  cover?: string;
  banner?: string;
  duration?: number | null;
}

const {
  slug = '',
  animeTitle = '',
  episodes = [],
  currentEpisode = null,
  cover = '',
  banner = '',
  duration = null,
} = Astro.props;

const PAGE_SIZE = 12;

const sorted = [...episodes].sort((a, b) => a.number - b.number);
const total = sorted.length;

if (total === 0) return null;

let initialLimit = PAGE_SIZE;
if (currentEpisode != null) {
  const activeIdx = sorted.findIndex((e) => e.number === currentEpisode);
  if (activeIdx >= 0) {
    initialLimit = Math.ceil((activeIdx + 1) / PAGE_SIZE) * PAGE_SIZE;
  }
}

const cardThumb = banner || cover;

function cleanText(s: string | null | undefined): string | null {
  if (typeof s !== 'string') return null;
  const t = s.trim();
  return t.length > 0 ? t : null;
}

function resolveDuration(epDuration?: number | null): string | null {
  const d = epDuration ?? duration;
  if (typeof d !== 'number' || d <= 0) return null;
  return `${d}m`;
}

function formatAired(raw: string | null | undefined): string | null {
  if (typeof raw !== 'string') return null;
  const t = raw.trim();
  if (!t) return null;
  const d = new Date(t);
  if (isNaN(d.getTime())) return null;
  try {
    return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('id-ID', {
      day: 'numeric',
      month: 'short',
      year: 'numeric',
    }).format(d);
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

const cleanAnimeTitle = cleanText(animeTitle);

const episodeRows = sorted.map((ep, index) => ({
  number: ep.number,
  title: cleanText(ep.title),
  durationLabel: resolveDuration(ep.duration),
  airedLabel: formatAired(ep.aired),
  isActive: currentEpisode != null && ep.number === currentEpisode,
  isHidden: index >= initialLimit,
}));

function formatEp(n: number): string {
  return String(n).padStart(2, '0');
}

const initialEpisodeAttr =
  currentEpisode != null ? String(currentEpisode) : '';
---

<section
  class="section"

    {episodeRows.map((row) => (
      <li

      type="button"

<script>
  // @ts-nocheck
  (function () {
    const root = document.querySelector('[data-episode-list]');
    if (!root) return;

    const items = Array.from(root.querySelectorAll('[data-episode-item]'));
    const loadMoreBtn = root.querySelector('[data-episode-loadmore]');

    const pageSize = Number(root.dataset.pageSize || 12);
    const total = Number(root.dataset.total || items.length);

    let visible = items.filter((el) => !el.hidden).length;
    if (visible < pageSize) visible = pageSize;

    function applyVisibility() {
      for (let i = 0; i < items.length; i++) {
        items[i].hidden = i >= visible;
      }
      if (loadMoreBtn) {
        loadMoreBtn.hidden = visible >= total;
      }
    }

    if (loadMoreBtn) {
      loadMoreBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        visible += pageSize;
        applyVisibility();
      });
    }

    function setActive(n) {
      for (const el of items) {
        const active = Number(el.dataset.number) === n;
        el.classList.toggle('is-active', active);
        if (active) el.setAttribute('aria-current', 'page');
        else el.removeAttribute('aria-current');
        if (active && el.hidden) {
          const idx = items.indexOf(el);
          visible = Math.ceil((idx + 1) / pageSize) * pageSize;
        }
      }
      applyVisibility();
      const activeEl = items.find(
        (el) => Number(el.dataset.number) === n && !el.hidden
      );
      if (activeEl && typeof activeEl.scrollIntoView === 'function') {
        activeEl.scrollIntoView({ block: 'nearest', behavior: 'smooth' });
      }
    }

    window.__qimochiEpisodeList = { setActive };

    const attr = (root.dataset.initialEpisode || '').trim();
    const initialEp = attr ? parseInt(attr, 10) : NaN;
    const sp = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
    const epParam = parseInt(sp.get('ep') ?? '', 10);
    const target = !isNaN(epParam) ? epParam : initialEp;
    if (!isNaN(target)) setActive(target);
  })();
</script>

<script>
  type MyVote = 1 | 0 | -1;

  interface VoteState {
    likes: number;
    dislikes: number;
    myVote: MyVote;
    canVote: boolean;
  }

  const DEBOUNCE_MS = 250;

  function formatCount(n: number): string {
    if (n >= 1_000_000) return (n / 1_000_000).toFixed(1).replace(/\.0$/, '') + 'M';
    if (n >= 1_000) return (n / 1_000).toFixed(1).replace(/\.0$/, '') + 'K';
    return String(n);
  }

  function storageKey(slug: string, ep: number): string {
    return `qimochi:votes:v2:${slug}:${ep}`;
  }

  function isMyVote(v: unknown): v is MyVote {
    return v === 1 || v === -1 || v === 0;
  }

  function loadLocal(slug: string, ep: number): VoteState | null {
    try {
      const raw = localStorage.getItem(storageKey(slug, ep));
      if (!raw) return null;
      const p = JSON.parse(raw) as Partial<VoteState>;
      return {
        likes: typeof p.likes === 'number' ? p.likes : 0,
        dislikes: typeof p.dislikes === 'number' ? p.dislikes : 0,
        myVote: isMyVote(p.myVote) ? p.myVote : 0,
        canVote: false,
      };
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  }

  function saveLocal(slug: string, ep: number, state: VoteState): void {
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(
        storageKey(slug, ep),
        JSON.stringify({
          likes: state.likes,
          dislikes: state.dislikes,
          myVote: state.myVote,
        })
      );
    } catch {}
  }

  function parseRemote(json: unknown): VoteState | null {
    const data = (json as {
      data?: {
        likes?: number;
        dislikes?: number;
        userVote?: number;
        canVote?: boolean;
      };
    })?.data;
    if (!data) return null;

    const likes = typeof data.likes === 'number' ? data.likes : 0;
    const dislikes = typeof data.dislikes === 'number' ? data.dislikes : 0;
    const myVote: MyVote =
      data.userVote === 1 || data.userVote === -1 ? data.userVote : 0;
    const canVote = data.canVote === true;

    return { likes, dislikes, myVote, canVote };
  }

  async function fetchRemote(
    apiBase: string,
    slug: string,
    ep: number
  ): Promise<VoteState | null> {
    if (!apiBase) return null;
    try {
      const url = `${apiBase.replace(/\/$/, '')}/api/v1/votes?target=episode&slug=${encodeURIComponent(slug)}&ep=${ep}`;
      const res = await fetch(url, { credentials: 'include' });
      if (!res.ok) return null;
      return parseRemote(await res.json());
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  }

  async function submitRemote(
    apiBase: string,
    slug: string,
    ep: number,
    value: MyVote
  ): Promise<{ state: VoteState | null; unauthorized: boolean }> {
    if (!apiBase) return { state: null, unauthorized: false };
    try {
      const url = `${apiBase.replace(/\/$/, '')}/api/v1/votes`;
      const res = await fetch(url, {
        method: 'POST',
        credentials: 'include',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ target: 'episode', slug, ep, value }),
      });

      if (res.status === 401) {
        return { state: null, unauthorized: true };
      }
      if (!res.ok) return { state: null, unauthorized: false };

      return { state: parseRemote(await res.json()), unauthorized: false };
    } catch {
      return { state: null, unauthorized: false };
    }
  }

  function openUserMenu(): void {
    const menu = (window as any).__qimochiUserMenu;
    if (menu?.open) {
      menu.open();
      return;
    }
    window.location.href = '/?login=1';
  }

  function init(root: HTMLElement): void {
    if (root.dataset.bound === '1') return;
    root.dataset.bound = '1';

    const slug = root.dataset.slug ?? '';
    const ep = parseInt(root.dataset.ep ?? '0', 10);
    const api = root.dataset.api ?? '';
    if (!slug || !ep) return;

    const likeBtn = root.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-vote="1"]');
    const dislikeBtn = root.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-vote="-1"]');
    const likeCount = root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-count="like"]');
    const dislikeCount = root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-count="dislike"]');

    if (!likeBtn || !dislikeBtn || !likeCount || !dislikeCount) return;

    let state: VoteState = loadLocal(slug, ep) ?? {
      likes: 0,
      dislikes: 0,
      myVote: 0,
      canVote: false,
    };
    let busy = false;

    function render(): void {
      likeCount.textContent = formatCount(state.likes);
      dislikeCount.textContent = formatCount(state.dislikes);

      likeBtn.classList.toggle('is-active', state.myVote === 1);
      dislikeBtn.classList.toggle('is-active', state.myVote === -1);
      likeBtn.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(state.myVote === 1));
      dislikeBtn.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(state.myVote === -1));

      likeBtn.disabled = busy;
      dislikeBtn.disabled = busy;

      const label = state.canVote ? null : 'Login untuk vote';
      likeBtn.title = label ?? 'Suka episode ini';
      dislikeBtn.title = label ?? 'Tidak suka episode ini';
    }

    async function vote(next: 1 | -1): Promise<void> {
      if (busy) return;

      if (!state.canVote) {
        openUserMenu();
        return;
      }

      busy = true;
      render();

      const prev = state.myVote;
      const newVote: MyVote = prev === next ? 0 : next;

      const likesDelta = (newVote === 1 ? 1 : 0) - (prev === 1 ? 1 : 0);
      const dislikesDelta = (newVote === -1 ? 1 : 0) - (prev === -1 ? 1 : 0);

      state = {
        ...state,
        likes: Math.max(0, state.likes + likesDelta),
        dislikes: Math.max(0, state.dislikes + dislikesDelta),
        myVote: newVote,
      };
      saveLocal(slug, ep, state);
      render();

      if (api) {
        const { state: remote, unauthorized } = await submitRemote(
          api,
          slug,
          ep,
          newVote
        );

        if (unauthorized) {
          state = { ...state, canVote: false };
          const fetched = await fetchRemote(api, slug, ep);
          if (fetched) state = fetched;
          saveLocal(slug, ep, state);
          render();
          busy = false;
          openUserMenu();
          return;
        }

        if (remote) {
          state = remote;
          saveLocal(slug, ep, state);
        }
      }

      busy = false;
      render();
    }

    likeBtn.addEventListener('click', () => vote(1));
    dislikeBtn.addEventListener('click', () => vote(-1));

    render();

    if (api) {
      (async () => {
        const remote = await fetchRemote(api, slug, ep);
        if (remote) {
          state = remote;
          saveLocal(slug, ep, state);
          render();
        }
      })();
    }
  }

  function initAll(scope: ParentNode = document): void {
    scope.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('[data-votes]').forEach(init);
  }

  /* ============================================================
     Debounced reset — cegah spam request saat user skip episode
     ============================================================ */

  let debounceTimer: number | null = null;

  function reset(root: HTMLElement, slug: string, ep: number, api: string): void {
    // 1. Reset visual langsung biar user tahu state berpindah
    const likeBtn = root.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-vote="1"]');
    const dislikeBtn = root.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-vote="-1"]');
    const likeCount = root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-count="like"]');
    const dislikeCount = root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-count="dislike"]');

    if (likeBtn) {
      likeBtn.classList.remove('is-active');
      likeBtn.setAttribute('aria-pressed', 'false');
    }
    if (dislikeBtn) {
      dislikeBtn.classList.remove('is-active');
      dislikeBtn.setAttribute('aria-pressed', 'false');
    }
    if (likeCount) likeCount.textContent = '…';
    if (dislikeCount) dislikeCount.textContent = '…';

    // 2. Update dataset (biar request baru pakai ep yang benar)
    root.dataset.slug = slug;
    root.dataset.ep = String(ep);
    root.dataset.api = api;

    // 3. Debounce fetch — cancel timer lama, jadwalkan baru
    if (debounceTimer !== null) {
      window.clearTimeout(debounceTimer);
    }

    debounceTimer = window.setTimeout(() => {
      debounceTimer = null;
      delete root.dataset.bound;
      init(root);
    }, DEBOUNCE_MS);
  }

  (window as any).__qimochiVotes = { reset, reload: reset };

  initAll();
  document.addEventListener('astro:page-load', () => initAll());
</script>
===== FILE: src/components/Featured.astro =====

import type { HydratedAnime } from '~/lib/anime';
import Icon from '~/user-components/Icons.astro';
import AnimeHero, { type HeroItem } from './AnimeHero.astro';
import { URLS } from '~/constants';

interface Props {
  items?: HydratedAnime[];
}

const { items = [] } = Astro.props;
if (items.length === 0) return null;

const AGE_LABEL: Record<string, string> = {
  G: 'G',
  PG: 'PG', 'PG-13': '13+',
  R: '17+', 'R+': '17+',
  Rx: '18+',
};

function plainExcerpt(input: string | undefined, maxLen = 180): string {
  if (!input) return '';
  const cleaned = input
    .replace(/!\[[^\]]*\]\([^)]*\)/g, '')
    .replace(/\[([^\]]+)\]\([^)]*\)/g, '$1')
    .replace(/[*_`>#]/g, '')
    .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
    .trim();
  return cleaned.length <= maxLen ? cleaned : cleaned.slice(0, maxLen).trimEnd() + '…';
}

const slides: HeroItem[] = items.map((anime) => {
  const d = anime.data;
  const epNumbers = d.episodeList.map((e) => e.number).sort((a, b) => a - b);
  const hasEpisodes = epNumbers.length > 0;
  const firstEpisode = epNumbers[0] ?? 1;

  return {
    id: anime.id,
    title: d.title,
    background: d.banner ?? d.cover ?? '',
    age: d.rating ? AGE_LABEL[d.rating] ?? d.rating : null,
    genres: (d.genre ?? []).slice(0, 5),
    excerpt: plainExcerpt(anime.body),
    watchUrl: hasEpisodes ? URLS.watch(anime.id, firstEpisode) : undefined,
    watchLabel: hasEpisodes ? `Mulai Nonton E${firstEpisode}` : 'Belum Ada Episode',
    watchDisabled: !hasEpisodes,
  };
});

const multiple = slides.length > 1;
const AUTOPLAY_MS = 7000;
---

<section

          aria-label={`Slide ${i + 1}: ${s.title}`}

          <span class="carousel__dot-fill" aria-hidden="true" />
        </button>

    </div>

<script>
  function initHero(root: HTMLElement): void {
    if (root.dataset.bound === '1') return;
    root.dataset.bound = '1';

    const track = root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-hero-track]');
    const slides = Array.from(root.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('[data-hero-slide]'));
    const dots = Array.from(root.querySelectorAll<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-hero-dot]'));
    const prev = root.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-hero-prev]');
    const next = root.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-hero-next]');

    if (!track || slides.length === 0) return;

    const total = slides.length;
    const multiple = total > 1;
    const autoplayMs = parseInt(root.dataset.autoplay ?? '7000', 10);
    const reduceMotion = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;

    let index = 0;
    let autoplayId: number | null = null;

    function setSlide(next: number): void {
      index = ((next % total) + total) % total;
      track!.style.transform = `translate3d(${-index * 100}%, 0, 0)`;

      slides.forEach((slide, i) => {
        const active = i === index;
        slide.classList.toggle('is-active', active);
        if (active) slide.removeAttribute('inert');
        else slide.setAttribute('inert', '');
      });

      dots.forEach((dot) => {
        dot.classList.remove('is-active');
        dot.setAttribute('aria-selected', 'false');
      });
      void root.offsetWidth;
      dots.forEach((dot, i) => {
        if (i === index) {
          dot.classList.add('is-active');
          dot.setAttribute('aria-selected', 'true');
        }
      });
    }

    function startAutoplay(): void {
      if (!multiple || reduceMotion) return;
      stopAutoplay();
      autoplayId = window.setInterval(() => setSlide(index + 1), autoplayMs);
    }

    function stopAutoplay(): void {
      if (autoplayId !== null) {
        window.clearInterval(autoplayId);
        autoplayId = null;
      }
    }

    function goto(next: number): void {
      setSlide(next);
      startAutoplay();
    }

    prev?.addEventListener('click', () => goto(index - 1));
    next?.addEventListener('click', () => goto(index + 1));

    dots.forEach((dot) => {
      dot.addEventListener('click', () => goto(Number(dot.dataset.heroDot ?? '0')));
    });

    root.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
      if (!multiple) return;
      if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft') {
        e.preventDefault();
        goto(index - 1);
      } else if (e.key === 'ArrowRight') {
        e.preventDefault();
        goto(index + 1);
      }
    });

    let startX = 0;
    let startY = 0;
    let tracking = false;

    root.addEventListener(
      'touchstart',
      (e) => {
        const t = e.touches[0];
        if (!t) return;
        startX = t.clientX;
        startY = t.clientY;
        tracking = true;
      },
      { passive: true }
    );

    root.addEventListener('touchend', (e) => {
      if (!tracking) return;
      tracking = false;
      const t = e.changedTouches[0];
      if (!t) return;
      const dx = t.clientX - startX;
      const dy = t.clientY - startY;
      if (Math.abs(dx) < 40 || Math.abs(dx) < Math.abs(dy)) return;
      goto(dx < 0 ? index + 1 : index - 1);
    });

    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', () => {
      if (document.hidden) stopAutoplay();
      else startAutoplay();
    });

    startAutoplay();
  }

  function initAll(scope: ParentNode = document): void {
    scope.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('[data-hero]').forEach(initHero);
  }

  initAll();
  document.addEventListener('astro:page-load', () => initAll());
</script>

<style>
  .carousel {
    position: relative;
    width: 100%;
    overflow: hidden;
    background: var(--bg-nav);
    isolation: isolate;
  }

  .carousel__track {
    display: flex;
    width: 100%;
    transition: transform 0.6s cubic-bezier(0.22, 1, 0.36, 1);
    will-change: transform;
  }

  .carousel__slide {
    flex: 0 0 100%;
    min-width: 0;
    display: flex;
  }

  .carousel__slide > :global(.hero) {
    width: 100%;
  }

  /* ==== Nav arrows ==== */
  .carousel__nav {
    position: absolute;
    top: 50%;
    transform: translateY(-50%);
    z-index: 3;
    display: inline-flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    width: 2.5rem;
    height: 3.25rem;
    padding: 0;
    background: hsla(0, 0%, 0%, 0.4);
    color: #fff;
    border: none;
    cursor: pointer;
    opacity: 0;

import { SITE } from '~/constants';
import { Font } from "astro:assets";

interface Props {
  title: string;
  description?: string;
  image?: string;
  canonical?: string;
  noindex?: boolean;
  type?: 'website' | 'article' | 'video.other';
  publishedTime?: string;
}

const {
  title,
  description = SITE.description,
  image,
  canonical,
  noindex = false,
  type = 'website',
  publishedTime,
} = Astro.props;

const canonicalURL = canonical
  ? new URL(canonical, Astro.site)
  : new URL(Astro.url.pathname, Astro.site);

const fullTitle = title === SITE.name ? title : `${title} | ${SITE.name}`;

const ogImage = image
  ? new URL(image, Astro.site)
  : SITE.ogImage
    ? new URL(SITE.ogImage, Astro.site)
    : null;

const twitterCard = ogImage ? 'summary_large_image' : 'summary';

const GA_ID = import.meta.env.PUBLIC_GA_ID;

<link rel="canonical" href={canonicalURL} />

{noindex ? (
  <meta name="robots" content="noindex, follow" />
) : (
  <meta name="robots" content="index, follow" />
)}

<meta property="og:type" content={type} />
<meta property="og:title" content={fullTitle} />
<meta property="og:description" content={description} />
<meta property="og:url" content={canonicalURL} />
{ogImage && <meta property="og:image" content={ogImage} />}
<meta property="og:site_name" content={SITE.name} />
<meta property="og:locale" content={SITE.locale} />
{publishedTime && (
  <meta property="article:published_time" content={publishedTime} />
)}

<meta name="twitter:card" content={twitterCard} />

{ogImage && <meta name="twitter:image" content={ogImage} />}

===== FILE: src/components/Header.astro =====

import { SITE, NAV_LINKS } from '~/constants';
import { Image } from 'astro:assets';
import logoImg from '~/assets/images/logo/logo.svg';
import Icon from '~/user-components/Icons.astro';
import UserMenu from '~/components/UserMenu.astro';
import MobileMenu from './MobileMenu.astro';

const currentPath = Astro.url.pathname;

function isActive(href: string): boolean {
  if (href === '/') return currentPath === '/';
  const matches = currentPath === href || currentPath.startsWith(href);
  if (!matches) return false;
  const hasMoreSpecific = NAV_LINKS.some(({ href: otherHref }) => {
    if (otherHref === href) return false;
    if (otherHref === '/') return false;
    if (otherHref.length <= href.length) return false;
    return currentPath === otherHref || currentPath.startsWith(otherHref);
  });
  return !hasMoreSpecific;
}
---

<script>
  const menu = document.querySelector<HTMLDetailsElement>('.menu');

  if (menu) {
    const mobile = window.matchMedia('(max-width: 50rem)');

    mobile.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
      if (e.matches && menu.hasAttribute('open')) {
        menu.removeAttribute('open');
      }
    });

    document.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
      if (!mobile.matches) return;
      if (!menu.hasAttribute('open')) return;
      if (!menu.contains(e.target as Node)) menu.removeAttribute('open');
    });

    menu.querySelectorAll('.nav a').forEach((a) => {
      a.addEventListener('click', () => {
        if (mobile.matches) menu.removeAttribute('open');
      });
    });

    document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape' && mobile.matches && menu.hasAttribute('open')) {
        menu.removeAttribute('open');
      }
    });
  }
</script>

===== FILE: src/components/HistoryCard.astro =====
---
import SectionLabel from '~/components/SectionLabel.astro';
import Icon from '~/user-components/Icons.astro';
---

<section class="history-section" data-history-section hidden>
  <header class="section-header">
    <SectionLabel>Lanjut Nonton</SectionLabel>
  </header>
  <ol class="history-list" data-history-list></ol>
</section>
<template id="history-item-tmpl">
  <li class="history-item">
    <a class="history-link" data-history-link>
      <span class="history-ep" data-history-ep></span>
      <span class="history-body">
        <span class="history-title" data-history-title></span>
        <span class="history-meta" data-history-meta></span>
        <span class="history-progress" data-history-progress hidden>
          <span class="history-progress-fill" data-history-progress-fill></span>
        </span>
      </span>
      <span class="history-arrow" aria-hidden="true">
        <Icon name="arrow-right" size={16} />
      </span>
    </a>
  </li>
</template>

<script>
  (function () {
    const KEY = 'qimochi:continue';
    const MAX_SHOW = 6;

    const section = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-history-section]');
    const list = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-history-list]');
    const tmpl = document.getElementById('history-item-tmpl') as HTMLTemplateElement | null;

    if (!section || !list || !tmpl) return;

    type Item = {
      slug: string;
      title: string;
      cover: string;
      episode: number;
      total: number;
      ts: number;
    };

    let items: Item[] = [];
    try {
      const raw = localStorage.getItem(KEY);
      if (raw) {
        const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
        if (Array.isArray(parsed)) {
          items = parsed.filter(
            (x): x is Item =>
              x &&
              typeof x.slug === 'string' &&
              typeof x.title === 'string' &&
              typeof x.episode === 'number'
          );
        }
      }
    } catch {}

    if (items.length === 0) return;

    items.sort((a, b) => (b.ts ?? 0) - (a.ts ?? 0));
    const visible = items.slice(0, MAX_SHOW);

    const frag = document.createDocumentFragment();

    for (const item of visible) {
      const node = tmpl.content.firstElementChild!.cloneNode(true) as HTMLElement;
      const link = node.querySelector('[data-history-link]') as HTMLAnchorElement;
      link.setAttribute('href', `/anime/watch/${item.slug}/episodes/${item.episode}/`);

      (node.querySelector('[data-history-ep]') as HTMLElement).textContent =
        String(item.episode).padStart(2, '0');

      (node.querySelector('[data-history-title]') as HTMLElement).textContent = item.title;

      const meta = node.querySelector('[data-history-meta]') as HTMLElement;
      if (item.total > 0) {
        meta.textContent = `Episode ${item.episode} dari ${item.total}`;
      } else {
        meta.textContent = `Episode ${item.episode}`;
      }

      if (item.total > 0 && item.episode > 0) {
        const progress = node.querySelector('[data-history-progress]') as HTMLElement;
        const fill = node.querySelector('[data-history-progress-fill]') as HTMLElement;
        const pct = Math.min(100, Math.round((item.episode / item.total) * 100));
        fill.style.width = `${pct}%`;
        progress.hidden = false;

        if (item.episode >= item.total) {
          fill.classList.add('is-complete');
        }
      }

      frag.appendChild(node);
    }

    list.appendChild(frag);
    section.hidden = false;
  })();
</script>

===== FILE: src/components/Pagination.astro =====

import Icon from '~/user-components/Icons.astro';

interface Props {
  current: number;
  total: number;
  base: string;
}

const { current, total, base } = Astro.props;

if (total <= 1) return null;

function urlFor(page: number): string {
  return page === 1 ? `${base}/` : `${base}/${page}/`;
}

function buildPages(): (number | '...')[] {
  if (total <= 7) {
    return Array.from({ length: total }, (_, i) => i + 1);
  }

  const pages: (number | '...')[] = [1];

  if (current > 3) pages.push('...');

  const start = Math.max(2, current - 1);
  const end = Math.min(total - 1, current + 1);

  for (let i = start; i <= end; i++) pages.push(i);

  if (current < total - 2) pages.push('...');
  pages.push(total);

  return pages;
}

const pages = buildPages();
---

<nav class="pagination" aria-label="Navigasi halaman">
  {current > 1 ? (
    <a href={urlFor(current - 1)} class="page-btn" rel="prev" aria-label="Halaman sebelumnya">
      <Icon name="arrow-left" />
    </a>
  ) : (
    <span class="page-btn disabled" aria-hidden="true">
      <Icon name="arrow-left" />
    </span>
  )}

  {pages.map(p =>
    p === '...' ? (
      <span class="page-ellipsis">…</span>
    ) : p === current ? (
      <span class="page-num active" aria-current="page">{p}</span>
    ) : (
      <a href={urlFor(p)} class="page-num">{p}</a>
    )
  )}

  {current < total ? (
    <a href={urlFor(current + 1)} class="page-btn" rel="next" aria-label="Halaman berikutnya">
      <Icon name="arrow-right" />
    </a>
  ) : (
    <span class="page-btn disabled" aria-hidden="true">
      <Icon name="arrow-right" />
    </span>
  )}
</nav>

interface Props {
  src?: string;
  title?: string;
}

const { src = '', title = '' } = Astro.props;

function encodeSrc(s: string): string {
  if (!s) return '';
  const bytes = new TextEncoder().encode(s);
  let bin = '';
  for (const b of bytes) bin += String.fromCharCode(b);
  return btoa(bin);
}

const encodedSrc = encodeSrc(src);

<script>
  interface PlayerAPI {
    load(url: string, label?: string): void;
    reload(): void;
    getCurrent(): string;
  }

  (function () {
    const wrapper = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-player]');
    const iframe = document.getElementById('player-iframe') as HTMLIFrameElement | null;
    if (!wrapper || !iframe) return;

    const loadingBox = wrapper.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-player-loading]');
    const loadingLabel = wrapper.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-loading-label]');
    const errorBox = wrapper.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-player-error]');
    const errorMsg = wrapper.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-error-msg]');
    const retryBtn = wrapper.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-player-retry]');

    if (!loadingBox || !errorBox || !errorMsg || !retryBtn) return;

    const LOAD_TIMEOUT_MS = 15000;
    let currentUrl = '';
    let currentLabel = '';
    let timeoutId: number | null = null;
    let loadId = 0;

    function showLoading(label: string): void {
      loadingLabel!.textContent = label ? ` (${label})` : '';
      loadingBox!.hidden = false;
      errorBox!.hidden = true;
      iframe!.classList.remove('is-loaded');
    }

    function showError(msg: string): void {
      loadingBox!.hidden = true;
      errorBox!.hidden = false;
      errorMsg!.textContent = msg;
      iframe!.classList.remove('is-loaded');
    }

    function showLoaded(): void {
      loadingBox!.hidden = true;
      errorBox!.hidden = true;
      iframe!.classList.add('is-loaded');
    }

    function clearTimer(): void {
      if (timeoutId !== null) {
        window.clearTimeout(timeoutId);
        timeoutId = null;
      }
    }

    function load(url: string, label = ''): void {
      if (!url) {
        showError('URL player tidak valid.');
        return;
      }

      const id = ++loadId;
      currentUrl = url;
      currentLabel = label;

      clearTimer();
      showLoading(label);

      iframe!.src = url;

      timeoutId = window.setTimeout(() => {
        if (id !== loadId) return;
        const elapsed = Math.round(LOAD_TIMEOUT_MS / 1000);
        showError(`Server tidak selesai memuat setelah ${elapsed} detik. Coba server lain.`);
      }, LOAD_TIMEOUT_MS);
    }

    function reload(): void {
      if (currentUrl) load(currentUrl, currentLabel);
    }

    iframe.addEventListener('load', () => {
      clearTimer();
      if (!errorBox!.hidden) return;
      showLoaded();
    });

    iframe.addEventListener('error', () => {
      clearTimer();
      showError('Gagal memuat player. Coba server lain atau klik Coba Lagi.');
    });

    retryBtn.addEventListener('click', reload);

    (window as any).__qimochiPlayer = {
      load,
      reload,
      getCurrent: () => currentUrl,
    } satisfies PlayerAPI;

    const enc = iframe.dataset.enc;
    if (enc) {
      try {
        const bin = atob(enc);
        const bytes = new Uint8Array(bin.length);
        for (let i = 0; i < bin.length; i++) bytes[i] = bin.charCodeAt(i);
        const realSrc = new TextDecoder().decode(bytes);
        if (realSrc) load(realSrc, '');
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('[Player] decode failed:', err);
        showError('URL player tidak valid.');
      }
    }
  })();
</script>

  id={dialogId}
  data-report-dialog
  aria-labelledby={`${dialogId}-title`}
>
  <form class="report-dialog__inner" data-report-form>
    <header class="report-dialog__header">
      <h3 class="report-dialog__title" id={`${dialogId}-title`}>
        <span data-report-slug></span>
        <span class="report-dialog__sep">·</span>
        <span>Episode <span data-report-episode></span></span>
      </h3>
      <button
        type="button"
        class="report-dialog__close"
        data-report-close
        aria-label="Tutup"
      >
        <Icon name="close" size={16} />
      </button>
    </header>

    <div class="report-dialog__body">
      <fieldset class="report-dialog__reasons">
        <legend class="report-dialog__legend">Pilih masalah</legend>

        <label class="report-reason">
          <input type="radio" name="reason" value="embed_dead" required />

        ></textarea>

    </div>

      <button type="button" class="report-dialog__btn" data-report-cancel>
        Batal
      </button>
      <button
        type="submit"
        class="report-dialog__btn report-dialog__btn--primary"
      >
        Kirim
      </button>
    </footer>
  </form>
</dialog>

<script>
  (function () {
    interface AuthUser {
      sub: string;
      name: string;
      email: string;
      picture: string | null;
    }

    function getUser(): AuthUser | null {
      try {
        const menu = (window as any).__qimochiUserMenu;
        if (menu?.getUser) return menu.getUser();
      } catch {}
      try {
        const raw = localStorage.getItem('qimochi:user-cache');
        if (!raw) return null;
        return JSON.parse(raw);
      } catch {
        return null;
      }
    }

    function openUserMenu(): void {
      const menu = (window as any).__qimochiUserMenu;
      if (menu?.open) menu.open();
    }

    function initDialog(btn: HTMLButtonElement): void {
      if (btn.dataset.bound === '1') return;
      btn.dataset.bound = '1';

      const dialogId = btn.dataset.dialogId ?? '';
      if (!dialogId) return;

      const dialog = document.getElementById(dialogId) as HTMLDialogElement | null;
      if (!dialog) return;

      const form = dialog.querySelector<HTMLFormElement>('[data-report-form]');
      const closeBtn = dialog.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-report-close]');
      const cancelBtn = dialog.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-report-cancel]');
      const statusEl = dialog.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-report-status]');
      const slugEl = dialog.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-report-slug]');
      const episodeEl = dialog.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-report-episode]');

      const slug = btn.dataset.slug ?? '';
      const episode = btn.dataset.episode ?? '';
      const server = btn.dataset.server ?? '';
      const quality = btn.dataset.quality ?? '';
      const api = (btn.dataset.api ?? '').replace(/\/$/, '');

      if (slugEl) slugEl.textContent = slug.replace(/-/g, ' ');
      if (episodeEl) episodeEl.textContent = episode;

      function openDialog(): void {
        if (!getUser()) {
          openUserMenu();
          return;
        }
        dialog.showModal();
        document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
      }

      function closeDialog(): void {
        if (dialog.open) dialog.close();
      }

      dialog.addEventListener('close', () => {
        document.body.style.overflow = '';
        if (statusEl) {
          statusEl.hidden = true;
          statusEl.classList.remove('is-error', 'is-success');
          statusEl.textContent = '';
        }
        form?.reset();
      });

      btn.addEventListener('click', openDialog);
      closeBtn?.addEventListener('click', closeDialog);
      cancelBtn?.addEventListener('click', closeDialog);

      dialog.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
        if (e.target === dialog) closeDialog();
      });

      form?.addEventListener('submit', async (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (statusEl) {
          statusEl.hidden = true;
          statusEl.classList.remove('is-error', 'is-success');
        }

        const submitBtn = form.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>(
          'button[type="submit"]'
        );
        if (submitBtn) submitBtn.disabled = true;

        const fd = new FormData(form);
        const reason = String(fd.get('reason') ?? '');
        const note = String(fd.get('note') ?? '').trim();

        try {
          const res = await fetch(`${api}/api/v1/reports`, {
            method: 'POST',
            credentials: 'include',
            headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
            body: JSON.stringify({
              slug,
              episode: Number(episode),
              server,
              quality,
              reason,
              note,
            }),
          });

          const json = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));

          if (res.status === 401) {
            closeDialog();
            openUserMenu();
            return;
          }

          if (res.status === 429) {
            if (statusEl) {
              statusEl.textContent =
                json?.error?.message ?? 'Terlalu banyak laporan. Coba lagi nanti.';
              statusEl.classList.add('is-error');
              statusEl.hidden = false;
            }
            return;
          }

          if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}`);

          if (statusEl) {
            statusEl.textContent = 'Laporan terkirim. Terima kasih!';
            statusEl.classList.add('is-success');
            statusEl.hidden = false;
          }

          window.setTimeout(() => closeDialog(), 1500);
        } catch (err) {
          console.error('[report] submit failed', err);
          if (statusEl) {
            statusEl.textContent = 'Gagal mengirim. Coba lagi.';
            statusEl.classList.add('is-error');
            statusEl.hidden = false;
          }
        } finally {
          if (submitBtn) submitBtn.disabled = false;
        }
      });
    }

    function initAll(scope: ParentNode = document): void {
      scope
        .querySelectorAll<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-report-btn]')
        .forEach(initDialog);
    }

    initAll();
    document.addEventListener('astro:page-load', () => initAll());
  })();
</script>
