import Icon from '~/user-components/Icons.astro';

interface Props {
  title: string;
  url?: string;
  class?: string;
}

const { title, url, class: className } = Astro.props;

const shareUrl = url ?? Astro.url.href;
---

<div class:list={['share-wrap', className]}>
  <button
    type="button"
    class="icon-btn"
    data-share-btn
    data-title={title}
    data-url={shareUrl}
    aria-label="Bagikan"
    title="Bagikan"
  >
    <Icon name="share" size={20} />
  </button>

  <span
    class="share-toast"
    data-share-toast
    role="status"
    aria-live="polite"
    hidden
  >
    Link disalin
  </span>
</div>

<script>
  (function () {
    function init(btn: HTMLButtonElement): void {
      if (btn.dataset.bound === '1') return;
      btn.dataset.bound = '1';

      const wrap = btn.closest<HTMLElement>('.share-wrap');
      const toast = wrap?.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-share-toast]') ?? null;
      let toastTimer: number | null = null;

      function showToast(): void {
        if (!toast) return;
        toast.hidden = false;
        if (toastTimer !== null) window.clearTimeout(toastTimer);
        toastTimer = window.setTimeout(() => {
          toast.hidden = true;
          toastTimer = null;
        }, 2000);
      }

      btn.addEventListener('click', async () => {
        const t = btn.dataset.title ?? document.title;
        const u = btn.dataset.url ?? location.href;

        if (typeof navigator.share === 'function') {
          try {
            await navigator.share({ title: t, url: u });
            return;
          } catch {}
        }

        try {
          await navigator.clipboard.writeText(u);
          showToast();
        } catch (err) {
          console.error('[share] copy failed', err);
        }
      });
    }

    document
      .querySelectorAll<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-share-btn]')
      .forEach(init);
  })();
</script>

<style>
    .skip-link {
        position: absolute;
        top: -3rem;
        left: var(--pad-x);
        z-index: var(--z-toast);
        padding: var(--s-2) var(--s-4);

import Icon from '~/user-components/Icons.astro';

interface Server { name: string; url: string; }
interface Stream { quality: string; servers: Server[]; }

interface Props { streams?: Stream[]; }

const { streams = [] } = Astro.props;
const defaultStream = streams[0];

function encodeUrl(s: string): string {
  if (!s) return '';
  const bytes = new TextEncoder().encode(s);
  let bin = '';
  for (const b of bytes) bin += String.fromCharCode(b);
  return btoa(bin);
}

function formatCurrent(): string {
  if (!defaultStream) return '—';
  return `${defaultStream.quality} · Server 1`;
}

<script>
  interface Server { name: string; url: string; }
  interface Stream { quality: string; servers: Server[]; }

  function encodeUrl(s: string): string {
    if (!s) return '';
    const bytes = new TextEncoder().encode(s);
    let bin = '';
    for (const b of bytes) bin += String.fromCharCode(b);
    return btoa(bin);
  }

  function escapeAttr(s: string): string {
    return s.replace(/&/g, '&amp;').replace(/"/g, '&quot;').replace(/</g, '&lt;');
  }

  function escapeHtml(s: string): string {
    return s.replace(/&/g, '&amp;').replace(/</g, '&lt;').replace(/>/g, '&gt;');
  }

  function buildHtml(streams: Stream[]): string {
    if (!streams || streams.length === 0) {
      return '<div class="stream-empty">Belum ada server untuk episode ini.</div>';
    }

    return streams
      .map((s, i) => {
        const chips = s.servers
          .map((srv, j) => {
            const id = `s-${i}-${j}`;
            const enc = escapeAttr(encodeUrl(srv.url));
            const quality = escapeAttr(s.quality);
            const server = `Server ${j + 1}`;
            const checked = i === 0 && j === 0 ? 'checked' : '';
            return `<input type="radio" name="stream" id="${id}" data-enc="${enc}" data-quality="${quality}" data-server="${server}" ${checked} hidden /><label for="${id}" class="chip">${server}</label>`;
          })
          .join('');
        return `<div class="quality-group"><span class="quality-label">${escapeHtml(s.quality)}</span><div class="chips">${chips}</div></div>`;
      })
      .join('');
  }

  function formatCurrentLabel(streams: Stream[], quality?: string, serverNum?: number): string {
    const first = streams?.[0];
    if (!first) return '—';
    const q = quality ?? first.quality;
    const s = serverNum ?? 1;
    return `${q} · Server ${s}`;
  }

  function mount(root: HTMLElement, streams: Stream[]): void {
    const details = root.querySelector<HTMLDetailsElement>('[data-stream-picker]');
    const body = root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-stream-body]');
    const current = root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-current]');
    if (!details || !body || !current) return;

    current.textContent = formatCurrentLabel(streams ?? []);
    body.innerHTML = buildHtml(streams ?? []);

    const firstRadio = body.querySelector<HTMLInputElement>('input[name="stream"]');
    if (firstRadio) firstRadio.checked = true;

    // Default closed
    details.open = false;
  }

  document.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
    const target = e.target as HTMLInputElement | null;
    if (!target || target.name !== 'stream' || !target.checked) return;

    const enc = target.dataset.enc;
    if (!enc) return;

    let real = '';
    try {
      const bin = atob(enc);
      const bytes = new Uint8Array(bin.length);
      for (let i = 0; i < bin.length; i++) bytes[i] = bin.charCodeAt(i);
      real = new TextDecoder().decode(bytes);
    } catch { return; }

    const quality = target.dataset.quality ?? '';
    const serverLabel = target.dataset.server ?? '';
    const playerLabel = quality && serverLabel ? `${quality} · ${serverLabel}` : '';

    const player = (window as any).__qimochiPlayer;
    if (player?.load) {
      player.load(real, playerLabel);
    } else {
      const iframe = document.getElementById('player-iframe') as HTMLIFrameElement | null;
      if (iframe) iframe.src = real;
    }

    const root = target.closest('[data-stream-selector]');
    const current = root?.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-current]');
    if (current) {
      current.textContent = `${quality} · ${serverLabel}`;
    }
  });

  (window as any).__qimochiStream = { mount };
</script>

<script>
  interface AuthUser {
    sub: string;
    name: string;
    email: string;
    picture: string | null;
  }

  (function () {
    const CACHE_KEY = 'qimochi:user-cache';

    const rootEl = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-user-menu]');
    if (!rootEl) return;
    const root: HTMLElement = rootEl;

    const CLIENT_ID = root.dataset.clientid || '';
    const API = (root.dataset.api || '').replace(/\/$/, '');
    const DIALOG_ID = root.dataset.dialogId || '';

    const dialogEl = DIALOG_ID
      ? (document.getElementById(DIALOG_ID) as HTMLDialogElement | null)
      : null;
    if (!dialogEl) return;
    const dialog: HTMLDialogElement = dialogEl;

    const triggerEl = root.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-trigger]');
    const guestIconEl = root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-guest]');
    const triggerAvatarEl = root.querySelector<HTMLImageElement>('[data-avatar]');

    const dialogCloseEl = dialog.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-dialog-close]');
    const dialogTitleEl = dialog.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-dialog-title]');
    const guestViewEl = dialog.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-guest-view]');
    const gsiSlotEl = dialog.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-gsi]');
    const profileBoxEl = dialog.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-profile]');
    const infoAvatarEl = dialog.querySelector<HTMLImageElement>('[data-info-avatar]');
    const infoNameEl = dialog.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-info-name]');
    const infoEmailEl = dialog.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-info-email]');
    const logoutBtnEl = dialog.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-logout]');

    if (
      !triggerEl || !guestIconEl || !triggerAvatarEl ||
      !dialogCloseEl || !dialogTitleEl || !guestViewEl || !gsiSlotEl ||
      !profileBoxEl || !infoAvatarEl || !infoNameEl || !infoEmailEl || !logoutBtnEl
    ) {
      return;
    }

    const trigger: HTMLButtonElement = triggerEl;
    const guestIcon: HTMLElement = guestIconEl;
    const triggerAvatar: HTMLImageElement = triggerAvatarEl;
    const dialogClose: HTMLButtonElement = dialogCloseEl;
    const dialogTitle: HTMLElement = dialogTitleEl;
    const guestView: HTMLElement = guestViewEl;
    const gsiSlot: HTMLElement = gsiSlotEl;
    const profileBox: HTMLElement = profileBoxEl;
    const infoAvatar: HTMLImageElement = infoAvatarEl;
    const infoName: HTMLElement = infoNameEl;
    const infoEmail: HTMLElement = infoEmailEl;
    const logoutBtn: HTMLButtonElement = logoutBtnEl;

    let currentUser: AuthUser | null = null;
    let gsiRendered = false;

    function readCache(): AuthUser | null {
      try {
        const raw = localStorage.getItem(CACHE_KEY);
        if (!raw) return null;
        const parsed = JSON.parse(raw) as Partial<AuthUser> | null;
        if (parsed && typeof parsed.sub === 'string' && typeof parsed.name === 'string') {
          return {
            sub: parsed.sub,
            name: parsed.name,
            email: typeof parsed.email === 'string' ? parsed.email : '',
            picture: typeof parsed.picture === 'string' ? parsed.picture : null,
          };
        }
      } catch {}
      return null;
    }

    function writeCache(user: AuthUser | null): void {
      try {
        if (user) localStorage.setItem(CACHE_KEY, JSON.stringify(user));
        else localStorage.removeItem(CACHE_KEY);
      } catch {}
    }

    function loadScript(src: string): Promise<void> {
      return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        const existing = document.querySelector<HTMLScriptElement>(`script[src="${src}"]`);
        if (existing) {
          if (existing.dataset.loaded === '1') return resolve();
          existing.addEventListener('load', () => resolve(), { once: true });
          existing.addEventListener('error', () => reject(), { once: true });
          return;
        }
        const s = document.createElement('script');
        s.src = src;
        s.async = true;
        s.defer = true;
        s.addEventListener('load', () => { s.dataset.loaded = '1'; resolve(); }, { once: true });
        s.addEventListener('error', () => reject(), { once: true });
        document.head.appendChild(s);
      });
    }

    function dispatchAuthChanged(): void {
      document.dispatchEvent(
        new CustomEvent('qimochi:auth-changed', { detail: { user: currentUser } })
      );
    }

    function applyUser(): void {
      if (currentUser) {
        guestIcon.hidden = true;
        triggerAvatar.hidden = false;
        if (currentUser.picture) {
          triggerAvatar.src = currentUser.picture;
          triggerAvatar.alt = currentUser.name;
        } else {
          triggerAvatar.removeAttribute('src');
        }

        dialogTitle.textContent = 'Akun Saya';
        guestView.hidden = true;
        profileBox.hidden = false;

        if (currentUser.picture) {
          infoAvatar.src = currentUser.picture;
        } else {
          infoAvatar.removeAttribute('src');
        }
        infoAvatar.alt = currentUser.name;
        infoName.textContent = currentUser.name;
        infoEmail.textContent = currentUser.email;
      } else {
        guestIcon.hidden = false;
        triggerAvatar.hidden = true;
        triggerAvatar.removeAttribute('src');

        dialogTitle.textContent = 'Masuk';
        guestView.hidden = false;
        profileBox.hidden = true;
        ensureGsi();
      }
    }

    function ensureGsi(): void {
      if (gsiRendered) return;
      if (!CLIENT_ID) return;
      const g = (window as any).google;
      if (!g?.accounts?.id) return;

      try {
        gsiSlot.innerHTML = '';
        g.accounts.id.renderButton(gsiSlot, {
          theme: 'outline',
          size: 'large',
          text: 'signin_with',
          shape: 'rect',
          logo_alignment: 'left',
          width: 280,
        });
        gsiRendered = true;
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('[usermenu] render gsi failed', err);
      }
    }

    function lockBodyScroll(): void {
      document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
    }

    function unlockBodyScroll(): void {
      document.body.style.overflow = '';
    }

    function openDialog(): void {
      if (dialog.open) return;
      dialog.showModal();
      lockBodyScroll();
      if (!currentUser) ensureGsi();
    }

    function closeDialog(): void {
      if (dialog.open) dialog.close();
    }

    dialog.addEventListener('close', unlockBodyScroll);
    dialog.addEventListener('cancel', unlockBodyScroll);

    trigger.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
      e.stopPropagation();
      openDialog();
    });

    dialogClose.addEventListener('click', closeDialog);

    dialog.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
      if (e.target === dialog) closeDialog();
    });

    async function handleCredential(response: { credential: string }): Promise<void> {
      try {
        const res = await fetch(`${API}/api/v1/auth/google`, {
          method: 'POST',
          credentials: 'include',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify({ credential: response.credential }),
        });
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}`);
        const json = (await res.json()) as { ok: boolean; user: AuthUser };
        if (json.ok && json.user) {
          currentUser = json.user;
          writeCache(json.user);
          applyUser();
          closeDialog();
          dispatchAuthChanged();
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('[usermenu] login failed', err);
      }
    }

    logoutBtn.addEventListener('click', async () => {
      try {
        await fetch(`${API}/api/v1/auth/logout`, {
          method: 'POST',
          credentials: 'include',
        });
      } catch {}
      currentUser = null;
      gsiRendered = false;
      writeCache(null);
      applyUser();
      closeDialog();
      dispatchAuthChanged();
    });

    async function fetchMe(): Promise<void> {
      try {
        const res = await fetch(`${API}/api/v1/auth/me`, { credentials: 'include' });
        if (!res.ok) return;
        const json = (await res.json()) as { ok: boolean; user: AuthUser | null };
        const user = json.ok && json.user ? json.user : null;
        const same = (user?.sub ?? null) === (currentUser?.sub ?? null);
        if (same) return;
        currentUser = user;
        writeCache(user);
        applyUser();
        dispatchAuthChanged();
      } catch {
        // biarkan state dari cache
      }
    }

    function onExternalChange(e: Event): void {
      const detail = (e as CustomEvent<{ user: AuthUser | null }>).detail;
      if (!detail) return;
      const sameUser = (detail.user?.sub ?? null) === (currentUser?.sub ?? null);
      if (sameUser) return;
      currentUser = detail.user;
      writeCache(detail.user);
      applyUser();
    }

    document.addEventListener('qimochi:auth-changed', onExternalChange);

    (function bootstrap(): void {
      currentUser = readCache();
      applyUser();
      if (currentUser) dispatchAuthChanged();
    })();

    (async () => {
      if (CLIENT_ID) {
        try {
          await loadScript('https://accounts.google.com/gsi/client');
          const g = (window as any).google;
          if (g?.accounts?.id) {
            g.accounts.id.initialize({
              client_id: CLIENT_ID,
              callback: handleCredential,
              auto_select: false,
              cancel_on_tap_outside: true,
            });
            document.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('qimochi:gsi-ready'));
          }
        } catch (err) {
          console.error('[usermenu] load gsi failed', err);
        }
      }

      await fetchMe();
    })();

    (window as any).__qimochiUserMenu = {
      getUser: () => currentUser,
      open: () => openDialog(),
      close: () => closeDialog(),
    };
  })();
</script>

  type="button"
  class:list={['icon-btn', className]}
  data-daftarku-btn
  data-anime={animeId}
  aria-label="Tambah ke daftarku"
  title="Tambah ke daftarku"
>
  <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="20" height="20" fill="none"
    stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.4" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"
    aria-hidden="true">
    <path d="M12 5v14M5 12h14"/>
  </svg>
</button>

<script>
  interface AuthUser {
    sub: string;
    name: string;
    email: string;
    picture: string | null;
  }

  (function () {
    function readCache(): AuthUser | null {
      try {
        const raw = localStorage.getItem('qimochi:user-cache');
        if (!raw) return null;
        const parsed = JSON.parse(raw) as Partial<AuthUser> | null;
        if (parsed && typeof parsed.sub === 'string' && typeof parsed.name === 'string') {
          return {
            sub: parsed.sub,
            name: parsed.name,
            email: typeof parsed.email === 'string' ? parsed.email : '',
            picture: typeof parsed.picture === 'string' ? parsed.picture : null,
          };
        }
      } catch {}
      return null;
    }

    function init(btn: HTMLButtonElement): void {
      if (btn.dataset.bound === '1') return;
      btn.dataset.bound = '1';

      let currentUser: AuthUser | null = readCache();

      function onAuthChanged(e: Event): void {
        const detail = (e as CustomEvent<{ user: AuthUser | null }>).detail;
        if (!detail) return;
        currentUser = detail.user;
      }
      document.addEventListener('qimochi:auth-changed', onAuthChanged);

      btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        if (!currentUser) {
          const menu = (window as any).__qimochiUserMenu;
          if (menu?.open) menu.open();
          return;
        }
        // TODO: fitur daftarku (user list) — belum diimplementasi.
        console.info('[daftarku] TODO: implement user list untuk', btn.dataset.anime);
      });
    }

    document
      .querySelectorAll<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-daftarku-btn]')
      .forEach(init);
  })();
</script>

// src/content.config.ts
import { defineCollection } from 'astro:content';
import { z } from 'astro/zod';
import { glob, file } from 'astro/loaders';

const Slug = z
  .string()
  .regex(/^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$/, 'Harus lowercase-kebab-case');

const ExternalUrl = z.url();

const AnimeType = z.enum([
  'TV', 'Movie', 'OVA', 'ONA', 'Special', 'Music', 'Unknown',
]);

const AnimeStatus = z.enum([
  'airing', 'finished', 'upcoming', 'hiatus', 'cancelled',
]);

const AnimeSource = z.enum([
  'original', 'manga', 'light_novel', 'visual_novel', 'game',
  'web_manga', 'web_novel', 'novel', 'book', 'picture_book',
  'radio', 'music', '4_koma_manga', 'card_game', 'other',
]);

const AnimeSeason = z.enum(['winter', 'spring', 'summer', 'fall']);
const AnimeRating = z.enum(['G', 'PG', 'PG-13', 'R', 'R+', 'Rx']);

const anime = defineCollection({
  loader: glob({
    pattern: ['**/*.md', '!**/_*.md'],
    base: './src/content/anime',
    deferRender: true,
  }),
  schema: z.object({
    title: z.string().min(1),
    titleEnglish: z.string().optional(),
    titleNative: z.string().optional(),
    malId: z.number().int().positive().optional(),
    anilistId: z.number().int().positive().optional(),
    kitsuId: z.string().optional(),
    type: AnimeType,
    status: AnimeStatus,
    source: AnimeSource.optional(),
    season: AnimeSeason.optional(),
    year: z.number().int().min(1900).max(2100).optional(),
    episodes: z.number().int().positive().nullable().optional(),
    duration: z.number().int().positive().optional(),
    rating: AnimeRating.optional(),
    aired: z.object({
      from: z.coerce.date().optional(),
      to: z.coerce.date().nullable().optional(),
    }).optional(),
    stats: z.object({
      score: z.number().min(0).max(10).optional(),
      scoredBy: z.number().int().nonnegative().optional(),
    }).optional(),
    genres: z.array(Slug).default([]),
    studios: z.array(Slug).default([]),
    image: ExternalUrl.optional(),
    banner: ExternalUrl.optional(),
    trailer: z.string().optional(),
    draft: z.boolean().default(false),
    addedAt: z.coerce.date().optional(),
    updatedAt: z.coerce.date().optional(),
  }),
});

const genres = defineCollection({
  loader: file('./src/data/genres.json'),
  schema: z.object({
    id: Slug,
    name: z.string(),
    description: z.string().optional(),
    category: z.enum(['genre', 'theme', 'demographic']).default('genre'),
  }),
});

const studios = defineCollection({
  loader: file('./src/data/studios.json'),
  schema: z.object({
    id: Slug,
    name: z.string(),
    nameNative: z.string().optional(),
    founded: z.number().int().min(1900).max(2100).optional(),
    website: ExternalUrl.optional(),
    description: z.string().optional(),
  }),
});

const blog = defineCollection({
  loader: glob({ pattern: '**/*.md', base: './src/content/blog' }),
  schema: z.object({
    title: z.string().max(120),
    description: z.string().max(200),
    cover: z.string().optional(),
    date: z.coerce.date(),
    updatedAt: z.coerce.date().optional(),
    author: z.string().default('Admin'),
    category: z.enum(['News', 'Review', 'List', 'Guide', 'Update']),
    tags: z.array(z.string()).default([]),
    draft: z.boolean().default(false),
  }),
});

export const collections = { anime, genres, studios, blog };

===== FILE: src/content/blog/selamat-datang-di-qimochi.md =====
---
title: "Selamat Datang di Qimochi — Nonton Anime Sub Indo, Ringan & Cepat"
description: "Perkenalan lengkap tentang Qimochi — fitur, cara pakai, dan komitmen kami untuk pengalaman nonton anime yang ringan dan cepat."

Selamat datang di **Qimochi** — tempat nonton anime subtitle Indonesia
dengan pengalaman yang ringan, cepat, dan tanpa ribet.

## Apa Itu Qimochi?

Qimochi adalah koleksi anime yang bisa kamu tonton langsung atau download
dalam berbagai kualitas. Kami fokus pada tiga hal utama:

### 1. Kecepatan

Situs dibangun dengan teknologi statis modern. Setiap halaman dimuat dalam
waktu kurang dari 1 detik, bahkan di koneksi lambat.

### 2. Kemudahan

Navigasi simpel — tanpa popup, tanpa iklan mengganggu, tanpa redirect aneh.
Kamu datang untuk nonton, kami pastikan itu yang kamu dapat.

### 3. Kelengkapan

Koleksi anime terus bertambah. Dari judul klasik sampai musim terbaru,
semuanya ada di satu tempat.

## Fitur Utama

- **Streaming langsung** — tonton tanpa download
- **Download** — pilih kualitas, unduh per episode
- **Multi-kualitas** — 360p, 480p, 720p, 1080p
- **Multi-server** — ganti server kalau yang satu lag
- **Pencarian canggih** — cari dengan typo tetap ketemu
- **Archive lengkap** — jelajahi berdasarkan abjad
- **Genre** — temukan sesuai selera
- **Daftar Saya** — simpan anime favorit (login Google)
- **Riwayat tonton** — lanjut dari episode terakhir
- **Rating komunitas** — beri nilai dan lihat rekomendasi
- **Komentar & reaksi** — diskusi bareng penggemar lain

## Cara Menggunakan

### 1. Jelajahi Anime

Buka halaman [Anime](/anime/) untuk daftar lengkap, atau langsung ke:

- [Ongoing](/anime/ongoing/) — anime yang sedang tayang
- [Completed](/anime/completed/) — anime yang sudah tamat
- [Genre](/anime/genre/) — jelajahi berdasarkan genre

### 2. Cari Anime

Halaman [Cari](/search/) mendukung pencarian dengan toleransi typo.
Ketik judul dengan salah huruf pun tetap ketemu.

### 3. Pilih Episode

Buka halaman detail anime untuk daftar episode lengkap. Klik episode
yang mau ditonton.

### 4. Pilih Server & Kualitas

Di halaman tonton, kamu bisa memilih:

- **Kualitas** — dari 360p (hemat kuota) sampai 1080p (kualitas terbaik)
- **Server** — ganti-ganti kalau yang satu bermasalah

Pilihanmu akan **diingat otomatis** untuk kunjungan berikutnya.

### 5. Simpan & Beri Rating

**Login dengan Google** untuk:

- **Bookmark** anime favoritmu
- **Catat riwayat tonton** — lanjut nonton dari episode terakhir
- **Beri rating** — bantu pengguna lain temukan anime bagus
- **Komentar** — diskusi bareng komunitas

## Yang Perlu Diketahui

Kami **tidak menyimpan file video** di server kami. Semua konten di-embed
dari layanan pihak ketiga yang tersedia bebas di internet. Kami hanya
menyediakan tautan untuk keperluan referensi.

Untuk informasi lebih lanjut, lihat halaman [DMCA](/dmca/) dan
[Disclaimer](/disclaimer/).

## Tetap Update

Jangan lewatkan anime baru:

- **RSS Feed** — subscribe di [RSS](/rss.xml) untuk update artikel baru
- **Blog** — pantau [Blog](/blog/) untuk review dan berita

## Apa Selanjutnya?

Kami terus mengembangkan Qimochi. Beberapa fitur yang sedang dalam
perencanaan:

- Notifikasi episode baru

===== FILE: src/lib/anime.ts =====
import { getCollection, getEntry, type CollectionEntry } from 'astro:content';
import { STATUS_LABEL, TYPE_LABEL, type RawStatus, type RawType } from '~/constants';

export interface EpisodeServer { name: string; url: string; }
export interface EpisodeStream { quality: string; servers: EpisodeServer[]; }
export interface EpisodeDownload { quality: string; size: string; servers: EpisodeServer[]; }
export interface EpisodeData {
  number: number;
  title?: string;
  duration?: number;
  aired?: string;
  streams: EpisodeStream[];
  downloads?: EpisodeDownload[];
}
export interface Franchise {
  relation: string;
  slug: string;
  title?: string;
}

export type AnimeEntry = CollectionEntry<'anime'>;

export type QimochiStatus = 'Ongoing' | 'Completed' | 'Hiatus' | 'Upcoming';
export type QimochiType = 'TV' | 'Movie' | 'OVA' | 'ONA' | 'Special';

export type HydratedAnime = Omit<AnimeEntry, 'data'> & {
  data: Omit<AnimeEntry['data'], 'status' | 'type'> & {
    status: QimochiStatus;
    type: QimochiType;
    cover: string;
    studio: string;
    genre: string[];
    releaseDate: Date | null;
    score: number | null;
    episodeList: EpisodeData[];
    franchises: Franchise[];
  };
};

interface ChunkRef { slug: string; start: number; end: number; path: string; }

const HIDDEN_RELATIONS = new Set([
  'character', 'adaptation', 'contains', 'other',
]);

interface RawEpisodeStreamObject {
  [quality: string]: string[];
}

interface RawEpisodeDownloadObject {
  [quality: string]: { size?: string; urls?: string[] };
}

interface RawEpisodeData {
  number: number;
  title?: string | null;
  aired?: string | null;
  duration?: number | null;
  streams?: RawEpisodeStreamObject | EpisodeStream[];
  downloads?: RawEpisodeDownloadObject | EpisodeDownload[];
}

function normalizeStreams(raw: RawEpisodeData['streams']): EpisodeStream[] {
  if (!raw) return [];
  if (Array.isArray(raw)) return raw as EpisodeStream[];

  return Object.entries(raw).map(([quality, urls]) => ({
    quality,
    servers: (Array.isArray(urls) ? urls : []).map((url, i) => ({
      name: `Server ${i + 1}`,
      url,
    })),
  }));
}

function normalizeDownloads(
  raw: RawEpisodeData['downloads']
): EpisodeDownload[] | undefined {
  if (!raw) return undefined;

  if (Array.isArray(raw)) return raw as EpisodeDownload[];

  const result: EpisodeDownload[] = [];
  for (const [quality, val] of Object.entries(raw)) {
    if (!val || !Array.isArray(val.urls) || val.urls.length === 0) continue;
    result.push({
      quality,
      size: val.size ?? '',
      servers: val.urls.map((url, i) => ({
        name: `Server ${i + 1}`,
        url,
      })),
    });
  }
  return result.length > 0 ? result : undefined;
}

function sanitizeStreams(streams: EpisodeStream[]): EpisodeStream[] {
  return streams
    .map((s) => ({
      ...s,
      servers: s.servers.filter(
        (srv) => typeof srv.url === 'string' && srv.url.trim() !== ''
      ),
    }))
    .filter((s) => s.servers.length > 0);
}

const episodeModules = import.meta.glob<{ default: RawEpisodeData[] }>(
  '../data/anime/*/episodes/*.json',
  { eager: true }
);

const franchiseModules = import.meta.glob<{ default: Franchise[] }>(
  '../data/anime/*/franchises.json',
  { eager: true }
);

const chunksBySlug: Record<string, ChunkRef[]> = {};
for (const [path, mod] of Object.entries(episodeModules)) {
  const match = path.match(/\/data\/anime\/([^/]+)\/episodes\/(\d+)-(\d+)\.json$/);
  if (!match) continue;
  const slug = match[1];
  const start = parseInt(match[2] ?? '0', 10);
  const end = parseInt(match[3] ?? '0', 10);
  if (!slug || isNaN(start) || isNaN(end)) continue;
  if (!Array.isArray(mod.default)) continue;
  (chunksBySlug[slug] ??= []).push({ slug, start, end, path });
}

const episodesBySlug: Record<string, EpisodeData[]> = {};
for (const [slug, chunks] of Object.entries(chunksBySlug)) {
  chunks.sort((a, b) => a.start - b.start);
  const merged: EpisodeData[] = [];

  for (const chunk of chunks) {
    const mod = episodeModules[chunk.path];
    if (!mod || !Array.isArray(mod.default)) continue;

    for (const raw of mod.default) {
      if (!raw || typeof raw.number !== 'number') continue;

      const streams = sanitizeStreams(normalizeStreams(raw.streams));
      if (streams.length === 0) continue;

      const trimmedTitle = raw.title?.trim();
      const trimmedAired = raw.aired?.trim();

      merged.push({
        number: raw.number,
        title: trimmedTitle ? trimmedTitle : undefined,
        duration:
          typeof raw.duration === 'number' && raw.duration > 0
            ? raw.duration
            : undefined,
        aired: trimmedAired ? trimmedAired : undefined,
        streams,
        downloads: normalizeDownloads(raw.downloads),
      });
    }
  }

  merged.sort((a, b) => a.number - b.number);
  episodesBySlug[slug] = merged;
}

const franchisesBySlug: Record<string, Franchise[]> = {};
for (const [path, mod] of Object.entries(franchiseModules)) {
  const match = path.match(/\/data\/anime\/([^/]+)\/franchises\.json$/);
  if (!match) continue;
  const slug = match[1];
  if (!slug) continue;
  const raw = Array.isArray(mod.default) ? mod.default : [];
  franchisesBySlug[slug] = raw.filter(
    (f) => f && typeof f.slug === 'string' && !HIDDEN_RELATIONS.has(f.relation)
  );
}

function toDisplayStatus(s: RawStatus): QimochiStatus {
  return STATUS_LABEL[s] as QimochiStatus;
}

function toDisplayType(t: RawType): QimochiType {
  return TYPE_LABEL[t] as QimochiType;
}

function titleCase(s: string): string {
  return s
    .split(/[-_\s]+/)
    .filter(Boolean)
    .map((w) => w.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + w.slice(1).toLowerCase())
    .join(' ');
}

function resolveReleaseDate(
  aired: { from?: Date } | undefined,
  year: number | undefined
): Date | null {
  if (aired?.from instanceof Date) return aired.from;
  if (year) return new Date(Date.UTC(year, 0, 1));
  return null;
}

function resolveStudio(studios: string[] | undefined): string {
  const first = studios?.[0];
  if (!first) return 'Unknown';
  return titleCase(first);
}

function resolveGenre(genres: string[] | undefined): string[] {
  if (!genres || genres.length === 0) return ['Unknown'];
  return genres.map(titleCase);
}

function resolveScore(stats: { score?: number } | undefined): number | null {
  const s = stats?.score;
  if (typeof s === 'number' && s >= 0) return s;
  return null;
}

async function hydrateOne(anime: AnimeEntry): Promise<HydratedAnime> {
  const slug = anime.id;
  const d = anime.data;

  const releaseDate = resolveReleaseDate(d.aired, d.year);

  return {
    ...anime,
    data: {
      ...d,
      status: toDisplayStatus(d.status as RawStatus),
      type: toDisplayType(d.type as RawType),
      cover: d.image ?? '',
      studio: resolveStudio(d.studios),
      genre: resolveGenre(d.genres),
      releaseDate,
      score: resolveScore(d.stats),
      episodeList: episodesBySlug[slug] ?? [],
      franchises: franchisesBySlug[slug] ?? [],
    },
  };
}

export async function getAllAnime(): Promise<HydratedAnime[]> {
  const all = await getCollection('anime', ({ data }) => !data.draft);
  return Promise.all(all.map(hydrateOne));
}

export async function getAnimeById(id: string): Promise<HydratedAnime | null> {
  const anime = await getEntry('anime', id);
  if (!anime || anime.data.draft) return null;
  return hydrateOne(anime);
}

export function sortByRecent(items: HydratedAnime[]): HydratedAnime[] {
  return [...items].sort((a, b) => {
    const at = (a.data.updatedAt ?? a.data.addedAt)?.getTime() ?? 0;
    const bt = (b.data.updatedAt ?? b.data.addedAt)?.getTime() ?? 0;
    return bt - at;
  });
}

export function getYear(anime: HydratedAnime): number | null {
  return anime.data.releaseDate?.getFullYear() ?? null;
}

export interface EpisodePathItem {
  slug: string;
  anime: HydratedAnime;
  episode: EpisodeData;
  episodeIndex: number;
  totalEpisodes: number;
}

export async function getAllEpisodePaths(): Promise<EpisodePathItem[]> {
  const all = await getAllAnime();
  const paths: EpisodePathItem[] = [];
  for (const anime of all) {
    const eps = anime.data.episodeList;
    const total = eps.length;
    eps.forEach((episode, episodeIndex) => {
      paths.push({ slug: anime.id, anime, episode, episodeIndex, totalEpisodes: total });
    });
  }
  return paths;
}

export interface WatchPathItem {
  slug: string;
  anime: HydratedAnime;
  episodes: EpisodeData[];
  firstEpisode: number;
  totalEpisodes: number;
}

export async function getAllWatchPaths(): Promise<WatchPathItem[]> {
  const all = await getAllAnime();
  const paths: WatchPathItem[] = [];

  for (const anime of all) {
    const eps = anime.data.episodeList;
    if (eps.length === 0) continue;

    const sorted = [...eps].sort((a, b) => a.number - b.number);
    paths.push({
      slug: anime.id,
      anime,
      episodes: sorted,
      firstEpisode: sorted[0]!.number,
      totalEpisodes: sorted.length,
    });
  }

  return paths;
}

export function findEpisode(anime: HydratedAnime, episodeNumber: number): EpisodeData | null {
  return anime.data.episodeList.find((e) => e.number === episodeNumber) ?? null;
}

===== FILE: src/lib/blog.ts =====
import { getCollection } from 'astro:content';
import type { CollectionEntry } from 'astro:content';

export type BlogEntry = CollectionEntry<'blog'>;

/**
 * Ambil semua artikel blog yang published (bukan draft).
 * Sort by date descending (terbaru dulu).
 */
export async function getPublishedPosts(): Promise<BlogEntry[]> {
  const allPosts = await getCollection('blog', ({ data }) => !data.draft);
  return [...allPosts].sort(
    (a, b) => b.data.date.getTime() - a.data.date.getTime()
  );
}

/**
 * Ambil artikel by slug — return null kalau draft atau tidak ada.
 */
export async function getPublishedPostBySlug(
  slug: string
): Promise<BlogEntry | null> {
  const allPosts = await getCollection('blog');
  const post = allPosts.find(p => p.id === slug);
  if (!post || post.data.draft) return null;
  return post;
}

/**
 * Ambil semua slug artikel published — untuk getStaticPaths.
 */
export async function getAllPostSlugs(): Promise<string[]> {
  const posts = await getPublishedPosts();
  return posts.map(p => p.id);
}

/**
 * Format tanggal ke format Indonesia.
 * Contoh: "15 Februari 2026"
 */
export function formatDate(date: Date): string {
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('id-ID', {
    day: 'numeric',
    month: 'long',
    year: 'numeric',
  }).format(date);
}

/**
 * Format tanggal singkat.
 * Contoh: "15 Feb 2026"
 */
export function formatDateShort(date: Date): string {
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('id-ID', {
    day: 'numeric',
    month: 'short',
    year: 'numeric',
  }).format(date);
}

/**
 * Ambil artikel terkait berdasarkan tag yang sama.
 * Return max `limit` artikel, exclude artikel itu sendiri.
 */
export function getRelatedPosts(
  current: BlogEntry,
  all: BlogEntry[],
  limit = 3
): BlogEntry[] {
  const currentTags = new Set(current.data.tags);

  if (currentTags.size === 0) return [];

  return all
    .filter(post => post.id !== current.id)
    .map(post => ({
      post,
      score: post.data.tags.filter(t => currentTags.has(t)).length,
    }))
    .filter(({ score }) => score > 0)
    .sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score)
    .slice(0, limit)
    .map(({ post }) => post);
}

/**
 * Ambil semua tag unik dari artikel published + jumlah per tag.
 * Sort by count descending, lalu alfabetis.
 */
export function collectTags(
  posts: BlogEntry[]
): { tag: string; count: number }[] {
  const counts: Record<string, number> = {};

  posts.forEach(post => {
    post.data.tags.forEach(tag => {
      counts[tag] = (counts[tag] || 0) + 1;
    });
  });

  return Object.entries(counts)
    .map(([tag, count]) => ({ tag, count }))
    .sort((a, b) => b.count - a.count || a.tag.localeCompare(b.tag, 'id-ID'));
}

/**
 * Ambil artikel by tag.
 */
export function filterByTag(posts: BlogEntry[], tag: string): BlogEntry[] {
  return posts.filter(post => post.data.tags.includes(tag));
}

/**
 * Ambil artikel by category.
 */
export function filterByCategory(
  posts: BlogEntry[],
  category: string
): BlogEntry[] {
  return posts.filter(post => post.data.category === category);
}

/**
 * Build canonical URL untuk artikel blog.
 */
export function postUrl(slug: string): string {
  return `/blog/${slug}/`;
}

// src/lib/dom.ts
export function cloneTemplate(id: string): HTMLElement | null {
  const tpl = document.getElementById(id) as HTMLTemplateElement | null;
  if (!tpl) return null;
  const frag = tpl.content.cloneNode(true) as DocumentFragment;
  return frag.firstElementChild as HTMLElement | null;
}

// src/lib/genre.ts
import { getCollection } from 'astro:content';
import { PER_PAGE } from '../constants';
import { getAllAnime, sortByRecent, type HydratedAnime } from './anime';

export type { HydratedAnime };

export interface GenreEntry {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  category: 'genre' | 'theme' | 'demographic';
}

export async function getAllGenreEntries(): Promise<GenreEntry[]> {
  const genres = await getCollection('genres');
  return genres.map(g => ({
    id: g.data.id,
    name: g.data.name,
    category: g.data.category,
  }));
}

export async function getAnimeByGenreSlug(
  slug: string
): Promise<HydratedAnime[]> {
  const allAnime = await getAllAnime();
  const filtered = allAnime.filter(a => a.data.genres.includes(slug));
  return sortByRecent(filtered);
}

export function paginate<T>(items: T[], page: number): T[] {
  const start = (page - 1) * PER_PAGE;
  return items.slice(start, start + PER_PAGE);
}
===== FILE: src/lib/pagination.ts =====
// src/lib/pagination.ts
export function buildPages(current: number, total: number): (number | '...')[] {
  if (total <= 7) {
    return Array.from({ length: total }, (_, i) => i + 1);
  }
  const pages: (number | '...')[] = [1];
  if (current > 3) pages.push('...');
  const start = Math.max(2, current - 1);
  const end = Math.min(total - 1, current + 1);
  for (let i = start; i <= end; i++) pages.push(i);
  if (current < total - 2) pages.push('...');
  pages.push(total);
  return pages;
}

export function paginateList<T>(list: T[], page: number, perPage: number) {
  const total = list.length;
  const totalPages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(total / perPage));
  const currentPage = Math.min(Math.max(1, page), totalPages);
  const start = (currentPage - 1) * perPage;
  const items = list.slice(start, start + perPage);
  return { items, total, totalPages, currentPage };
}

===== FILE: src/lib/status.ts =====
// src/lib/status.ts
import { PER_PAGE } from '../constants';
import {
  getAllAnime,
  sortByRecent,
  type HydratedAnime,
} from './anime';

export const STATUS_MAP = {
  ongoing: 'Ongoing',
  complete: 'Completed',
  hiatus: 'Hiatus',
} as const;

export type StatusSlug = keyof typeof STATUS_MAP;

export async function getAnimeByStatus(
  status: string
): Promise<HydratedAnime[]> {
  const allAnime = await getAllAnime();
  const filtered = allAnime.filter((a) => a.data.status === status);
  return sortByRecent(filtered);
}

export async function getStatusData(slug: StatusSlug) {
  const status = STATUS_MAP[slug];
  const anime = await getAnimeByStatus(status);
  const totalPages = Math.ceil(anime.length / PER_PAGE);
  return { status, anime, totalPages };
}

interface StatusPath {
  params: { page: string | undefined };
  props: {
    anime: HydratedAnime[];
    totalPages: number;
    pageNumber: number;
  };
}

export function buildStatusPaths(
  _slug: StatusSlug,
  anime: HydratedAnime[],
  totalPages: number
): StatusPath[] {
  const paths: StatusPath[] = [
    {
      params: { page: undefined },
      props: { anime, totalPages, pageNumber: 1 },
    },
  ];

  for (let page = 2; page <= totalPages; page++) {
    paths.push({
      params: { page: String(page) },
      props: { anime, totalPages, pageNumber: page },
    });
  }

  return paths;
}

export function paginate<T>(items: T[], page: number): T[] {
  const start = (page - 1) * PER_PAGE;
  return items.slice(start, start + PER_PAGE);
}

import { render, getEntry } from 'astro:content';
import { getAllAnime } from '~/lib/anime';

import {
  SITE, URLS, ADS,
  SOURCE_LABEL,
  AGE_RATING_LABEL,
  type Source,
  type AgeRating,
} from '~/constants';

import BaseLayout from '~/layouts/BaseLayout.astro';
import AnimeHero from '~/components/AnimeHero.astro';
import AnimeInfo from '~/components/AnimeInfo.astro';
import AnimeRatings from '~/components/AnimeRatings.astro';
import AnimeFranchise from '~/components/AnimeFranchise.astro';
import ShareButton from '~/components/ShareButton.astro';
import EpisodeList from '~/components/EpisodeList.astro';
import Comments from '~/components/Comments.astro';
import AdSlot from '~/components/AdSlot.astro';

export async function getStaticPaths() {
  const allAnime = await getAllAnime();
  return allAnime.map((anime) => ({
    params: { slug: anime.id },
    props: { anime },
  }));
}

const { anime } = Astro.props;
const { data } = anime;

const entry = await getEntry('anime', anime.id);

if (!entry) {
  throw new Error(`Entri anime '${anime.id}' tidak ditemukan.`);
}

const { Content } = await render(entry);

const episodeItems = data.episodeList
  .map((e) => ({
    number: e.number,
    title: e.title ?? null,
    duration: e.duration ?? null,
    aired: e.aired ?? null,
  }))
  .sort((a, b) => a.number - b.number);

const hasEpisodes = episodeItems.length > 0;
const firstEpisode = episodeItems[0]?.number ?? 1;
const totalEpisodes = episodeItems.length;

const heroImage = data.cover ?? '';

const genres: { name: string; slug: string }[] = [];
const rawGenres = data.genres ?? [];
for (let i = 0; i < rawGenres.length; i++) {
  const slug = rawGenres[i];
  const name = data.genre[i] ?? slug;
  if (slug && name && name !== 'Unknown') {
    genres.push({ name, slug });
  }
}
const MAX_GENRES = 3;
const visibleGenres = genres.slice(0, MAX_GENRES);

let airedLabel: string | null = null;
if (data.aired?.from) {
  const from = new Intl.DateTimeFormat(SITE.locale, {
    day: 'numeric',
    month: 'short',
    year: 'numeric',
  }).format(data.aired.from);
  const to = data.aired.to
    ? new Intl.DateTimeFormat(SITE.locale, {
        day: 'numeric',
        month: 'short',
        year: 'numeric',
      }).format(data.aired.to)
    : null;
  airedLabel = to ? `${from} – ${to}` : from;
}

const durationLabel = data.duration ? `${data.duration} menit / episode` : null;
const malScore = data.stats?.score ?? null;
const ageRating = data.rating ?? null;
const studioName = data.studio && data.studio !== 'Unknown' ? data.studio : null;
const sourceLabel = data.source
  ? SOURCE_LABEL[data.source as Source] ?? data.source
  : null;

const year = data.releaseDate?.getFullYear() ?? null;
const pageDescription = [
  `Nonton ${data.title} Sub Indo.`,
  studioName,
  year ? String(year) : null,
].filter(Boolean).join(' ');

const infoItems: { label: string; value: string }[] = [];

if (data.type) infoItems.push({ label: 'Tipe', value: data.type });
if (totalEpisodes > 0) infoItems.push({ label: 'Episode', value: String(totalEpisodes) });
if (data.status) infoItems.push({ label: 'Status', value: data.status });
if (airedLabel) infoItems.push({ label: 'Rilis', value: airedLabel });
if (studioName) infoItems.push({ label: 'Studio', value: studioName });
if (sourceLabel) infoItems.push({ label: 'Sumber', value: sourceLabel });
if (malScore != null) infoItems.push({ label: 'Skor MAL', value: `${malScore.toFixed(1)} / 10` });
if (durationLabel) infoItems.push({ label: 'Durasi', value: durationLabel });
if (ageRating) {
  infoItems.push({
    label: 'Rating',
    value: AGE_RATING_LABEL[ageRating as AgeRating] ?? ageRating,
  });
}

const genreLinks = visibleGenres.map((g) => ({
  name: g.name,
  href: URLS.genre(g.name),
}));
---

<script>
  (function () {
    const gridEl = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-meta]');
    if (!gridEl) return;
    const grid: HTMLElement = gridEl;

    const btnEl = document.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-synopsis-toggle]');
    if (!btnEl) return;
    const btn: HTMLButtonElement = btnEl;

    const labelEl = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-synopsis-label]');
    if (!labelEl) return;
    const label: HTMLElement = labelEl;

    let collapsedHeight = 0;

    function measure() {
      if (!grid.classList.contains('is-expanded')) {
        collapsedHeight = grid.clientHeight;
      }

      const prevMax = grid.style.maxHeight;
      grid.style.maxHeight = 'none';
      const full = grid.scrollHeight;
      grid.style.maxHeight = prevMax;

      const hasOverflow = full > collapsedHeight + 2;
      btn.classList.toggle('is-hidden', !hasOverflow);
    }

    function run() {
      if (document.readyState === 'complete') {
        requestAnimationFrame(measure);
      } else {
        window.addEventListener(
          'load',
          () => requestAnimationFrame(measure),
          { once: true }
        );
      }
    }

    run();

    let resizeTimer = 0;
    window.addEventListener('resize', () => {
      window.clearTimeout(resizeTimer);
      resizeTimer = window.setTimeout(measure, 200);
    });

    btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const expanded = grid.classList.toggle('is-expanded');
      label.textContent = expanded ? 'Sembunyikan' : 'Lebih Banyak';
    });
  })();
</script>

===== FILE: src/pages/anime/completed/index.astro =====

import BaseLayout from '~/layouts/BaseLayout.astro';
import Page from '~/components/Page.astro';
import Hero from '~/components/Hero.astro';
import AnimeCard from '~/components/AnimeCard.astro';
import SkeletonGrid from '~/components/SkeletonGrid.astro';

<BaseLayout
  title="Anime Completed"
  description="Koleksi anime yang sudah selesai tayang. Urutkan berdasarkan popularitas atau terbaru."
  canonical="/anime/completed/"
>
  <Page class="wrapper">
    <Hero title="Anime Completed">
      <Fragment slot="action">
        <label class="sort-wrap">
          <span class="sort-label">Urutkan</span>
          <select class="sort-select" data-sort>
            <option value="popular">Populer</option>
            <option value="newest">Terbaru</option>
            <option value="title">A-Z</option>
          </select>
        </label>
      </Fragment>
    </Hero>

    <SkeletonGrid />

    <div class="anime-grid" data-grid hidden></div>

    <div class="load-more-wrap" data-load-more-wrap hidden>
      <button type="button" class="load-more-btn" data-load-more>
        <span data-load-more-text>Muat Lebih Banyak</span>
      </button>
    </div>
  </Page>

  <AnimeCard mode="template" />
</BaseLayout>

<script>
  // @ts-nocheck
  import {
    STATUS_LABEL,
    STATUS_VARIANT,
    TYPE_LABEL,
    type RawStatus,
    type RawType,
    type Status,
  } from '~/constants';

  (function () {
    const API = 'https://api-qimochi.pages.dev/api/v1/anime.json';
    const AVAILABLE_API = '/api/available-episodes.json';
    const SYNOPSIS_API = '/api/anime-synopsis.json';
    const BATCH = 24;
    const DEFAULT_SORT = 'popular';

    type ApiItem = {
      id: string;
      title: string;
      titleEnglish: string | null;
      titleNative: string | null;
      image: string | null;
      banner?: string | null;
      type: string;
      status: string;
      season: string | null;
      year: number | null;
      episodes: number | null;
      duration: number | null;
      rating: string | null;
      genres: string[];
      studios: string[];
      score?: number | null;
      stats?: { score?: number; scoredBy?: number } | null;
    };

    const grid = document.querySelector('[data-grid]') as HTMLElement | null;
    const skeleton = document.querySelector('[data-skeleton]') as HTMLElement | null;
    const loadMoreWrap = document.querySelector('[data-load-more-wrap]') as HTMLElement | null;
    const loadMoreBtn = document.querySelector('[data-load-more]') as HTMLButtonElement | null;
    const loadMoreText = document.querySelector('[data-load-more-text]') as HTMLElement | null;
    const sortEl = document.querySelector('[data-sort]') as HTMLSelectElement | null;
    const tpl = document.getElementById('anime-card-template') as HTMLTemplateElement | null;

    if (!grid || !tpl || !sortEl) return;

    let all: ApiItem[] = [];
    let sorted: ApiItem[] = [];
    let visibleCount = 0;
    let availableMap = new Map<string, number[]>();
    let synopsisMap = new Map<string, string>();

    function hideSkeleton(): void {
      if (skeleton) skeleton.hidden = true;
    }

    function titleCase(s: string): string {
      if (!s) return '';
      return s
        .split(/[-_\s]+/)
        .filter(Boolean)
        .map((w) => w.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + w.slice(1).toLowerCase())
        .join(' ');
    }

    function formatCount(n: number): string {
      if (n >= 1_000_000) return (n / 1_000_000).toFixed(1).replace(/\.0$/, '') + 'M';
      if (n >= 1_000) return (n / 1_000).toFixed(1).replace(/\.0$/, '') + 'K';
      return String(n);
    }

    function mapStatus(raw: string) {
      const label = STATUS_LABEL[raw as RawStatus] ?? raw;
      const variant = STATUS_VARIANT[label as Status] ?? 'default';
      return { label, variant };
    }

    function mapType(raw: string): string {
      return TYPE_LABEL[raw as RawType] ?? raw;
    }

    function getScore(item: ApiItem): number | null {
      if (typeof item.score === 'number') return item.score;
      if (typeof item.stats?.score === 'number') return item.stats.score;
      return null;
    }

    function getVotes(item: ApiItem): number {
      return item.stats?.scoredBy ?? 0;
    }

    function buildCard(item: ApiItem): HTMLElement | null {
      const frag = tpl!.content.cloneNode(true) as DocumentFragment;
      const card = frag.firstElementChild as HTMLElement | null;
      if (!card) return null;

      const available = availableMap.get(item.id) ?? [];
      const firstEp = available[0] ?? 1;

      const link = card.querySelector('[data-card-link]') as HTMLAnchorElement | null;
      if (link) {
        link.href = `/anime/${item.id}/`;
        link.setAttribute('aria-label', item.title);
      }

      const img = card.querySelector('[data-cover-img]') as HTMLImageElement | null;
      const placeholder = card.querySelector('.cover-placeholder') as HTMLElement | null;

      if (img && item.image) {
        img.src = item.image;
        img.alt = `Cover ${item.title}`;

        const stopShimmer = () => placeholder?.classList.remove('is-loading');
        const fail = () => {
          img.hidden = true;
          stopShimmer();
        };

        if (img.complete && img.naturalWidth > 0) {
          stopShimmer();
        } else {
          img.addEventListener('load', stopShimmer, { once: true });
          img.addEventListener('error', fail, { once: true });
        }

        img.dataset.ready = '1';
      } else if (placeholder) {
        placeholder.classList.remove('is-loading');
      }

      const typeEl = card.querySelector('[data-card-type]') as HTMLElement | null;
      if (typeEl) typeEl.textContent = mapType(item.type);

      const statusEl = card.querySelector('[data-card-status]') as HTMLElement | null;
      if (statusEl) {
        const { label, variant: v } = mapStatus(item.status);
        statusEl.textContent = label;
        statusEl.dataset.variant = v;
      }

      const titleEl = card.querySelector('[data-card-title]') as HTMLElement | null;
      if (titleEl) titleEl.textContent = item.title;

      const titleInfoEl = card.querySelector('[data-card-title-info]') as HTMLElement | null;
      if (titleInfoEl) titleInfoEl.textContent = item.title;

      const score = getScore(item);
      const votes = getVotes(item);
      const ratingEl = card.querySelector('[data-card-rating]') as HTMLElement | null;
      if (ratingEl && score != null) {
        ratingEl.hidden = false;
        const fill = card.querySelector('[data-card-stars-fill]') as HTMLElement | null;
        if (fill) fill.style.width = `${(score / 10) * 100}%`;
        const scoreEl = card.querySelector('[data-card-score]') as HTMLElement | null;
        if (scoreEl) scoreEl.textContent = score.toFixed(1);
        const votesEl = card.querySelector('[data-card-votes]') as HTMLElement | null;
        if (votesEl) votesEl.textContent = votes > 0 ? `(${formatCount(votes)})` : '';
      }

      const studioEl = card.querySelector('[data-card-studio]') as HTMLElement | null;
      if (studioEl && Array.isArray(item.studios) && item.studios.length > 0) {
        const name = titleCase(item.studios[0]);
        if (name && name.toLowerCase() !== 'unknown') {
          const li = studioEl.querySelector('li');
          if (li) li.textContent = name;
          studioEl.hidden = false;
        }
      }

      const excerptEl = card.querySelector('[data-card-excerpt]') as HTMLElement | null;
      if (excerptEl) {
        const text = synopsisMap.get(item.id);
        if (text) {
          excerptEl.textContent = text;
          excerptEl.hidden = false;
        }
      }

      const episodeText = available.length > 0 ? `${available.length} Episode` : '';

      const episodesEl = card.querySelector('[data-card-episodes]') as HTMLElement | null;
      if (episodesEl && episodeText) {
        episodesEl.textContent = episodeText;
        episodesEl.hidden = false;
      }

      const metaTextEl = card.querySelector('[data-card-meta-text]') as HTMLElement | null;
      if (metaTextEl && episodeText) {
        metaTextEl.textContent = episodeText;
      }

      const playEl = card.querySelector('[data-card-play]') as HTMLAnchorElement | null;
      if (playEl) {
        playEl.href = firstEp > 1
          ? `/anime/watch/${item.id}/?ep=${firstEp}`
          : `/anime/watch/${item.id}/`;
        playEl.setAttribute('aria-label', `Mulai menonton ${item.title}`);
      }

      const bookmarkEl = card.querySelector('[data-bookmark-btn]') as HTMLButtonElement | null;
      if (bookmarkEl) bookmarkEl.dataset.anime = item.id;

      return card;
    }

    function sortList(list: ApiItem[], sort: string): ApiItem[] {
      const copy = [...list];
      if (sort === 'newest') {
        return copy.sort((a, b) => {
          const ya = a.year ?? 0;
          const yb = b.year ?? 0;
          if (yb !== ya) return yb - ya;
          return a.title.localeCompare(b.title);
        });
      }
      if (sort === 'title') {
        return copy.sort((a, b) => a.title.localeCompare(b.title));
      }
      return copy.sort((a, b) => {
        const sa = getScore(a) ?? 0;
        const sb = getScore(b) ?? 0;
        if (sb !== sa) return sb - sa;
        const va = getVotes(a);
        const vb = getVotes(b);
        return vb - va;
      });
    }

    function renderNext(): void {
      const end = Math.min(visibleCount + BATCH, sorted.length);
      if (end <= visibleCount) return;

      const frag = document.createDocumentFragment();
      for (let i = visibleCount; i < end; i++) {
        const card = buildCard(sorted[i]!);
        if (card) frag.appendChild(card);
      }
      grid.appendChild(frag);
      visibleCount = end;

      window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('qimochi:bookmarks', { detail: grid }));
      updateLoadMore();
    }

    function updateLoadMore(): void {
      const remaining = sorted.length - visibleCount;
      if (!loadMoreWrap || !loadMoreText) return;
      loadMoreWrap.hidden = remaining <= 0;
      if (remaining > 0) {
        loadMoreText.textContent = `Muat Lebih Banyak (${remaining} lagi)`;
      }
    }

    function render(reset = false): void {
      if (reset) {
        grid.innerHTML = '';
        visibleCount = 0;
      }

      const hasItems = sorted.length > 0;
      grid.hidden = !hasItems;

      if (hasItems) {
        renderNext();
      }
    }

    function applySort(sort: string): void {
      sorted = sortList(all, sort);
      render(true);
    }

    sortEl.addEventListener('change', () => {
      applySort(sortEl.value || DEFAULT_SORT);
    });

    if (loadMoreBtn) {
      loadMoreBtn.addEventListener('click', () => renderNext());
    }

    Promise.all([
      fetch(API, { headers: { Accept: 'application/json' } }).then((r) => {
        if (!r.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${r.status}`);
        return r.json() as Promise<{ data: ApiItem[] }>;
      }),
      fetch(AVAILABLE_API, { headers: { Accept: 'application/json' } })
        .then((r) => (r.ok ? r.json() : {}))
        .catch(() => ({})) as Promise<Record<string, number[]>>,
      fetch(SYNOPSIS_API, { headers: { Accept: 'application/json' } })
        .then((r) => (r.ok ? r.json() : {}))
        .catch(() => ({})) as Promise<Record<string, string>>,
    ])
      .then(([json, availableRaw, synRaw]) => {
        const list = json?.data;
        if (!Array.isArray(list)) return;

        availableMap = new Map<string, number[]>();
        if (availableRaw && typeof availableRaw === 'object') {
          for (const [slug, eps] of Object.entries(availableRaw)) {
            if (Array.isArray(eps)) {
              availableMap.set(slug, eps.filter((n): n is number => typeof n === 'number'));
            }
          }
        }

        synopsisMap = new Map<string, string>();
        if (synRaw && typeof synRaw === 'object') {
          for (const [slug, text] of Object.entries(synRaw)) {
            if (typeof text === 'string' && text.length > 0) {
              synopsisMap.set(slug, text);
            }
          }
        }

        all = list.filter(
          (item) =>
            item.status === 'finished' &&
            (availableMap.get(item.id)?.length ?? 0) > 0
        );

        sortEl.value = DEFAULT_SORT;
        applySort(DEFAULT_SORT);
      })
      .catch((err) => {
        console.error('[completed] fetch failed:', err);
      })
      .finally(hideSkeleton);
  })();
</script>

===== FILE: src/pages/anime/genre/[slug]/index.astro =====

import { getCollection } from 'astro:content';
import BaseLayout from '~/layouts/BaseLayout.astro';
import Page from '~/components/Page.astro';
import Hero from '~/components/Hero.astro';
import AnimeCard from '~/components/AnimeCard.astro';
import SectionLabel from '~/components/SectionLabel.astro';
import SkeletonGrid from '~/components/SkeletonGrid.astro';

export async function getStaticPaths() {
  const genres = await getCollection('genres');
  return genres.map((g) => ({
    params: { slug: g.data.id },
    props: {
      genreId: g.data.id,
      genreName: g.data.name,
      genreCategory: g.data.category,
    },
  }));
}

const { genreId, genreName, genreCategory } = Astro.props;

const pageTitle = `Anime ${genreName}`;
const pageDesc = `Koleksi anime genre ${genreName} subtitle Indonesia.`;
const canonical = `/anime/genre/${genreId}/`;
---

<BaseLayout
  title={pageTitle}
  description={pageDesc}
  canonical={canonical}
>
  <Page class="wrapper">
    <Hero title={genreName}>
      <Fragment slot="action">
        <label class="sort-wrap">
          <span class="sort-label">Urutkan</span>
          <select class="sort-select" data-sort>
            <option value="popular">Populer</option>
            <option value="newest">Terbaru</option>
            <option value="title">A-Z</option>
          </select>
        </label>
      </Fragment>
    </Hero>

    <SkeletonGrid />

    <section class="section" data-popular-section hidden>
      <SectionLabel>Populer</SectionLabel>
      <div class="anime-grid" data-popular-grid></div>
    </section>

    <section class="section" data-list-section hidden>
      <SectionLabel>Anime Genre {genreName}</SectionLabel>
      <div class="anime-grid" data-grid hidden></div>

      <div class="load-more-wrap" data-load-more-wrap hidden>
        <button type="button" class="load-more-btn" data-load-more>
          <span data-load-more-text>Muat Lebih Banyak</span>
        </button>
      </div>
    </section>

    <div class="empty" data-empty hidden>
      <p>Belum ada anime untuk genre ini.</p>
      <p class="empty-hint">
        Coba jelajahi <a href="/anime/genre/">genre lain</a>.
      </p>
    </div>
  </Page>

  <AnimeCard mode="template" />
</BaseLayout>

<script>
  // @ts-nocheck
  import {
    STATUS_LABEL,
    STATUS_VARIANT,
    TYPE_LABEL,
    type RawStatus,
    type RawType,
    type Status,
  } from '~/constants';

  (function () {
    const pathMatch = window.location.pathname.match(/\/anime\/genre\/([^/]+)\/?$/);
    const SLUG = pathMatch ? decodeURIComponent(pathMatch[1]) : '';
    if (!SLUG) return;

    const API = 'https://api-qimochi.pages.dev/api/v1/anime.json';
    const AVAILABLE_API = '/api/available-episodes.json';
    const SYNOPSIS_API = '/api/anime-synopsis.json';
    const BATCH = 24;
    const POPULAR_LIMIT = 6;
    const DEFAULT_SORT = 'popular';

    type ApiItem = {
      id: string;
      title: string;
      titleEnglish: string | null;
      titleNative: string | null;
      image: string | null;
      banner?: string | null;
      type: string;
      status: string;
      season: string | null;
      year: number | null;
      episodes: number | null;
      duration: number | null;
      rating: string | null;
      genres: string[];
      studios: string[];
      score?: number | null;
      stats?: { score?: number; scoredBy?: number } | null;
    };

    const grid = document.querySelector('[data-grid]');
    const skeleton = document.querySelector('[data-skeleton]');
    const popularSection = document.querySelector('[data-popular-section]');
    const popularGrid = document.querySelector('[data-popular-grid]');
    const listSection = document.querySelector('[data-list-section]');
    const emptyState = document.querySelector('[data-empty]');
    const loadMoreWrap = document.querySelector('[data-load-more-wrap]');
    const loadMoreBtn = document.querySelector('[data-load-more]');
    const loadMoreText = document.querySelector('[data-load-more-text]');
    const sortEl = document.querySelector('[data-sort]');
    const tpl = document.getElementById('anime-card-template');

    if (!grid || !tpl || !sortEl) return;

    let all: ApiItem[] = [];
    let sorted: ApiItem[] = [];
    let visibleCount = 0;
    let availableMap = new Map<string, number[]>();
    let synopsisMap = new Map<string, string>();

    function hideSkeleton(): void {
      if (skeleton) skeleton.hidden = true;
    }

    function titleCase(s: string): string {
      if (!s) return '';
      return s
        .split(/[-_\s]+/)
        .filter(Boolean)
        .map((w) => w.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + w.slice(1).toLowerCase())
        .join(' ');
    }

    function formatCount(n: number): string {
      if (n >= 1_000_000) return (n / 1_000_000).toFixed(1).replace(/\.0$/, '') + 'M';
      if (n >= 1_000) return (n / 1_000).toFixed(1).replace(/\.0$/, '') + 'K';
      return String(n);
    }

    function mapStatus(raw: string) {
      const label = STATUS_LABEL[raw as RawStatus] ?? raw;
      const variant = STATUS_VARIANT[label as Status] ?? 'default';
      return { label, variant };
    }

    function mapType(raw: string): string {
      return TYPE_LABEL[raw as RawType] ?? raw;
    }

    function getScore(item: ApiItem): number | null {
      if (typeof item.score === 'number') return item.score;
      if (typeof item.stats?.score === 'number') return item.stats.score;
      return null;
    }

    function getVotes(item: ApiItem): number {
      return item.stats?.scoredBy ?? 0;
    }

    function buildCard(item: ApiItem): HTMLElement | null {
      const frag = tpl!.content.cloneNode(true);
      const card = frag.firstElementChild;
      if (!card) return null;

      const available = availableMap.get(item.id) ?? [];
      const firstEp = available[0] ?? 1;

      const link = card.querySelector('[data-card-link]');
      if (link) {
        link.href = `/anime/${item.id}/`;
        link.setAttribute('aria-label', item.title);
      }

      const img = card.querySelector('[data-cover-img]');
      const placeholder = card.querySelector('.cover-placeholder');

      if (img && item.image) {
        img.src = item.image;
        img.alt = `Cover ${item.title}`;

        const stopShimmer = () => placeholder?.classList.remove('is-loading');
        const fail = () => {
          img.hidden = true;
          stopShimmer();
        };

        if (img.complete && img.naturalWidth > 0) {
          stopShimmer();
        } else {
          img.addEventListener('load', stopShimmer, { once: true });
          img.addEventListener('error', fail, { once: true });
        }

        img.dataset.ready = '1';
      } else if (placeholder) {
        placeholder.classList.remove('is-loading');
      }

      const typeEl = card.querySelector('[data-card-type]');
      if (typeEl) typeEl.textContent = mapType(item.type);

      const statusEl = card.querySelector('[data-card-status]');
      if (statusEl) {
        const { label, variant: v } = mapStatus(item.status);
        statusEl.textContent = label;
        statusEl.dataset.variant = v;
      }

      const titleEl = card.querySelector('[data-card-title]');
      if (titleEl) titleEl.textContent = item.title;

      const titleInfoEl = card.querySelector('[data-card-title-info]');
      if (titleInfoEl) titleInfoEl.textContent = item.title;

      const score = getScore(item);
      const votes = getVotes(item);
      const ratingEl = card.querySelector('[data-card-rating]');
      if (ratingEl && score != null) {
        ratingEl.hidden = false;
        const fill = card.querySelector('[data-card-stars-fill]');
        if (fill) fill.style.width = `${(score / 10) * 100}%`;
        const scoreEl = card.querySelector('[data-card-score]');
        if (scoreEl) scoreEl.textContent = score.toFixed(1);
        const votesEl = card.querySelector('[data-card-votes]');
        if (votesEl) votesEl.textContent = votes > 0 ? `(${formatCount(votes)})` : '';
      }

      const studioEl = card.querySelector('[data-card-studio]');
      if (studioEl && Array.isArray(item.studios) && item.studios.length > 0) {
        const name = titleCase(item.studios[0]);
        if (name && name.toLowerCase() !== 'unknown') {
          const li = studioEl.querySelector('li');
          if (li) li.textContent = name;
          studioEl.hidden = false;
        }
      }

      const excerptEl = card.querySelector('[data-card-excerpt]');
      if (excerptEl) {
        const text = synopsisMap.get(item.id);
        if (text) {
          excerptEl.textContent = text;
          excerptEl.hidden = false;
        }
      }

      const episodeText = available.length > 0 ? `${available.length} Episode` : '';

      const episodesEl = card.querySelector('[data-card-episodes]') as HTMLElement | null;
      if (episodesEl && episodeText) {
        episodesEl.textContent = episodeText;
        episodesEl.hidden = false;
      }

      const metaTextEl = card.querySelector('[data-card-meta-text]') as HTMLElement | null;
      if (metaTextEl && episodeText) {
        metaTextEl.textContent = episodeText;
      }

      const playEl = card.querySelector('[data-card-play]');
      if (playEl) {
        playEl.href = firstEp > 1
          ? `/anime/watch/${item.id}/?ep=${firstEp}`
          : `/anime/watch/${item.id}/`;
        playEl.setAttribute('aria-label', `Mulai menonton ${item.title}`);
      }

      const bookmarkEl = card.querySelector('[data-bookmark-btn]');
      if (bookmarkEl) bookmarkEl.dataset.anime = item.id;

      return card;
    }

    function sortList(list: ApiItem[], sort: string): ApiItem[] {
      const copy = [...list];
      if (sort === 'newest') {
        return copy.sort((a, b) => {
          const ya = a.year ?? 0;
          const yb = b.year ?? 0;
          if (yb !== ya) return yb - ya;
          return a.title.localeCompare(b.title);
        });
      }
      if (sort === 'title') {
        return copy.sort((a, b) => a.title.localeCompare(b.title));
      }
      return copy.sort((a, b) => {
        const sa = getScore(a) ?? 0;
        const sb = getScore(b) ?? 0;
        if (sb !== sa) return sb - sa;
        const va = getVotes(a);
        const vb = getVotes(b);
        return vb - va;
      });
    }

    function renderPopular(): void {
      if (!popularGrid || !popularSection) return;

      const top = sorted.slice(0, POPULAR_LIMIT);

      if (top.length === 0) {
        popularSection.hidden = true;
        return;
      }

      popularGrid.innerHTML = '';
      const frag = document.createDocumentFragment();
      for (const item of top) {
        const card = buildCard(item);
        if (card) frag.appendChild(card);
      }
      popularGrid.appendChild(frag);
      popularSection.hidden = false;

      window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('qimochi:bookmarks', { detail: popularGrid }));
    }

    function renderNext(): void {
      const end = Math.min(visibleCount + BATCH, sorted.length);
      if (end <= visibleCount) return;

      const frag = document.createDocumentFragment();
      for (let i = visibleCount; i < end; i++) {
        const card = buildCard(sorted[i]);
        if (card) frag.appendChild(card);
      }
      grid.appendChild(frag);
      visibleCount = end;

      window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('qimochi:bookmarks', { detail: grid }));
      updateLoadMore();
    }

    function updateLoadMore(): void {
      const remaining = sorted.length - visibleCount;
      if (!loadMoreWrap || !loadMoreText) return;
      loadMoreWrap.hidden = remaining <= 0;
      if (remaining > 0) {
        loadMoreText.textContent = `Muat Lebih Banyak (${remaining} lagi)`;
      }
    }

    function render(reset = false): void {
      if (reset) {
        grid.innerHTML = '';
        visibleCount = 0;
      }

      const hasItems = sorted.length > 0;

      if (listSection) listSection.hidden = !hasItems;
      if (emptyState) emptyState.hidden = hasItems;

      grid.hidden = !hasItems;

      renderPopular();

      if (hasItems) {
        renderNext();
      }
    }

    function applySort(sort: string): void {
      sorted = sortList(all, sort);
      render(true);
    }

    sortEl.addEventListener('change', () => {
      applySort(sortEl.value || DEFAULT_SORT);
    });

    if (loadMoreBtn) {
      loadMoreBtn.addEventListener('click', () => renderNext());
    }

    Promise.all([
      fetch(API, { headers: { Accept: 'application/json' } }).then((res) => {
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}`);
        return res.json();
      }),
      fetch(AVAILABLE_API, { headers: { Accept: 'application/json' } })
        .then((r) => (r.ok ? r.json() : {}))
        .catch(() => ({})),
      fetch(SYNOPSIS_API, { headers: { Accept: 'application/json' } })
        .then((r) => (r.ok ? r.json() : {}))
        .catch(() => ({})),
    ])
      .then(([json, availableRaw, synRaw]) => {
        const list = json?.data;
        if (!Array.isArray(list)) return;

        availableMap = new Map();
        if (availableRaw && typeof availableRaw === 'object') {
          for (const [slug, eps] of Object.entries(availableRaw)) {
            if (Array.isArray(eps)) {
              availableMap.set(slug, eps.filter((n) => typeof n === 'number'));
            }
          }
        }

        synopsisMap = new Map();
        if (synRaw && typeof synRaw === 'object') {
          for (const [slug, text] of Object.entries(synRaw)) {
            if (typeof text === 'string' && text.length > 0) {
              synopsisMap.set(slug, text);
            }
          }
        }

        all = list.filter((item) => {
          if (!Array.isArray(item.genres)) return false;
          if (!item.genres.includes(SLUG)) return false;
          return (availableMap.get(item.id)?.length ?? 0) > 0;
        });

        if (all.length === 0) {
          if (popularSection) popularSection.hidden = true;
          if (listSection) listSection.hidden = true;
          if (emptyState) emptyState.hidden = false;
          return;
        }

        sortEl.value = DEFAULT_SORT;
        applySort(DEFAULT_SORT);
      })
      .catch((err) => {
        console.error('[genre] fetch failed:', err);
      })
      .finally(hideSkeleton);
  })();
</script>

<style>

===== FILE: src/pages/anime/genre/index.astro =====

import { getCollection } from 'astro:content';
import BaseLayout from '~/layouts/BaseLayout.astro';
import Hero from '~/components/Hero.astro';
import LinkCard from '~/user-components/LinkCard.astro';
import { URLS } from '~/constants';
import Page from '~/components/Page.astro';

const allGenres = await getCollection('genres');

const categoryOrder = { genre: 0, theme: 1, demographic: 2 } as const;

const genres = allGenres
  .map(g => ({
    id: g.data.id,
    name: g.data.name,
    category: g.data.category,
  }))
  .sort((a, b) => {
    const diff = categoryOrder[a.category] - categoryOrder[b.category];
    if (diff !== 0) return diff;
    return a.name.localeCompare(b.name, 'id-ID');
  });
---

<BaseLayout
  title="Genre Anime"
  description="Jelajahi anime berdasarkan genre."
  canonical="/anime/genre/"
>
  <Page class="wrapper">

    <Hero title="Semua Genre" />

    <div class="genre-grid">
      {genres.map(g => (
        <LinkCard
          href={URLS.genre(g.id)}
          variant="outline"
          padding="md"
        >
          <div class="genre-content">
            <span class="genre-name">{g.name}</span>
          </div>
        </LinkCard>
      ))}
    </div>

  </Page>
</BaseLayout>

    min-width: 0;
    overflow-wrap: anywhere;
  }

===== FILE: src/pages/anime/index.astro =====
---
import { getAllAnime } from '~/lib/anime';
import BaseLayout from '~/layouts/BaseLayout.astro';
import Hero from '~/components/Hero.astro';
import Badge from '~/user-components/Badge.astro';
import { URLS, STATUS_VARIANT, type Status } from '~/constants';
import Page from '~/components/Page.astro';

const allAnime = await getAllAnime();

const sorted = [...allAnime].sort((a, b) =>
  a.data.title.localeCompare(b.data.title, 'id-ID')
);

function getFirstLetter(title: string): string {
  const first = title[0]?.toUpperCase() ?? '';
  if (/[A-Z]/.test(first)) return first;
  return '#';
}

const letters = new Set<string>();
sorted.forEach(a => letters.add(getFirstLetter(a.data.title)));

const activeLetters = Array.from(letters).sort((a, b) => {
  if (a === '#') return 1;
  if (b === '#') return -1;
  return a.localeCompare(b);
});

const totalAnime = allAnime.length;

const groups = activeLetters.map(letter => ({
  letter,
  items: sorted.filter(a => getFirstLetter(a.data.title) === letter),
}));
---

<BaseLayout
  title="Archive Anime"
  description="Daftar anime di Qimochi"
  canonical="/anime/"
>
  <Page class="wrapper">

    <Hero
      title="Archive Anime"
    />

    {activeLetters.length > 1 && (
      <nav class="letter-nav" aria-label="Navigasi huruf">
        <ul class="letter-list">
          {activeLetters.map(letter => (
            <li><a href={`#letter-${letter}`}>{letter}</a></li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      </nav>
    )}

    {groups.map(({ letter, items }) => (
      <section class="letter-group" id={`letter-${letter}`}>
        <h2 class="letter-heading">{letter}</h2>

        <ol class="anime-list">
          {items.map(anime => (
            <li>
              <a href={URLS.anime(anime.id)} class="anime-link">
                <span class="anime-title">{anime.data.title}</span>
                {anime.data.status !== 'Completed' && (
                  <Badge
                    variant={STATUS_VARIANT[anime.data.status as Status]}
                    size="sm"
                  >
                    {anime.data.status}
                  </Badge>
                )}
              </a>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ol>
      </section>
    ))}

    {totalAnime === 0 && (
      <p class="empty">Belum ada anime di database.</p>
    )}

  </Page>
</BaseLayout>

<style>
  .wrapper {
    gap: var(--s-6);
  }

  .letter-nav {
    position: sticky;
    top: var(--header-height);
    z-index: 50;
    background: var(--bg-base);

    margin-inline: calc(var(--pad-x) * -1);
    padding-block: var(--s-3);
    padding-inline: var(--pad-x);
  }

  .letter-list {
    display: flex;
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    gap: var(--s-1);
    list-style: none;
    padding: 0;
    margin: 0;
  }

  .letter-list a {
    display: inline-flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    min-width: 2rem;
    height: 2rem;
    padding: 0 var(--s-2);
    background: var(--bg-surface);
    border: 1px solid transparent;
    font-size: var(--fs-xs);
    font-weight: var(--fw-semibold);
    color: var(--text-secondary);
    transition: all var(--t-fast);
    text-decoration: none;
  }

  .letter-list a:hover {
    border-color: var(--accent);
    color: var(--accent);
  }

  .letter-group {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: var(--s-2);
    scroll-margin-top: calc(var(--header-height) + 4rem);
    margin-bottom: var(--s-4);
  }

  .letter-heading {
    font-size: var(--fs-2xl);
    font-weight: var(--fw-bold);
    color: var(--accent);
    margin: 0;
    padding-bottom: var(--s-2);
    border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border);
    letter-spacing: 0.05em;
  }

  .anime-list {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: var(--s-1);
    list-style: none;
    padding: 0;
    margin: 0;
  }

  .anime-link {
    display: flex;
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: space-between;
    gap: var(--s-2) var(--s-4);

  .anime-title {

    font-size: var(--fs-sm);
    color: var(--text-primary);
    font-weight: var(--fw-medium);
    min-width: 0;
    overflow-wrap: anywhere;
    &::after {
      content: '';

      background-color: var(--accent);
      -webkit-mask-image: var(--underline);
      mask-image: var(--underline);
      -webkit-mask-size: 100% 100%;
      mask-size: 100% 100%;

      pointer-events: none;
      opacity: 0;
      transition: opacity var(--t-fast);
    }

  .anime-link:hover .anime-title {
