===== FILE: src/pages/anime/ongoing/index.astro =====
---
import BaseLayout from '~/layouts/BaseLayout.astro';
import Page from '~/components/Page.astro';
import Hero from '~/components/Hero.astro';
import AnimeCard from '~/components/AnimeCard.astro';
import SkeletonGrid from '~/components/SkeletonGrid.astro';
---

<BaseLayout
  title="Anime Ongoing"
  description="Koleksi anime yang sedang tayang. Urutkan berdasarkan popularitas atau terbaru."
  canonical="/anime/ongoing/"
>
  <Page class="wrapper">
    <Hero title="Anime Ongoing">
      <Fragment slot="action">
        <label class="sort-wrap">
          <span class="sort-label">Urutkan</span>
          <select class="sort-select" data-sort>
            <option value="popular">Populer</option>
            <option value="newest">Terbaru</option>
            <option value="title">A-Z</option>
          </select>
        </label>
      </Fragment>
    </Hero>

    <SkeletonGrid />

    <div class="anime-grid" data-grid hidden></div>

    <div class="load-more-wrap" data-load-more-wrap hidden>
      <button type="button" class="load-more-btn" data-load-more>
        <span data-load-more-text>Muat Lebih Banyak</span>
      </button>
    </div>
  </Page>

  <AnimeCard mode="template" />
</BaseLayout>

<script>
  // @ts-nocheck
  import {
    STATUS_LABEL,
    STATUS_VARIANT,
    TYPE_LABEL,
    type RawStatus,
    type RawType,
    type Status,
  } from '~/constants';

  (function () {
    const API = 'https://api-qimochi.pages.dev/api/v1/anime.json';
    const AVAILABLE_API = '/api/available-episodes.json';
    const SYNOPSIS_API = '/api/anime-synopsis.json';
    const BATCH = 24;
    const DEFAULT_SORT = 'popular';

    type ApiItem = {
      id: string;
      title: string;
      titleEnglish: string | null;
      titleNative: string | null;
      image: string | null;
      banner?: string | null;
      type: string;
      status: string;
      season: string | null;
      year: number | null;
      episodes: number | null;
      duration: number | null;
      rating: string | null;
      genres: string[];
      studios: string[];
      score?: number | null;
      stats?: { score?: number; scoredBy?: number } | null;
    };

    const grid = document.querySelector('[data-grid]') as HTMLElement | null;
    const skeleton = document.querySelector('[data-skeleton]') as HTMLElement | null;
    const loadMoreWrap = document.querySelector('[data-load-more-wrap]') as HTMLElement | null;
    const loadMoreBtn = document.querySelector('[data-load-more]') as HTMLButtonElement | null;
    const loadMoreText = document.querySelector('[data-load-more-text]') as HTMLElement | null;
    const sortEl = document.querySelector('[data-sort]') as HTMLSelectElement | null;
    const tpl = document.getElementById('anime-card-template') as HTMLTemplateElement | null;

    if (!grid || !tpl || !sortEl) return;

    let all: ApiItem[] = [];
    let sorted: ApiItem[] = [];
    let visibleCount = 0;
    let availableMap = new Map<string, number[]>();
    let synopsisMap = new Map<string, string>();

    function hideSkeleton(): void {
      if (skeleton) skeleton.hidden = true;
    }

    function titleCase(s: string): string {
      if (!s) return '';
      return s
        .split(/[-_\s]+/)
        .filter(Boolean)
        .map((w) => w.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + w.slice(1).toLowerCase())
        .join(' ');
    }

    function formatCount(n: number): string {
      if (n >= 1_000_000) return (n / 1_000_000).toFixed(1).replace(/\.0$/, '') + 'M';
      if (n >= 1_000) return (n / 1_000).toFixed(1).replace(/\.0$/, '') + 'K';
      return String(n);
    }

    function mapStatus(raw: string) {
      const label = STATUS_LABEL[raw as RawStatus] ?? raw;
      const variant = STATUS_VARIANT[label as Status] ?? 'default';
      return { label, variant };
    }

    function mapType(raw: string): string {
      return TYPE_LABEL[raw as RawType] ?? raw;
    }

    function getScore(item: ApiItem): number | null {
      if (typeof item.score === 'number') return item.score;
      if (typeof item.stats?.score === 'number') return item.stats.score;
      return null;
    }

    function getVotes(item: ApiItem): number {
      return item.stats?.scoredBy ?? 0;
    }

    function buildCard(item: ApiItem): HTMLElement | null {
      const frag = tpl!.content.cloneNode(true) as DocumentFragment;
      const card = frag.firstElementChild as HTMLElement | null;
      if (!card) return null;

      const available = availableMap.get(item.id) ?? [];
      const firstEp = available[0] ?? 1;

      const link = card.querySelector('[data-card-link]') as HTMLAnchorElement | null;
      if (link) {
        link.href = `/anime/${item.id}/`;
        link.setAttribute('aria-label', item.title);
      }

      const img = card.querySelector('[data-cover-img]') as HTMLImageElement | null;
      const placeholder = card.querySelector('.cover-placeholder') as HTMLElement | null;

      if (img && item.image) {
        img.src = item.image;
        img.alt = `Cover ${item.title}`;

        const stopShimmer = () => placeholder?.classList.remove('is-loading');
        const fail = () => {
          img.hidden = true;
          stopShimmer();
        };

        if (img.complete && img.naturalWidth > 0) {
          stopShimmer();
        } else {
          img.addEventListener('load', stopShimmer, { once: true });
          img.addEventListener('error', fail, { once: true });
        }

        img.dataset.ready = '1';
      } else if (placeholder) {
        placeholder.classList.remove('is-loading');
      }

      const typeEl = card.querySelector('[data-card-type]') as HTMLElement | null;
      if (typeEl) typeEl.textContent = mapType(item.type);

      const statusEl = card.querySelector('[data-card-status]') as HTMLElement | null;
      if (statusEl) {
        const { label, variant: v } = mapStatus(item.status);
        statusEl.textContent = label;
        statusEl.dataset.variant = v;
      }

      const titleEl = card.querySelector('[data-card-title]') as HTMLElement | null;
      if (titleEl) titleEl.textContent = item.title;

      const titleInfoEl = card.querySelector('[data-card-title-info]') as HTMLElement | null;
      if (titleInfoEl) titleInfoEl.textContent = item.title;

      const score = getScore(item);
      const votes = getVotes(item);
      const ratingEl = card.querySelector('[data-card-rating]') as HTMLElement | null;
      if (ratingEl && score != null) {
        ratingEl.hidden = false;
        const fill = card.querySelector('[data-card-stars-fill]') as HTMLElement | null;
        if (fill) fill.style.width = `${(score / 10) * 100}%`;
        const scoreEl = card.querySelector('[data-card-score]') as HTMLElement | null;
        if (scoreEl) scoreEl.textContent = score.toFixed(1);
        const votesEl = card.querySelector('[data-card-votes]') as HTMLElement | null;
        if (votesEl) votesEl.textContent = votes > 0 ? `(${formatCount(votes)})` : '';
      }

      // Studio
      const studioEl = card.querySelector('[data-card-studio]') as HTMLElement | null;
      if (studioEl && Array.isArray(item.studios) && item.studios.length > 0) {
        const name = titleCase(item.studios[0]);
        if (name && name.toLowerCase() !== 'unknown') {
          const li = studioEl.querySelector('li');
          if (li) li.textContent = name;
          studioEl.hidden = false;
        }
      }

      // Sinopsis
      const excerptEl = card.querySelector('[data-card-excerpt]') as HTMLElement | null;
      if (excerptEl) {
        const text = synopsisMap.get(item.id);
        if (text) {
          excerptEl.textContent = text;
          excerptEl.hidden = false;
        }
      }

      const episodeText = available.length > 0 ? `${available.length} Episode` : '';

      const episodesEl = card.querySelector('[data-card-episodes]') as HTMLElement | null;
      if (episodesEl && episodeText) {
        episodesEl.textContent = episodeText;
        episodesEl.hidden = false;
      }

      const metaTextEl = card.querySelector('[data-card-meta-text]') as HTMLElement | null;
      if (metaTextEl && episodeText) {
        metaTextEl.textContent = episodeText;
      }

      const playEl = card.querySelector('[data-card-play]') as HTMLAnchorElement | null;
      if (playEl) {
        playEl.href = firstEp > 1
          ? `/anime/watch/${item.id}/?ep=${firstEp}`
          : `/anime/watch/${item.id}/`;
        playEl.setAttribute('aria-label', `Mulai menonton ${item.title}`);
      }

      const bookmarkEl = card.querySelector('[data-bookmark-btn]') as HTMLButtonElement | null;
      if (bookmarkEl) bookmarkEl.dataset.anime = item.id;

      return card;
    }

    function sortList(list: ApiItem[], sort: string): ApiItem[] {
      const copy = [...list];
      if (sort === 'newest') {
        return copy.sort((a, b) => {
          const ya = a.year ?? 0;
          const yb = b.year ?? 0;
          if (yb !== ya) return yb - ya;
          return a.title.localeCompare(b.title);
        });
      }
      if (sort === 'title') {
        return copy.sort((a, b) => a.title.localeCompare(b.title));
      }
      return copy.sort((a, b) => {
        const sa = getScore(a) ?? 0;
        const sb = getScore(b) ?? 0;
        if (sb !== sa) return sb - sa;
        const va = getVotes(a);
        const vb = getVotes(b);
        return vb - va;
      });
    }

    function renderNext(): void {
      const end = Math.min(visibleCount + BATCH, sorted.length);
      if (end <= visibleCount) return;

      const frag = document.createDocumentFragment();
      for (let i = visibleCount; i < end; i++) {
        const card = buildCard(sorted[i]!);
        if (card) frag.appendChild(card);
      }
      grid.appendChild(frag);
      visibleCount = end;

      window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('qimochi:bookmarks', { detail: grid }));
      updateLoadMore();
    }

    function updateLoadMore(): void {
      const remaining = sorted.length - visibleCount;
      if (!loadMoreWrap || !loadMoreText) return;
      loadMoreWrap.hidden = remaining <= 0;
      if (remaining > 0) {
        loadMoreText.textContent = `Muat Lebih Banyak (${remaining} lagi)`;
      }
    }

    function render(reset = false): void {
      if (reset) {
        grid.innerHTML = '';
        visibleCount = 0;
      }

      const hasItems = sorted.length > 0;
      grid.hidden = !hasItems;

      if (hasItems) {
        renderNext();
      }
    }

    function applySort(sort: string): void {
      sorted = sortList(all, sort);
      render(true);
    }

    sortEl.addEventListener('change', () => {
      applySort(sortEl.value || DEFAULT_SORT);
    });

    if (loadMoreBtn) {
      loadMoreBtn.addEventListener('click', () => renderNext());
    }

    Promise.all([
      fetch(API, { headers: { Accept: 'application/json' } }).then((r) => {
        if (!r.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${r.status}`);
        return r.json() as Promise<{ data: ApiItem[] }>;
      }),
      fetch(AVAILABLE_API, { headers: { Accept: 'application/json' } })
        .then((r) => (r.ok ? r.json() : {}))
        .catch(() => ({})) as Promise<Record<string, number[]>>,
      fetch(SYNOPSIS_API, { headers: { Accept: 'application/json' } })
        .then((r) => (r.ok ? r.json() : {}))
        .catch(() => ({})) as Promise<Record<string, string>>,
    ])
      .then(([json, availableRaw, synRaw]) => {
        const list = json?.data;
        if (!Array.isArray(list)) return;

        availableMap = new Map<string, number[]>();
        if (availableRaw && typeof availableRaw === 'object') {
          for (const [slug, eps] of Object.entries(availableRaw)) {
            if (Array.isArray(eps)) {
              availableMap.set(slug, eps.filter((n): n is number => typeof n === 'number'));
            }
          }
        }

        synopsisMap = new Map<string, string>();
        if (synRaw && typeof synRaw === 'object') {
          for (const [slug, text] of Object.entries(synRaw)) {
            if (typeof text === 'string' && text.length > 0) {
              synopsisMap.set(slug, text);
            }
          }
        }

        all = list.filter(
          (item) =>
            item.status === 'airing' &&
            (availableMap.get(item.id)?.length ?? 0) > 0
        );

        sortEl.value = DEFAULT_SORT;
        applySort(DEFAULT_SORT);
      })
      .catch((err) => {
        console.error('[ongoing] fetch failed:', err);
      })
      .finally(hideSkeleton);
  })();
</script>

<style>
  .wrapper {
    padding-block: var(--s-6) var(--s-12);
    gap: var(--s-6);
  }
</style>

===== FILE: src/pages/anime/watch/[slug]/index.astro =====
---
import {
  getAllWatchPaths,
  type HydratedAnime,
  type EpisodeData,
} from '~/lib/anime';

import BaseLayout from '~/layouts/BaseLayout.astro';
import Player from '~/components/Player.astro';
import EpisodeHeader from '~/components/EpisodeHeader.astro';
import StreamSelector from '~/components/StreamSelector.astro';
import EpisodeActionBar from '~/components/EpisodeActionBar.astro';
import DownloadPanel from '~/components/DownloadPanel.astro';
import EpisodeList from '~/components/EpisodeList.astro';
import Page from '~/components/Page.astro';
import Icon from '~/user-components/Icons.astro';

export async function getStaticPaths() {
  const paths = await getAllWatchPaths();

  return paths.map((p) => ({
    params: { slug: p.slug },
    props: {
      anime: p.anime,
      episodes: p.episodes,
      totalEpisodes: p.totalEpisodes,
    },
  }));
}

interface Props {
  anime: HydratedAnime;
  episodes: EpisodeData[];
  totalEpisodes: number;
}

const { anime, episodes, totalEpisodes } = Astro.props as Props;
const { data } = anime;

const defaultEpisode = episodes[0]!;
const defaultStream = defaultEpisode.streams?.[0];
const defaultServer = defaultStream?.servers?.[0];
const playerSrc = defaultServer?.url ?? '';

const pageTitle = `${data.title} Sub Indo`;
const pageDesc = `Nonton ${data.title} subtitle Indonesia. Total ${totalEpisodes} episode.`;
const canonical = `/anime/watch/${anime.id}/`;

const votesApiBase = import.meta.env.PUBLIC_API_BASE ?? '';

const nextEpForSsr = episodes.find((e) => e.number === defaultEpisode.number + 1) ?? null;
const prevEpForSsr = episodes.find((e) => e.number === defaultEpisode.number - 1) ?? null;
const thumbnail = data.banner ?? data.cover ?? '';

const watchData = {
  slug: anime.id,
  animeTitle: data.title,
  cover: data.cover,
  banner: data.banner ?? '',
  thumbnail,
  ageRating: data.rating ?? null,
  votesApiBase,
  episodes: episodes.map((e) => ({
    number: e.number,
    title: e.title ?? null,
    duration: e.duration ?? null,
    streams: e.streams,
    downloads: e.downloads ?? [],
  })),
};

const progressPayload = {
  slug: anime.id,
  title: data.title,
  cover: data.cover,
  episode: defaultEpisode.number,
  total: totalEpisodes,
};
---

<BaseLayout
  title={pageTitle}
  description={pageDesc}
  image={data.cover}
  type="video.other"
  canonical={canonical}
  noindex={true}
>
  <section class="player-bleed">
    <Player
      src={playerSrc}
      title={`${data.title} Episode ${defaultEpisode.number}`}
    />
  </section>

  <Page class="wrapper">
    <div class="content-grid">
      <div class="content-main">
        <div data-episode-header>
          <EpisodeHeader
            animeSlug={anime.id}
            animeTitle={data.title}
            episodeNumber={defaultEpisode.number}
            episodeTitle={defaultEpisode.title ?? null}
            ageRating={data.rating ?? null}
            votesApiBase={votesApiBase}
            showReport={true}
          />
        </div>
        <div data-stream-selector>
          <StreamSelector streams={defaultEpisode.streams} />
        </div>
        <div data-download-panel>
          <DownloadPanel downloads={defaultEpisode.downloads ?? []} />
        </div>
      </div>

      <aside class="content-aside">
        <div data-episode-action-bar>
          <EpisodeActionBar
            slug={anime.id}
            animeTitle={data.title}
            currentEpisode={defaultEpisode.number}
            totalEpisodes={totalEpisodes}
            prevEpisode={
              prevEpForSsr
                ? {
                    number: prevEpForSsr.number,
                    title: prevEpForSsr.title ?? null,
                    duration: prevEpForSsr.duration ?? null,
                  }
                : null
            }
            nextEpisode={
              nextEpForSsr
                ? {
                    number: nextEpForSsr.number,
                    title: nextEpForSsr.title ?? null,
                    duration: nextEpForSsr.duration ?? null,
                  }
                : null
            }
            thumbnail={thumbnail}
            server={defaultStream?.quality ?? ''}
            quality={defaultStream?.quality ?? ''}
          />
        </div>
      </aside>
    </div>
  </Page>

  <dialog
    class="ep-modal"
    data-episodes-modal
    aria-labelledby="ep-modal-title"
  >
    <div class="ep-modal__inner">
      <header class="ep-modal__header">
        <div class="ep-modal__heading">
          <h2 class="ep-modal__title" id="ep-modal-title">Episode</h2>
          <p class="ep-modal__subtitle">
            {data.title} · {totalEpisodes} episode
          </p>
        </div>
        <button
          type="button"
          class="ep-modal__close"
          data-episodes-modal-close
          aria-label="Tutup"
        >
          <Icon name="close" size={20} />
        </button>
      </header>

      <div class="ep-modal__body">
        <EpisodeList
          slug={anime.id}
          animeTitle={data.title}
          episodes={episodes}
          currentEpisode={defaultEpisode.number}
          cover={data.cover}
          banner={data.banner ?? ''}
        />
      </div>
    </div>
  </dialog>

  <script
    type="application/json"
    id="watch-data"
    is:inline
    set:html={JSON.stringify(watchData).replace(/</g, '\\u003c')}
  />

  <script
    type="application/json"
    id="progress-payload"
    is:inline
    set:html={JSON.stringify(progressPayload).replace(/</g, '\\u003c')}
  />

  <script>
    (function () {
      interface WatchEpisode {
        number: number;
        title: string | null;
        duration: number | null;
        streams: { quality: string; servers: { name: string; url: string }[] }[];
        downloads: { quality: string; size: string; servers: { name: string; url: string }[] }[];
      }

      interface WatchData {
        slug: string;
        animeTitle: string;
        cover: string;
        banner: string;
        thumbnail: string;
        ageRating: string | null;
        votesApiBase: string;
        episodes: WatchEpisode[];
      }

      const dataEl = document.getElementById('watch-data');
      if (!dataEl) return;

      let data: WatchData;
      try {
        data = JSON.parse(dataEl.textContent || '{}');
      } catch {
        return;
      }

      if (!data.slug || !data.episodes?.length) return;

      const defaultNumber = data.episodes[0]!.number;
      const modalThumb = data.thumbnail || data.banner || data.cover;

      function writeProgress(epNumber: number): void {
        const payload = {
          slug: data.slug,
          title: data.animeTitle,
          cover: data.cover,
          episode: epNumber,
          total: data.episodes.length,
        };
        try {
          const KEY = 'qimochi:continue';
          const raw = localStorage.getItem(KEY);
          let list: unknown[] = [];
          if (raw) {
            const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
            if (Array.isArray(parsed)) list = parsed;
          }
          list = list.filter(
            (x): x is Record<string, unknown> =>
              !!x && typeof x === 'object' && (x as any).slug !== data.slug
          );
          list.unshift({ ...payload, ts: Date.now() });
          localStorage.setItem(KEY, JSON.stringify(list.slice(0, 20)));
        } catch {}
      }

      function getEpisode(n: number): WatchEpisode | null {
        return data.episodes.find((e) => e.number === n) ?? null;
      }

      function urlFor(n: number): string {
        return n === defaultNumber
          ? `/anime/watch/${data.slug}/`
          : `/anime/watch/${data.slug}/?ep=${n}`;
      }

      function readUrlEpisode(): number {
        const sp = new URLSearchParams(location.search);
        const n = parseInt(sp.get('ep') ?? '', 10);
        if (!isNaN(n) && getEpisode(n)) return n;

        const m = location.pathname.match(/\/episodes\/(\d+)\/?$/);
        if (m) {
          const n2 = parseInt(m[1] ?? '', 10);
          if (!isNaN(n2) && getEpisode(n2)) return n2;
        }
        return readQueryParamEpisode() ?? defaultNumber;
      }

      function readQueryParamEpisode(): number | null {
        const sp = new URLSearchParams(location.search);
        const n = parseInt(sp.get('ep') ?? '', 10);
        return !isNaN(n) ? n : null;
      }

      const modalEl = document.querySelector<HTMLDialogElement>('[data-episodes-modal]');
      const modalClose = modalEl?.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-episodes-modal-close]') ?? null;
      const openModalBtn = document.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-open-episodes-modal]');

      let currentNumber = readUrlEpisode();

      function openModal(): void {
        if (!modalEl) return;
        
        const epListAPI = (window as any).__qimochiEpisodeList;
        if (typeof epListAPI?.setActive === 'function') {
           epListAPI.setActive(currentNumber);
        }

        modalEl.showModal();
        document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
      }

      function closeModal(): void {
        if (!modalEl) return;
        if (modalEl.open) modalEl.close();
      }

      if (modalEl) {
        modalEl.addEventListener('close', () => {
          document.body.style.overflow = '';
        });
        
        modalEl.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
          const target = e.target as HTMLElement;
          if (target === modalEl) {
            closeModal();
            return;
          }

          const btn = target.closest<HTMLElement>('[data-episode-item]');
          if (btn && modalEl.contains(btn)) {
            e.preventDefault();
            const n = Number(btn.dataset.number);
            if (!isNaN(n) && getEpisode(n)) {
              switchEpisode(n);
              closeModal();
              window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
            }
          }
        });
      }

      if (openModalBtn) openModalBtn.addEventListener('click', openModal);
      if (modalClose) modalClose.addEventListener('click', closeModal);

      function switchEpisode(n: number, push = true): void {
        const ep = getEpisode(n);
        if (!ep) return;
        if (n === currentNumber && !push) return;

        currentNumber = n;

        const firstServer = ep.streams[0]?.servers[0];

        const player = (window as any).__qimochiPlayer;
        if (player?.load && firstServer) {
          player.load(firstServer.url, `Ep ${n}`);
        } else if (firstServer) {
          const iframe = document.getElementById('player-iframe') as HTMLIFrameElement | null;
          if (iframe) iframe.src = firstServer.url;
        }

        const headingEl = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(
          '[data-episode-header] .ep-header__episode'
        );
        if (headingEl) {
          headingEl.textContent = ep.title?.trim() ? ep.title : `Episode ${n}`;
        }

        const votesRoot = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(
          '[data-episode-header] [data-votes]'
        );
        if (votesRoot) {
          votesRoot.dataset.ep = String(n);
          const reloadFn = (window as any).__qimochiVotes?.reload;
          if (typeof reloadFn === 'function') {
            reloadFn(votesRoot, data.slug, n, data.votesApiBase);
          }
        }

        const streamRoot = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-stream-selector]');
        if (streamRoot) {
          const mountFn = (window as any).__qimochiStream?.mount;
          if (typeof mountFn === 'function') {
            mountFn(streamRoot, ep.streams);
          }
        }

        const dlRoot = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-download-panel]');
        if (dlRoot) {
          const mountDl = (window as any).__qimochiDownload?.mount;
          if (typeof mountDl === 'function') {
            mountDl(dlRoot, ep.downloads);
          }
        }

        const actionRoot = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-episode-action-bar]');
        if (actionRoot) {
          const mountAction = (window as any).__qimochiActionBar?.mount;
          if (typeof mountAction === 'function') {
            const prevEp = data.episodes.find((e) => e.number === n - 1) ?? null;
            const nextEp = data.episodes.find((e) => e.number === n + 1) ?? null;

            mountAction(actionRoot, {
              slug: data.slug,
              current: n,
              total: data.episodes.length,
              thumbnail: modalThumb,
              prev: prevEp
                ? {
                    number: prevEp.number,
                    title: prevEp.title ?? null,
                    duration: prevEp.duration ?? null,
                  }
                : null,
              next: nextEp
                ? {
                    number: nextEp.number,
                    title: nextEp.title ?? null,
                    duration: nextEp.duration ?? null,
                  }
                : null,
            });
          }
        }

        const epListAPI = (window as any).__qimochiEpisodeList;
        if (typeof epListAPI?.setActive === 'function') {
           epListAPI.setActive(n);
        }

        if (push) {
          const target = urlFor(n);
          history.pushState({ ep: n }, '', target);
        }

        writeProgress(n);
        document.title = `${data.animeTitle} Episode ${n} | Qimochi`;
      }

      document.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
        const target = e.target as HTMLElement | null;
        if (!target) return;

        const actionLink = target.closest<HTMLAnchorElement>('[data-episode-nav]');
        if (actionLink && !modalEl?.contains(actionLink)) {
          const n = Number(actionLink.dataset.episodeNav);
          if (!isNaN(n) && getEpisode(n)) {
            e.preventDefault();
            switchEpisode(n);
            window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
            return;
          }
        }
      });

      window.addEventListener('popstate', () => {
        const n = readUrlEpisode();
        if (n !== currentNumber) switchEpisode(n, false);
      });

      const initial = readUrlEpisode();
      if (initial !== defaultNumber) {
        switchEpisode(initial, false);
        const target = urlFor(initial);
        history.replaceState({ ep: initial }, '', target);
      } else {
        writeProgress(defaultNumber);
        const epListAPI = (window as any).__qimochiEpisodeList;
        if (typeof epListAPI?.setActive === 'function') {
           epListAPI.setActive(defaultNumber);
        }
      }
    })();
  </script>
</BaseLayout>

===== FILE: src/pages/anime/watchlist/index.astro =====

<BaseLayout
  title="Watchlist"
  description="Daftar tonton dan riwayat nonton kamu."
  noindex={true}
>
  <template id="history-item-template">
    <li class="history-item">
      <a class="history-item__link" data-history-link>
        <span class="history-item__ep" data-history-ep></span>
        <span class="history-item__body">
          <span class="history-item__title" data-history-title></span>
          <span class="history-item__meta" data-history-meta></span>

  <Page class="wrapper">

<script>
  // @ts-nocheck
  const API = 'https://api.qimochi.web.id';
  const ANIME_API = 'https://api-qimochi.pages.dev/api/v1/anime.json';
  const AVAILABLE_API = '/api/available-episodes.json';
  const SYNOPSIS_API = '/api/anime-synopsis.json';
  const CACHE_KEY = 'qimochi:user-cache';
  const HISTORY_KEY = 'qimochi:continue';

  const tabs = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('[data-tab]'));
  const panels = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('[data-panel]'));

  const listLoading = document.querySelector('[data-list-loading]');
  const listGuest = document.querySelector('[data-list-guest]');
  const listToolbar = document.querySelector('[data-list-toolbar]');
  const listCount = document.querySelector('[data-list-count]');
  const listGrid = document.querySelector('[data-list-grid]');
  const listEmpty = document.querySelector('[data-list-empty]');
  const listSort = document.querySelector('[data-list-sort]');

  const historyToolbar = document.querySelector('[data-history-toolbar]');
  const historyCount = document.querySelector('[data-history-count]');
  const historyClear = document.querySelector('[data-history-clear]');
  const historyList = document.querySelector('[data-history-list]');
  const historyEmpty = document.querySelector('[data-history-empty]');

  const loginLinks = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('[data-login]'));

  const historyTpl = document.getElementById('history-item-template');
  const featuredTpl = document.getElementById('anime-card-featured-template');

  let bookmarks = [];
  let animeMap = new Map();
  let availableMap = new Map();
  let synopsisMap = new Map();
  let listSortKey = 'recent';

  function getUser() {
    try {
      const menu = window.__qimochiUserMenu;
      if (menu?.getUser) return menu.getUser();
    } catch {}
    try {
      const raw = localStorage.getItem(CACHE_KEY);
      if (!raw) return null;
      return JSON.parse(raw);
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  }

  function setActiveTab(name) {
    for (const t of tabs) {
      const isActive = t.dataset.tab === name;
      t.classList.toggle('is-active', isActive);
      t.setAttribute('aria-selected', isActive ? 'true' : 'false');
    }
    for (const p of panels) {
      p.hidden = p.dataset.panel !== name;
    }
  }

  function readTab() {
    const sp = new URLSearchParams(location.search);
    return sp.get('tab') === 'history' ? 'history' : 'list';
  }

  function setTab(name, push = true) {
    setActiveTab(name);
    if (push) {
      const url = new URL(location.href);
      if (name === 'history') url.searchParams.set('tab', 'history');
      else url.searchParams.delete('tab');
      window.history.pushState({}, '', url.toString());
    }
    if (name === 'history') renderHistory();
  }

  for (const t of tabs) {
    t.addEventListener('click', () => setTab(t.dataset.tab));
  }

  window.addEventListener('popstate', () => {
    setActiveTab(readTab());
    if (readTab() === 'history') renderHistory();
  });

  for (const link of loginLinks) {
    link.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      const menu = window.__qimochiUserMenu;
      if (menu?.open) menu.open();
    });
  }

  function showListState(state) {
    listLoading.hidden = state !== 'loading';
    listGuest.hidden = state !== 'guest';
    listToolbar.hidden = state !== 'toolbar' && state !== 'grid';
    listGrid.hidden = state !== 'grid';
    listEmpty.hidden = state !== 'empty';
  }

  function titleCase(s) {
    if (!s) return '';
    return s
      .split(/[-_\s]+/)
      .filter(Boolean)
      .map((w) => w.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + w.slice(1).toLowerCase())
      .join(' ');
  }

  function formatCount(n) {
    if (n >= 1_000_000) return (n / 1_000_000).toFixed(1).replace(/\.0$/, '') + 'M';
    if (n >= 1_000) return (n / 1_000).toFixed(1).replace(/\.0$/, '') + 'K';
    return String(n);
  }

  async function loadBookmarks() {
    const res = await fetch(`${API}/api/v1/bookmarks`, { credentials: 'include' });
    if (res.status === 401) return null;
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}`);
    const json = await res.json();
    return Array.isArray(json.data) ? json.data : [];
  }

  async function loadAnimeIndex() {
    const [animeRes, availRes, synRes] = await Promise.all([
      fetch(ANIME_API, { headers: { Accept: 'application/json' } }).then((r) => {
        if (!r.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${r.status}`);
        return r.json();
      }),
      fetch(AVAILABLE_API, { headers: { Accept: 'application/json' } })
        .then((r) => (r.ok ? r.json() : {}))
        .catch(() => ({})),
      fetch(SYNOPSIS_API, { headers: { Accept: 'application/json' } })
        .then((r) => (r.ok ? r.json() : {}))
        .catch(() => ({})),
    ]);

    const list = Array.isArray(animeRes?.data) ? animeRes.data : [];

    availableMap = new Map();
    if (availRes && typeof availRes === 'object') {
      for (const [slug, eps] of Object.entries(availRes)) {
        if (Array.isArray(eps)) {
          availableMap.set(slug, eps.filter((n) => typeof n === 'number'));
        }
      }
    }

    synopsisMap = new Map();
    if (synRes && typeof synRes === 'object') {
      for (const [slug, text] of Object.entries(synRes)) {
        if (typeof text === 'string' && text.length > 0) {
          synopsisMap.set(slug, text);
        }
      }
    }

    const map = new Map();
    for (const a of list) {
      if ((availableMap.get(a.id)?.length ?? 0) === 0) continue;
      map.set(a.id, a);
    }
    return map;
  }

  function sortBookmarks(list) {
    const copy = [...list];
    if (listSortKey === 'oldest') {
      copy.sort((a, b) => (a.createdAt ?? 0) - (b.createdAt ?? 0));
    } else if (listSortKey === 'title') {
      copy.sort((a, b) => {
        const ta = animeMap.get(a.animeId)?.title ?? a.animeId;
        const tb = animeMap.get(b.animeId)?.title ?? b.animeId;
        return ta.localeCompare(tb);
      });
    } else {
      copy.sort((a, b) => (b.createdAt ?? 0) - (a.createdAt ?? 0));
    }
    return copy;
  }

  function createBookmarkCard(item) {
    if (!featuredTpl) return null;
    const anime = animeMap.get(item.animeId);
    if (!anime) return null;

    const available = availableMap.get(anime.id) ?? [];
    const firstEp = available[0] ?? 1;

    const frag = featuredTpl.content.cloneNode(true);
    const card = frag.firstElementChild;
    if (!card) return null;

    // ── Link
    const link = card.querySelector('[data-card-link]');
    if (link) {
      link.href = `/anime/${anime.id}/`;
      link.setAttribute('aria-label', anime.title);
    }

    // ── Cover (banner utama, fallback ke cover)
    const img = card.querySelector('[data-cover-img]');
    const placeholder = card.querySelector('.cover-placeholder');
    const landscape = anime.banner || anime.image;

    if (img && landscape) {
      img.src = landscape;
      img.alt = `Cover ${anime.title}`;

      const stopShimmer = () => placeholder?.classList.remove('is-loading');
      const fail = () => {
        img.hidden = true;
        stopShimmer();
      };

      if (img.complete && img.naturalWidth > 0) stopShimmer();
      else {
        img.addEventListener('load', stopShimmer, { once: true });
        img.addEventListener('error', fail, { once: true });
      }

      img.dataset.ready = '1';
    } else if (placeholder) {
      placeholder.classList.remove('is-loading');
    }

    // ── Info bawah cover
    const titleInfoEl = card.querySelector('[data-card-title-info]');
    if (titleInfoEl) titleInfoEl.textContent = anime.title;

    const metaTextEl = card.querySelector('[data-card-meta-text]');
    if (metaTextEl) {
      const parts = [
        anime.type,
        available.length > 0 ? `${available.length} Episode` : null,
      ].filter(Boolean);
      metaTextEl.textContent = parts.join(' · ');
    }

    // ── Preview: title
    const titleEl = card.querySelector('[data-card-title]');
    if (titleEl) titleEl.textContent = anime.title;

    // ── Preview: rating
    const score = typeof anime.score === 'number'
      ? anime.score
      : (typeof anime.stats?.score === 'number' ? anime.stats.score : null);
    const votes = anime.stats?.scoredBy ?? 0;

    const ratingEl = card.querySelector('[data-card-rating]');
    if (ratingEl && score != null) {
      ratingEl.hidden = false;
      const fill = card.querySelector('[data-card-stars-fill]');
      if (fill) fill.style.width = `${(score / 10) * 100}%`;
      const scoreEl = card.querySelector('[data-card-score]');
      if (scoreEl) scoreEl.textContent = score.toFixed(1);
      const votesEl = card.querySelector('[data-card-votes]');
      if (votesEl) votesEl.textContent = votes > 0 ? `(${formatCount(votes)})` : '';
    }

    // ── Preview: studio
    const studioEl = card.querySelector('[data-card-studio]');
    if (studioEl && Array.isArray(anime.studios) && anime.studios.length > 0) {
      const name = titleCase(anime.studios[0]);
      if (name && name.toLowerCase() !== 'unknown') {
        const li = studioEl.querySelector('li');
        if (li) li.textContent = name;
        studioEl.hidden = false;
      }
    }

    // ── Preview: sinopsis
    const excerptEl = card.querySelector('[data-card-excerpt]');
    if (excerptEl) {
      const text = synopsisMap.get(anime.id);
      if (text) {
        excerptEl.textContent = text;
        excerptEl.hidden = false;
      }
    }

    // ── Preview: play + bookmark
    const playEl = card.querySelector('[data-card-play]');
    if (playEl) {
      playEl.href = firstEp > 1
        ? `/anime/watch/${anime.id}/?ep=${firstEp}`
        : `/anime/watch/${anime.id}/`;
      playEl.setAttribute('aria-label', `Mulai menonton ${anime.title}`);
    }

    const bookmarkEl = card.querySelector('[data-bookmark-btn]');
    if (bookmarkEl) bookmarkEl.dataset.anime = anime.id;

    // ── Tombol hapus (inject runtime)
    const removeBtn = document.createElement('button');
    removeBtn.type = 'button';
    removeBtn.className = 'bookmark-remove';
    removeBtn.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Hapus dari daftar');
    removeBtn.title = 'Hapus dari daftar';
    removeBtn.innerHTML = `
      <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="14" height="14" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.4" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true">
        <path d="M6 6l12 12M18 6L6 18"/>
      </svg>
    `;
    removeBtn.addEventListener('click', async (e) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      e.stopPropagation();
      removeBtn.disabled = true;
      try {
        const res = await fetch(
          `${API}/api/v1/bookmarks/${encodeURIComponent(anime.id)}`,
          { method: 'DELETE', credentials: 'include' }
        );
        if (!res.ok && res.status !== 404) throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}`);
        bookmarks = bookmarks.filter((b) => b.animeId !== anime.id);
        card.remove();
        updateListCount();
        if (bookmarks.length === 0) showListState('empty');
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('[bookmark] remove failed', err);
        removeBtn.disabled = false;
      }
    });
    card.appendChild(removeBtn);

    return card;
  }

  function updateListCount() {
    if (!listCount) return;
    const playable = bookmarks.filter((b) => animeMap.has(b.animeId));
    listCount.textContent = `${playable.length} anime`;
  }

  function renderList() {
    if (!listGrid) return;
    const sorted = sortBookmarks(bookmarks).filter((b) => animeMap.has(b.animeId));
    listGrid.innerHTML = '';
    const frag = document.createDocumentFragment();
    for (const b of sorted) {
      const card = createBookmarkCard(b);
      if (card) frag.appendChild(card);
    }
    listGrid.appendChild(frag);
    updateListCount();
    showListState('grid');
    window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('qimochi:bookmarks', { detail: listGrid }));
  }

  async function loadList() {
    const user = getUser();
    if (!user) {
      showListState('guest');
      return;
    }

    showListState('loading');
    try {
      const [bm, index] = await Promise.all([loadBookmarks(), loadAnimeIndex()]);
      if (bm === null) {
        showListState('guest');
        return;
      }
      bookmarks = bm;
      animeMap = index;

      const playable = bookmarks.filter((b) => animeMap.has(b.animeId));

      if (playable.length === 0) {
        showListState('empty');
        return;
      }
      renderList();
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('[bookmark] load failed', err);
      showListState('guest');
    }
  }

  if (listSort) {
    listSort.addEventListener('change', () => {
      listSortKey = listSort.value;
      if (bookmarks.length > 0) renderList();
    });
  }

  function readHistory() {
    try {
      const raw = localStorage.getItem(HISTORY_KEY);
      if (!raw) return [];
      const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
      if (!Array.isArray(parsed)) return [];
      return parsed
        .filter(
          (x) =>
            x &&
            typeof x.slug === 'string' &&
            typeof x.title === 'string' &&
            typeof x.episode === 'number'
        )
        .sort((a, b) => (b.ts ?? 0) - (a.ts ?? 0));
    } catch {
      return [];
    }
  }

  function saveHistory(list) {
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(HISTORY_KEY, JSON.stringify(list));
    } catch {}
  }

  function createHistoryItem(item) {
    if (!historyTpl) return null;
    const frag = historyTpl.content.cloneNode(true);
    const node = frag.firstElementChild;
    const link = frag.querySelector('[data-history-link]');
    const ep = frag.querySelector('[data-history-ep]');
    const title = frag.querySelector('[data-history-title]');
    const meta = frag.querySelector('[data-history-meta]');
    const progress = frag.querySelector('[data-history-progress]');
    const progressFill = frag.querySelector('[data-history-progress-fill]');

    link.href = item.episode > 1
      ? `/anime/watch/${item.slug}/?ep=${item.episode}`
      : `/anime/watch/${item.slug}/`;
    ep.textContent = String(item.episode).padStart(2, '0');
    title.textContent = item.title;

    if (item.total > 0) {
      meta.textContent = `Episode ${item.episode} dari ${item.total}`;
    } else {
      meta.textContent = `Episode ${item.episode}`;
    }

    if (progress && progressFill && item.total > 0 && item.episode > 0) {
      const pct = Math.min(100, Math.round((item.episode / item.total) * 100));
      progressFill.style.width = `${pct}%`;
      progress.hidden = false;
      if (item.episode >= item.total) {
        progressFill.classList.add('is-complete');
      }
    }

    return node;
  }

  function renderHistory() {
    const items = readHistory();

    if (items.length === 0) {
      historyToolbar.hidden = true;
      historyList.innerHTML = '';
      historyEmpty.hidden = false;
      return;
    }

    historyEmpty.hidden = true;
    historyToolbar.hidden = false;
    historyCount.textContent = `${items.length} riwayat`;

    historyList.innerHTML = '';
    const frag = document.createDocumentFragment();
    for (const item of items) {
      const node = createHistoryItem(item);
      if (node) frag.appendChild(node);
    }
    historyList.appendChild(frag);
  }

  if (historyClear) {
    historyClear.addEventListener('click', () => {
      if (!window.confirm('Hapus semua riwayat tonton?')) return;
      saveHistory([]);
      renderHistory();
    });
  }

  document.addEventListener('qimochi:auth-changed', () => {
    loadList();
  });

  const initialTab = readTab();
  setActiveTab(initialTab);
  if (initialTab === 'history') {
    renderHistory();
  }
  loadList();
</script>

===== FILE: src/pages/api/anime-synopsis.json.ts =====
import type { APIRoute } from 'astro';
import { getAllAnime } from '~/lib/anime';

const MAX_LEN = 240;

function plainExcerpt(input: string | undefined, maxLen = MAX_LEN): string {
  if (!input) return '';
  const cleaned = input
    .replace(/!\[[^\]]*\]\([^)]*\)/g, '')
    .replace(/\[([^\]]+)\]\([^)]*\)/g, '$1')
    .replace(/[*_`>#]/g, '')
    .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
    .trim();
  return cleaned.length <= maxLen
    ? cleaned
    : cleaned.slice(0, maxLen).trimEnd() + '…';
}

export const GET: APIRoute = async () => {
  const allAnime = await getAllAnime();
  const map: Record<string, string> = {};

  for (const anime of allAnime) {
    const excerpt = plainExcerpt(anime.body);
    if (excerpt) map[anime.id] = excerpt;
  }

  return new Response(JSON.stringify(map), {
    headers: {
      'Content-Type': 'application/json; charset=utf-8',
      'Cache-Control':
        'public, max-age=300, s-maxage=3600, stale-while-revalidate=86400',
    },
  });
};

===== FILE: src/pages/api/available-episodes.json.ts =====
import type { APIRoute } from 'astro';
import { getAllAnime } from '~/lib/anime';

export const GET: APIRoute = async () => {
  const allAnime = await getAllAnime();

  const map: Record<string, number[]> = {};

  for (const anime of allAnime) {
    const eps = anime.data.episodeList;
    if (eps.length === 0) continue;

    map[anime.id] = eps
      .map((e) => e.number)
      .sort((a, b) => a - b);
  }

  return new Response(JSON.stringify(map), {
    headers: {
      'Content-Type': 'application/json; charset=utf-8',
      'Cache-Control':
        'public, max-age=300, s-maxage=3600, stale-while-revalidate=86400',
    },
  });
};

import { render } from 'astro:content';
import BaseLayout from '~/layouts/BaseLayout.astro';
import PostCard from '~/components/PostCard.astro';
import Badge from '~/user-components/Badge.astro';
import Icon from '~/user-components/Icons.astro';
import {
  getPublishedPosts,
  getRelatedPosts,
  formatDate,
} from '~/lib/blog';

export async function getStaticPaths() {
  const posts = await getPublishedPosts();
  return posts.map((post) => ({
    params: { slug: post.id },
    props: { post },
  }));
}

const { post } = Astro.props;
const { data } = post;
const { Content } = await render(post);

const dateText = formatDate(data.date);
const updatedText = data.updatedAt ? formatDate(data.updatedAt) : null;

const allPosts = await getPublishedPosts();
const related = getRelatedPosts(post, allPosts, 3);
---

<BaseLayout
  title={data.title}
  description={data.description}
  image={data.cover}
  type="article"
  publishedTime={data.date.toISOString()}
  canonical={`/blog/${post.id}/`}
>
  <div class="wrapper">
    <article>
      <header>
        <Badge variant="accent" size="sm">{data.category}</Badge>
        <h1 set:html={data.title} />
        <div class="post-meta">
          <span class="post-icon" aria-hidden="true">
            <Icon name="book" size={24} />
          </span>
          <p class="post-author">Oleh {data.author}</p>
          <p class="post-date">
            <time datetime={data.date.toISOString()}>{dateText}</time>
          </p>
        </div>
      </header>
      {data.cover && (
        <div class="cover">
          <img
            src={data.cover}
            alt={`Cover ${data.title}`}
            width="1200"
            height="675"
            loading="eager"
            fetchpriority="high"
            decoding="async"
          />
        </div>
      )}

      <div class="markdown markdown--prose post-body">
        <Content />
      </div>

      {data.tags.length > 0 && (
        <footer class="post-footer">
          <p class="update">
            {updatedText && (
              <time datetime={data.date.toISOString()}> Diperbarui {updatedText}</time>
            )}
          </p>
          <div class="post-tags">
            <span class="tags-label">Tags:</span>
            {data.tags.map(tag => (
              <span class="tag">{tag}</span>
            ))}
          </div>
        </footer>
      )}
    </article>
    {related.length > 0 && (
      <section class="related-section">
        <h2 class="related-title">Terkait</h2>
        <div class="related-grid">
          {related.map(p => <PostCard post={p} />)}
        </div>
      </section>
    )}
  </div>

===== FILE: src/pages/index.astro =====

import { getCollection } from 'astro:content';
import { SITE, PER_PAGE, URLS, ADS } from '~/constants';
import { getAllAnime, type HydratedAnime } from '~/lib/anime';

import BaseLayout from '~/layouts/BaseLayout.astro';
import Featured from '~/components/Featured.astro';
import AnimeCard from '~/components/AnimeCard.astro';
import AdSlot from '~/components/AdSlot.astro';
import LinkButton from '~/user-components/LinkButton.astro';
import LinkCard from '~/user-components/LinkCard.astro';
import SectionLabel from '~/components/SectionLabel.astro';
import Page from '~/components/Page.astro';

const allAnime = await getAllAnime();

function latestUpdateTime(a: HydratedAnime): number {
  return (a.data.updatedAt ?? a.data.addedAt)?.getTime() ?? 0;
}

const ongoingWithEpisodes = allAnime.filter(
  (a) =>
    a.data.status === 'Ongoing' && (a.data.episodeList?.length ?? 0) > 0
);

const latest = [...ongoingWithEpisodes]
  .sort((a, b) => latestUpdateTime(b) - latestUpdateTime(a))
  .slice(0, 3);

const ongoing = [...allAnime]
  .filter((a) => a.data.status === 'Ongoing')
  .sort((a, b) => latestUpdateTime(b) - latestUpdateTime(a))
  .slice(0, PER_PAGE);

const completed = [...allAnime]
  .filter((a) => a.data.status === 'Completed')
  .sort((a, b) => latestUpdateTime(b) - latestUpdateTime(a))
  .slice(0, PER_PAGE);
---

<BaseLayout title={SITE.title}>
  <Featured items={latest} />
  <Page class="wrapper">
    {ongoing.length > 0 && (

          <SectionLabel>Ongoing</SectionLabel>
          <LinkButton
            href={URLS.status('Ongoing')}

            Lihat Semua
          </LinkButton>

            variant="ghost"

            icon="arrow-right"
            iconPos="suffix"

            Lihat Semua
          </LinkButton>
        </header>
        <div class="anime-grid">
          {completed.map(anime => <AnimeCard anime={anime} />)}

===== FILE: src/pages/rss.xml.ts =====
import rss from '@astrojs/rss';
import type { APIContext } from 'astro';
import { SITE } from '~/constants';
import { getPublishedPosts } from '~/lib/blog';

export async function GET(context: APIContext) {
  const posts = await getPublishedPosts();

  return rss({
    title: SITE.name,
    description: SITE.description,
    site: context.site!,
    items: posts.map(post => ({
      title: post.data.title,
      description: post.data.description,
      link: `/blog/${post.id}/`,
      pubDate: post.data.date,
      categories: [post.data.category, ...post.data.tags],
      author: post.data.author,
    })),
    customData: `<language>id-ID</language>`,
  });
}

import BaseLayout from '~/layouts/BaseLayout.astro';

<BaseLayout

<script>
  // @ts-nocheck
  import {
    STATUS_LABEL,
    STATUS_VARIANT,
    TYPE_LABEL,
    type RawStatus,
    type RawType,
    type Status,
  } from '~/constants';

  (function () {
    const API = 'https://api-qimochi.pages.dev/api/v1/anime.json';
    const AVAILABLE_API = '/api/available-episodes.json';
    const SYNOPSIS_API = '/api/anime-synopsis.json';

    const DEBOUNCE_MS = 300;
    const MIN_QUERY = 2;
    const FUZZY_MIN_QUERY = 3;
    const FUZZY_THRESHOLD = 0.55;
    const FUZZY_LIMIT = 50;
    const SUBSTRING_MIN = 20;
    const TOP_RESULTS = 3;
    const SECTION_LIMIT = 6;
    const SECTION_ORDER = ['TV', 'Movie', 'OVA', 'ONA', 'Special', 'Music', 'Unknown'];

    type YukionimeItem = {
      id: string;
      title: string;
      titleEnglish: string | null;
      titleNative: string | null;
      image: string | null;
      banner?: string | null;
      type: string;
      status: string;
      season: string | null;
      year: number | null;
      episodes: number | null;
      duration: number | null;
      rating: string | null;
      genres: string[];
      studios: string[];
      score?: number | null;
      stats?: { score?: number; scoredBy?: number } | null;
    };

    type Entry = {
      item: YukionimeItem;
      search: string;
    };

    const form = document.getElementById('search-form') as HTMLFormElement;
    const input = document.getElementById('search-input') as HTMLInputElement;
    const clearBtn = document.getElementById('search-clear') as HTMLButtonElement;
    const emptyState = document.getElementById('search-empty')!;
    const status = document.getElementById('search-status')!;
    const sections = document.getElementById('search-sections')!;
    const skeleton = document.querySelector('[data-skeleton]') as HTMLElement | null;
    const gridTpl = document.getElementById('anime-card-template') as HTMLTemplateElement | null;
    const featuredTpl = document.getElementById('anime-card-featured-template') as HTMLTemplateElement | null;
    const compactTpl = document.getElementById('anime-card-compact-template') as HTMLTemplateElement | null;

    if (!form || !gridTpl || !featuredTpl || !compactTpl) return;

    let index: Entry[] = [];
    let ready = false;
    let availableMap = new Map<string, number[]>();
    let synopsisMap = new Map<string, string>();

    const resultCache = new Map<string, YukionimeItem[]>();
    const CACHE_LIMIT = 50;

    function cacheSet(key: string, value: YukionimeItem[]) {
      if (resultCache.size >= CACHE_LIMIT) {
        const firstKey = resultCache.keys().next().value;
        if (firstKey !== undefined) resultCache.delete(firstKey);
      }
      resultCache.set(key, value);
    }

    function titleCase(s: string): string {
      if (!s) return '';
      return s
        .split(/[-_\s]+/)
        .filter(Boolean)
        .map((w) => w.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + w.slice(1).toLowerCase())
        .join(' ');
    }

    function levenshtein(a: string, b: string): number {
      if (a === b) return 0;
      if (a.length === 0) return b.length;
      if (b.length === 0) return a.length;
      const m: number[][] = [];
      for (let i = 0; i <= b.length; i++) m[i] = [i];
      for (let j = 0; j <= a.length; j++) m[0][j] = j;
      for (let i = 1; i <= b.length; i++) {
        for (let j = 1; j <= a.length; j++) {
          const cost = a[j - 1] === b[i - 1] ? 0 : 1;
          m[i][j] = Math.min(m[i - 1][j] + 1, m[i][j - 1] + 1, m[i - 1][j - 1] + cost);
        }
      }
      return m[b.length][a.length];
    }

    function fuzzyScore(q: string, t: string): number {
      if (!q || !t) return 0;
      const words = t.split(' ');
      let best = 0;
      for (const w of words) {
        if (Math.abs(w.length - q.length) > 3) continue;
        const dist = levenshtein(q, w);
        const max = Math.max(q.length, w.length);
        const sim = 1 - dist / max;
        if (sim > best) best = sim;
        if (best === 1) break;
      }
      return best;
    }

    function formatCount(n: number): string {
      if (n >= 1_000_000) return (n / 1_000_000).toFixed(1).replace(/\.0$/, '') + 'M';
      if (n >= 1_000) return (n / 1_000).toFixed(1).replace(/\.0$/, '') + 'K';
      return String(n);
    }

    function mapStatus(raw: string) {
      const label = STATUS_LABEL[raw as RawStatus] ?? raw;
      const variant = STATUS_VARIANT[label as Status] ?? 'default';
      return { label, variant };
    }

    function mapType(raw: string): string {
      return TYPE_LABEL[raw as RawType] ?? raw;
    }

    function typeLabel(raw: string): string {
      const mapped = mapType(raw);
      if (mapped === 'Special' && raw === 'Music') return 'Musik';
      if (mapped === 'TV' && raw === 'Unknown') return 'Lainnya';
      return mapped;
    }

    function getScore(item: YukionimeItem): number | null {
      if (typeof item.score === 'number') return item.score;
      if (typeof item.stats?.score === 'number') return item.stats.score;
      return null;
    }

    function getVotes(item: YukionimeItem): number {
      return item.stats?.scoredBy ?? 0;
    }

    function buildCard(
      tpl: HTMLTemplateElement,
      item: YukionimeItem,
      variant: 'grid' | 'featured' | 'compact'
    ): HTMLElement | null {
      const frag = tpl.content.cloneNode(true) as DocumentFragment;
      const card = frag.firstElementChild as HTMLElement | null;
      if (!card) return null;

      const available = availableMap.get(item.id) ?? [];
      const firstEp = available[0] ?? 1;

      const link = card.querySelector('[data-card-link]') as HTMLAnchorElement | null;
      if (link) {
        link.href = `/anime/${item.id}/`;
        link.setAttribute('aria-label', item.title);
      }

      const img = card.querySelector('[data-cover-img]') as HTMLImageElement | null;
      const placeholder = card.querySelector('.cover-placeholder') as HTMLElement | null;

      if (img && item.image) {
        img.src = item.image;
        img.alt = variant === 'grid' ? `Cover ${item.title}` : '';

        const stopShimmer = () => placeholder?.classList.remove('is-loading');
        const fail = () => {
          img.hidden = true;
          stopShimmer();
        };

        if (img.complete && img.naturalWidth > 0) {
          stopShimmer();
        } else {
          img.addEventListener('load', stopShimmer, { once: true });
          img.addEventListener('error', fail, { once: true });
        }

        img.dataset.ready = '1';
      } else if (placeholder) {
        placeholder.classList.remove('is-loading');
      }

      const typeEl = card.querySelector('[data-card-type]') as HTMLElement | null;
      if (typeEl) typeEl.textContent = mapType(item.type);

      const statusEl = card.querySelector('[data-card-status]') as HTMLElement | null;
      if (statusEl) {
        const { label, variant: v } = mapStatus(item.status);
        statusEl.textContent = label;
        statusEl.dataset.variant = v;
      }

      const titleEl = card.querySelector('[data-card-title]') as HTMLElement | null;
      if (titleEl) titleEl.textContent = item.title;

      const titleInfoEl = card.querySelector('[data-card-title-info]') as HTMLElement | null;
      if (titleInfoEl) titleInfoEl.textContent = item.title;

      const score = getScore(item);
      const votes = getVotes(item);
      const ratingEl = card.querySelector('[data-card-rating]') as HTMLElement | null;
      if (ratingEl && score != null) {
        ratingEl.hidden = false;
        const fill = card.querySelector('[data-card-stars-fill]') as HTMLElement | null;
        if (fill) fill.style.width = `${(score / 10) * 100}%`;
        const scoreEl = card.querySelector('[data-card-score]') as HTMLElement | null;
        if (scoreEl) scoreEl.textContent = score.toFixed(1);
        const votesEl = card.querySelector('[data-card-votes]') as HTMLElement | null;
        if (votesEl) votesEl.textContent = votes > 0 ? `(${formatCount(votes)})` : '';
      }

      // Studio — hanya kalau template punya slotnya (grid & featured)
      const studioEl = card.querySelector('[data-card-studio]') as HTMLElement | null;
      if (studioEl && Array.isArray(item.studios) && item.studios.length > 0) {
        const name = titleCase(item.studios[0]);
        if (name && name.toLowerCase() !== 'unknown') {
          const li = studioEl.querySelector('li');
          if (li) li.textContent = name;
          studioEl.hidden = false;
        }
      }

      // Sinopsis — hanya kalau template punya slotnya
      const excerptEl = card.querySelector('[data-card-excerpt]') as HTMLElement | null;
      if (excerptEl) {
        const text = synopsisMap.get(item.id);
        if (text) {
          excerptEl.textContent = text;
          excerptEl.hidden = false;
        }
      }

      const episodeText = available.length > 0 ? `${available.length} Episode` : '';

      const episodesEl = card.querySelector('[data-card-episodes]') as HTMLElement | null;
      if (episodesEl && episodeText) {
        episodesEl.textContent = episodeText;
        episodesEl.hidden = false;
      }

      const metaTextEl = card.querySelector('[data-card-meta-text]') as HTMLElement | null;
      if (metaTextEl && episodeText) {
        metaTextEl.textContent = episodeText;
      }

      const playEl = card.querySelector('[data-card-play]') as HTMLAnchorElement | null;
      if (playEl) {
        playEl.href = firstEp > 1
          ? `/anime/watch/${item.id}/?ep=${firstEp}`
          : `/anime/watch/${item.id}/`;
        playEl.setAttribute('aria-label', `Mulai menonton ${item.title}`);
      }

      const bookmarkEl = card.querySelector('[data-bookmark-btn]') as HTMLButtonElement | null;
      if (bookmarkEl) {
        bookmarkEl.dataset.anime = item.id;
      }

      return card;
    }

    function runFilter(qRaw: string): YukionimeItem[] {
      const q = qRaw.toLowerCase().trim();
      if (!q) return [];

      if (resultCache.has(q)) {
        return resultCache.get(q)!;
      }

      const substring: YukionimeItem[] = [];
      for (const entry of index) {
        if (entry.search.includes(q)) substring.push(entry.item);
      }

      const sortSubstring = (arr: YukionimeItem[]) => {
        arr.sort((a, b) => {
          const at = a.title.toLowerCase();
          const bt = b.title.toLowerCase();
          const ai = at.indexOf(q);
          const bi = bt.indexOf(q);
          if (ai !== bi) return ai - bi;
          return at.length - bt.length;
        });
      };

      if (substring.length >= SUBSTRING_MIN) {
        sortSubstring(substring);
        cacheSet(q, substring);
        return substring;
      }

      if (q.length < FUZZY_MIN_QUERY) {
        sortSubstring(substring);
        cacheSet(q, substring);
        return substring;
      }

      const fuzzy = index
        .map((e) => ({ item: e.item, score: fuzzyScore(q, e.search) }))
        .filter(({ score }) => score >= FUZZY_THRESHOLD)
        .sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score)
        .slice(0, FUZZY_LIMIT)
        .map((s) => s.item);

      const seen = new Set<string>();
      const merged: YukionimeItem[] = [];

      sortSubstring(substring);
      for (const item of substring) {
        if (seen.has(item.id)) continue;
        seen.add(item.id);
        merged.push(item);
      }
      for (const item of fuzzy) {
        if (seen.has(item.id)) continue;
        seen.add(item.id);
        merged.push(item);
      }

      cacheSet(q, merged);
      return merged;
    }

    function buildSection(
      title: string,
      items: YukionimeItem[],
      sectionKey: string
    ): HTMLElement {
      const section = document.createElement('section');
      section.className = 'search-section';
      section.dataset.section = sectionKey;

      const header = document.createElement('div');
      header.className = 'search-section__header';

      const heading = document.createElement('h2');
      heading.className = 'search-section__title';
      heading.textContent = title;

      const countEl = document.createElement('span');
      countEl.className = 'search-section__count';
      countEl.textContent = `${items.length}`;

      header.appendChild(heading);
      header.appendChild(countEl);

      const grid = document.createElement('div');
      grid.className = `search-section__grid search-section__grid--${sectionKey}`;

      const visible = items.slice(0, SECTION_LIMIT);
      for (const item of visible) {
        const card = buildCard(compactTpl!, item, 'compact');
        if (card) grid.appendChild(card);
      }

      section.appendChild(header);
      section.appendChild(grid);

      if (items.length > SECTION_LIMIT) {
        const hidden = document.createElement('div');
        hidden.className = 'search-section__more';
        hidden.hidden = true;

        const hiddenGrid = document.createElement('div');
        hiddenGrid.className = grid.className;
        for (let i = SECTION_LIMIT; i < items.length; i++) {
          const card = buildCard(compactTpl!, items[i]!, 'compact');
          if (card) hiddenGrid.appendChild(card);
        }
        hidden.appendChild(hiddenGrid);
        section.appendChild(hidden);

        const moreBtn = document.createElement('button');
        moreBtn.type = 'button';
        moreBtn.className = 'search-section__more-btn';
        moreBtn.textContent = `Lihat Semua (${items.length - SECTION_LIMIT} lagi)`;
        moreBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
          const isHidden = hidden.hidden;
          hidden.hidden = !isHidden;
          moreBtn.textContent = isHidden
            ? 'Sembunyikan'
            : `Lihat Semua (${items.length - SECTION_LIMIT} lagi)`;
        });
        section.appendChild(moreBtn);
      }

      return section;
    }

    function buildTopResults(items: YukionimeItem[]): HTMLElement {
      const section = document.createElement('section');
      section.className = 'search-section search-section--top';

      const header = document.createElement('div');
      header.className = 'search-section__header';
      const heading = document.createElement('h2');
      heading.className = 'search-section__title';
      heading.textContent = 'Hasil Teratas';
      header.appendChild(heading);

      const grid = document.createElement('div');
      grid.className = 'search-section__grid search-section__grid--featured';

      for (const item of items) {
        const card = buildCard(featuredTpl!, item, 'featured');
        if (card) grid.appendChild(card);
      }

      section.appendChild(header);
      section.appendChild(grid);
      return section;
    }

    function renderAll(items: YukionimeItem[]): void {
      sections.innerHTML = '';

      if (items.length === 0) {
        sections.hidden = true;
        return;
      }

      const top = items.slice(0, TOP_RESULTS);
      if (top.length > 0) {
        sections.appendChild(buildTopResults(top));
      }

      const groups = new Map<string, YukionimeItem[]>();
      for (const item of items) {
        const t = item.type || 'Unknown';
        const list = groups.get(t) ?? [];
        list.push(item);
        groups.set(t, list);
      }

      const orderIdx = (t: string) => {
        const i = SECTION_ORDER.indexOf(t);
        return i === -1 ? 99 : i;
      };
      const sortedGroups = [...groups.entries()].sort(
        (a, b) => orderIdx(a[0]) - orderIdx(b[0])
      );

      for (const [type, items] of sortedGroups) {
        sections.appendChild(buildSection(typeLabel(type), items, type.toLowerCase()));
      }

      sections.hidden = false;
      window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('qimochi:covers', { detail: sections }));
      window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('qimochi:bookmarks', { detail: sections }));
    }

    function applyFilter(query: string): void {
      const q = query.trim();

      // Skeleton hanya muncul kalau index belum siap DAN user sudah
      // mengetik query yang cukup panjang. Di state lain, skeleton hidden.
      const shouldShowSkeleton = !ready && q.length >= MIN_QUERY;
      if (skeleton) skeleton.hidden = !shouldShowSkeleton;

      if (!ready) {
        // Fetch belum selesai — reset sections
        sections.innerHTML = '';
        sections.hidden = true;

        if (q.length === 0) {
          emptyState.hidden = false;
          status.hidden = true;
        } else if (q.length < MIN_QUERY) {
          emptyState.hidden = true;
          status.textContent = `Ketik minimal ${MIN_QUERY} karakter.`;
          status.hidden = false;
        } else {
          // Skeleton tampil; sembunyikan state lain
          emptyState.hidden = true;
          status.hidden = true;
        }
        return;
      }

      // Index sudah siap
      if (q.length === 0) {
        sections.innerHTML = '';
        emptyState.hidden = false;
        status.hidden = true;
        sections.hidden = true;
        return;
      }

      if (q.length < MIN_QUERY) {
        sections.innerHTML = '';
        emptyState.hidden = true;
        status.textContent = `Ketik minimal ${MIN_QUERY} karakter.`;
        status.hidden = false;
        sections.hidden = true;
        return;
      }

      const matched = runFilter(q);

      if (matched.length === 0) {
        sections.innerHTML = '';
        emptyState.hidden = true;
        status.textContent = `Tidak ada hasil untuk "${q}".`;
        status.hidden = false;
        sections.hidden = true;
        return;
      }

      emptyState.hidden = true;
      status.textContent = `${matched.length} hasil untuk "${q}"`;
      status.hidden = false;
      renderAll(matched);
    }

    let debounceId: number | null = null;
    function debouncedFilter(q: string): void {
      if (debounceId !== null) window.clearTimeout(debounceId);
      debounceId = window.setTimeout(() => applyFilter(q), DEBOUNCE_MS);
    }

    function updateClearBtn(): void {
      clearBtn.hidden = input.value.length === 0;
    }

    input.addEventListener('input', () => {
      updateClearBtn();
      debouncedFilter(input.value);
    });
    updateClearBtn();

    clearBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      input.value = '';
      input.focus();
      updateClearBtn();
      applyFilter('');
      const url = new URL(window.location.href);
      url.searchParams.delete('q');
      window.history.pushState({}, '', url);
    });

    form.addEventListener('submit', (e) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      if (debounceId !== null) window.clearTimeout(debounceId);
      const q = input.value.trim();
      const url = new URL(window.location.href);
      if (q) url.searchParams.set('q', q);
      else url.searchParams.delete('q');
      window.history.pushState({}, '', url);
      applyFilter(q);
    });

    window.addEventListener('popstate', () => {
      const p = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
      const q = p.get('q') || '';
      input.value = q;
      updateClearBtn();
      if (debounceId !== null) window.clearTimeout(debounceId);
      applyFilter(q);
    });

    const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
    const initialQuery = params.get('q') || '';
    input.value = initialQuery;
    updateClearBtn();

    // Bootstrap: set state awal berdasarkan query di URL.
    // - No query → empty state (tanpa skeleton)
    // - Query ≥ MIN_QUERY → skeleton (kalau fetch belum selesai)
    applyFilter(initialQuery);

    Promise.all([
      fetch(API, { headers: { Accept: 'application/json' } }).then((res) => {
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}`);
        return res.json() as Promise<{ data: YukionimeItem[] }>;
      }),
      fetch(AVAILABLE_API, { headers: { Accept: 'application/json' } })
        .then((r) => (r.ok ? r.json() : {}))
        .catch(() => ({})) as Promise<Record<string, number[]>>,
      fetch(SYNOPSIS_API, { headers: { Accept: 'application/json' } })
        .then((r) => (r.ok ? r.json() : {}))
        .catch(() => ({})) as Promise<Record<string, string>>,
    ])
      .then(([json, availableRaw, synRaw]) => {
        const list = json?.data;
        if (!Array.isArray(list)) {
          console.error('[Search] Invalid payload');
          if (skeleton) skeleton.hidden = true;
          status.textContent = 'Data tidak valid. Coba refresh halaman.';
          status.hidden = false;
          emptyState.hidden = true;
          sections.hidden = true;
          return;
        }

        availableMap = new Map<string, number[]>();
        if (availableRaw && typeof availableRaw === 'object') {
          for (const [slug, eps] of Object.entries(availableRaw)) {
            if (Array.isArray(eps)) {
              availableMap.set(
                slug,
                eps.filter((n): n is number => typeof n === 'number')
              );
            }
          }
        }

        synopsisMap = new Map<string, string>();
        if (synRaw && typeof synRaw === 'object') {
          for (const [slug, text] of Object.entries(synRaw)) {
            if (typeof text === 'string' && text.length > 0) {
              synopsisMap.set(slug, text);
            }
          }
        }

        const playable = list.filter(
          (item) => (availableMap.get(item.id)?.length ?? 0) > 0
        );

        index = playable.map((item) => {
          const search = [item.title, item.titleEnglish ?? '', item.titleNative ?? '']
            .join(' ')
            .toLowerCase();
          return { item, search };
        });

        ready = true;

        // Re-apply filter dengan input value terkini — user mungkin sudah
        // mengetik lebih lanjut saat fetch belum selesai.
        applyFilter(input.value);
      })
      .catch((err) => {
        console.error('[Search] fetch failed:', err);
        if (skeleton) skeleton.hidden = true;
        status.textContent = 'Gagal memuat data. Coba refresh halaman.';
        status.hidden = false;
        emptyState.hidden = true;
        sections.hidden = true;
      });
  })();
</script>

  text-decoration: none;

import Icon from './Icons.astro';

type IconName = Parameters<typeof Icon>[0]['name'];

interface Props {
  type?: 'button' | 'submit' | 'reset';
  variant?: 'accent' | 'outline' | 'ghost' | 'gold' | 'danger';
  size?: 'sm' | 'md' | 'lg';
  icon?: IconName;
  iconPos?: 'prefix' | 'suffix';
  block?: boolean;
  disabled?: boolean;
  ariaLabel?: string;
  id?: string;
}

const {
  type = 'button',
  variant = 'accent',
  size = 'md',
  icon,
  iconPos = 'prefix',
  block = false,
  disabled = false,
  ariaLabel,
  id,
} = Astro.props;
---

<button
  type={type}
  id={id}
  class:list={[
    'btn',
    `btn-${variant}`,
    `btn-${size}`,
    { 'btn-block': block },
  ]}
  disabled={disabled}
  aria-label={ariaLabel}
>
  {icon && iconPos === 'prefix' && <Icon name={icon} />}
  <span class="btn-label"><slot /></span>
  {icon && iconPos === 'suffix' && <Icon name={icon} />}
</button>

===== FILE: src/user-components/LinkButton.astro =====
---
import Icon from './Icons.astro';

type IconName = Parameters<typeof Icon>[0]['name'];

interface Props {
  href?: string;
  variant?: 'accent' | 'outline' | 'ghost' | 'gold';
  size?: 'sm' | 'md' | 'lg';
  icon?: IconName;
  iconPos?: 'prefix' | 'suffix';
  external?: boolean;
  block?: boolean;
  disabled?: boolean;
  hidden?: boolean;
  ariaLabel?: string;
  class?: string;
  [key: string]: any;
}

const {
  href = '#',
  variant = 'accent',
  size = 'md',
  icon,
  iconPos = 'prefix',
  external = false,
  block = false,
  disabled = false,
  hidden = false,
  ariaLabel,
  class: className,
  ...rest
} = Astro.props;

const Tag = disabled ? 'span' : 'a';
---

<Tag
  href={disabled ? undefined : href}
  class:list={[
    'link-btn',
    `btn-${variant}`,
    `btn-${size}`,
    {
      'btn-block': block,
      'link-btn--disabled': disabled,
    },
    className,
  ]}
  aria-label={ariaLabel}
  aria-disabled={disabled ? 'true' : undefined}
  hidden={hidden}
  target={!disabled && external ? '_blank' : undefined}
  rel={!disabled && external ? 'noopener noreferrer' : undefined}
  {...rest}
>
  {icon && iconPos === 'prefix' && <Icon name={icon} />}
  <span class="btn-label"><slot /></span>
  {icon && iconPos === 'suffix' && <Icon name={icon} />}
</Tag>

<style>
  .link-btn {
    display: inline-flex;
    align-items: center;

  }

---
interface Props {
  href: string;
  variant?: 'default' | 'outline' | 'flat' | 'trans';
  padding?: 'none' | 'sm' | 'md' | 'lg';
  external?: boolean;
  lift?: boolean;
  class?: string;
  ariaLabel?: string;
}

const {
  href,
  variant = 'default',
  padding = 'none',
  external = false,
  lift = true,
  class: className,
  ariaLabel,
} = Astro.props;
---

<a
  href={href}
  class:list={[
    'link-card',
    `card-${variant}`,
    `card-pad-${padding}`,
    { 'card-lift': lift },
    className,
  ]}
  aria-label={ariaLabel}
  target={external ? '_blank' : undefined}
  rel={external ? 'noopener noreferrer' : undefined}
>
  <slot />
</a>

<style>
  .link-card {
    display: block;
    border: 1px solid transparent;
    color: inherit;
    text-decoration: none;
    transition: border-color var(--t-fast), background var(--t-fast);
    overflow: hidden;
  }

  .card-default {
    background: var(--bg-elevated);
  }
  .card-default:hover {
    background: var(--bg-surface);
  }

  .card-outline {
    background: transparent;
    border-color: var(--border);
  }
  .card-outline:hover {
    border-color: var(--accent);
  }

  .card-flat {
    background: var(--bg-surface);
  }
  .card-flat:hover {
    background: var(--bg-hover);
  }

  .card-trans {
    background: transparent;
    border-color: transparent;
  }

  .card-pad-none { padding: 0; }
  .card-pad-sm   { padding: var(--s-3); }
  .card-pad-md   { padding: var(--s-4); }
  .card-pad-lg   { padding: var(--s-6); }

  .card-lift {
    transition: transform var(--t-fast), border-color var(--t-fast), background var(--t-fast);
  }
  .card-lift:hover {
    transform: translateY(-2px);
  }
</style>
